### [1] [V-JEPA 2 Meets AQA: Phase-Based Scoring and Reference-Based Feedback for Mountain Biking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30160)
*Santhiya Rajan,Savio Cardoz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索了使用弱标签和候选反馈评估山地自行车技巧的质量，提出了一种基于视频特征和关键点测量的方法，验证了其在内部和外部测试集上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在没有连续专家评分的情况下，利用有限的标注和候选反馈信息来评估山地自行车技巧的质量是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 研究通过分析95段野外记录的跳跃片段（标签为好或坏），使用冻结的V-JEPA 2视频特征和二维姿态及运动测量，构建了一个基于小变压器和参考层的评分系统。还详细描述了测量方法和网络结构，并进行了五折交叉验证。

Result: 在五折交叉验证中，整个评分系统在外包率达到0.768，平均精确度为0.812。去除生物力学评分输入后，外包率达到0.809。对于外部探针集，一个早期检查点获得了二元准确性0.412，与专家评分的相关性为-0.135。候选指示剂在两位教练的检测中具有高召回率但较低的精确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前方法存在一定的局限性，无法独立于骑手进行评分或提供可靠的纠正反馈，但为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study mountain-bike action quality assessment (AQA) with weak labels and candidate feedback. The training corpus comprises 95 field-recorded jump clips with binary labels denoting good or poor technique. These are coarse categorical labels, not continuous expert quality scores; phase-boundary and aspect-level annotations are unavailable. Our pipeline summarizes four heuristic phases using frozen V-JEPA 2 video features and two-dimensional pose and motion measurements. A small transformer predicts a binary-label score. A separate reference layer compares phase-specific measurements with those of clips labelled as good technique to propose phase, body-parameter, and direction cues. We detail the measurements, network, and evaluation so that the limits of each output are explicit. In five-fold clip-level cross-validation, the full scorer obtained pooled out-of-fold AUROC 0.768 and average precision 0.812. These are exploratory figures because the reporting fold also selected the neural training epoch and riders were not separated between folds. Removing biomechanical scoring inputs raised AUROC to 0.809. A separate inner-selection run obtained AUROC 0.678, but used about 57 rather than 76 training clips per outer fold, so its difference from the test-selected run does not quantify selection bias. The unsupervised aspect head did not learn distinct factors. On a 17-clip external probe, an earlier checkpoint obtained binary accuracy 0.412 and Spearman association -0.135 with expert scores. Candidate cues had precision 0.118 and recall 1.0 against each of two coaches, with substantial disagreement about direction. These findings do not establish rider-independent scoring or reliable corrective feedback; they identify the empirical work needed to evaluate either use.

</details>


### [2] [EgoHand: Monocular 3D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30293)
*Chao-Yeh Chen,Tiffany Chen,Lin Duan,Tianyi Hu,Yu-Hsiang Huang,Umar Iqbal,Jiefeng Li,Edy Lim,Yanchen Liu,Yihan Pang,Hang Qiu,Jun Saito,Shao Su,Qingsi Wang,Xiufeng Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoHand 是一种单目3D手部重建模型，通过合成数据、野外视频和多相机动作捕捉的结合训练，实现了在第一人称视频上的最新性能。模型展现了强大的泛化能力，用户偏好研究也显示了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种高性能的单目3D手部重建模型，能够在不同场景中准确重建手部姿态，具有广泛的应用前景。

Method: EgoHand 模型采用了 SOMA-Hand 参数化表示，利用变压器编码器处理手部2D图，并结合相机线索和辅助2D分支提高几何一致性与图像对齐。

Result: EgoHand 在HOT3D、ARCTIC和H2O等数据集上的零样本评估中，展示了较低的关节位置误差、根位移误差和加速度误差，在近9.69毫秒的推理延迟下仍具有较强的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: EgoHand 的提出和发布为手部重建领域提供了新的解决方案，促进了相关技术的发展和应用。

Abstract: We introduce EgoHand, a monocular 3D hand reconstruction model that achieves state-of-the-art performance on egocentric video. Central to our approach is the curation of a high-quality training corpus of 14 million images, combining synthetic data, in-the-wild video, and multi-camera motion capture to balance scene diversity and supervision quality. The model adopts SOMA-Hand as its parametric representation and processes 2D hand crops with a transformer encoder, incorporating camera cues and an auxiliary 2D branch to improve geometric consistency and image alignment. Zero-shot evaluations on HOT3D, ARCTIC, and H2O demonstrate strong out-of-domain generalization, with lower joint-position, root-translation, and acceleration errors than competing baselines at an inference latency of approximately 9.69 ms. A blinded user preference study further shows that participants favor EgoHand's reconstructed hand poses over those from Apple EgoDex and Ropedia Xperience-10M. We release the EgoHand model and source code under the OpenMDW license, allowing free commercial use of the model.

</details>


### [3] [R4DGS: Referring Segmentation in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30326)
*Bangpu Chen,Yaxuan Li,Shirui Peng,Xiangtian Si,Liuxin Chu,Xitong Cao,Hongbo Jin,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了R4DGS任务，旨在对动态四维场景中的时间变化、多目标或推理密集、无目标或有干扰表达进行语义关联。引入了R4D-Bench-QA基准，并提出了一种名为ReferGaussian的统一框架，该框架将低级动态高斯分布转化为持久的语言可寻址实体，实现了无需训练的指针推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的方法在支持复杂的自然语言参考描述动态场景方面存在限制，现有的4D高斯方法主要针对渲染导向的动态建模，而目前的语义扩展经常依赖于特定场景的语义优化或在相对简单的查询设置上进行评估。因此，论文引入了R4DGS任务和ReferGaussian框架以改进这一问题。

Method: ReferGaussian框架基于4D高斯分布，通过EntityBank组织带有掩码的支持高斯实体，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）分解复杂查询为可追踪的对象短语和结构化参考约束，以及实现具备约束的语义关联，从而在不需要特定场景语义优化的情况下进行训练。

Result: 在R4D-Bench-QA和4D LangSplat协议上的实验结果显示了R4DGS任务的更好时间目标选择和具有竞争力的空间-时间地关联，无需特定场景的语义训练。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于提出了R4DGS任务以及基于4D高斯变换的解决方法ReferGaussian，能有效进行动态场景中复杂自然语言表达的语义关联，并在评估中展示了明显的优势。

Abstract: Dynamic representations based on 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) have shown strong capabilities in reconstruction and rendering, yet current methods remain limited in supporting complex natural-language referring in dynamic scenes. Existing 4D Gaussian methods are primarily designed for rendering-oriented dynamic modeling, while current semantic extensions often rely on scene-specific semantic optimization or are evaluated on relatively simple query settings. In this paper, we study Referring 4D Gaussian Splatting (R4DGS), a task for grounding temporally varying, multi-target or reasoning-intensive, and zero-target or distractor expressions in dynamic 4D scenes. To support this task, we introduce R4D-Bench-QA, a benchmark with structured query annotations. We further present ReferGaussian, a unified framework built upon 4DGS that converts low-level dynamic Gaussians into persistent, language-addressable entities for training-free referring inference. ReferGaussian organizes mask-supported Gaussian entities in an EntityBank, uses Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to decompose complex queries into trackable object phrases and structured referring constraints, and performs constraint-aware grounding without scene-specific semantic optimization. Experiments on R4D-Bench-QA and the 4D LangSplat protocol demonstrate improved temporal target selection and competitive spatiotemporal grounding without scene-specific semantic training. Project resources are available at https://chenbangpu.github.io/R4DGS/.

</details>


### [4] [MoRE: Motion-Regularized Encoding for Articulated 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30337)
*Guantian Zheng,Shan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRE 提出了一种通过单张图像生成 articulated 3D 对象的方法，通过运动正则化编码和坐标对齐关节线来改进对象的关节表示，从而提高重建对象的运动一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的3D生成方法在处理articulated对象时，难以保证重建对象的运动一致性。为了填补这一空白，作者提出了MoRE，旨在通过运动正则化编码和改进的关节表示，提升articulated对象的3D生成效果。

Method: MoRE采用了轴分类和两轴正交Plücker矩来表示关节线，避免了关节线原点的选择。在训练过程中，解码出的关节假设被用来移动相应的三维锚点，并将它们的运动与注释关节进行比较。通过差分期望，关节类型和轴方向的耦合促进了属性重建及其引起的运动。同时，使用图像条件流学习功能潜在变量，并采用多状态适应的高斯模型提供视觉特征。

Result: 与基准相比，MoRE在PartNet-Mobility和ACD数据集上表现出更强的部分盒子一致性、运动几何和视觉相似性。特别是在关节预测方面，MoRE能够减少轴方向、原点位置和范围误差。

Conclusion: MoRE通过改进的关节线表示和运动监督训练，提高了articulated 3D对象的生成质量。

Abstract: Generating an articulated object from a single image requires recovering both its appearance and the mechanism governing its parts. A compact encoding of joint attributes does not by itself ensure that reconstruction preserves the resulting motion. We present MoRE, Motion-Regularized Encoding for articulated 3D generation. MoRE represents coordinate-aligned joint lines with axis classification and two axis-orthogonal Plücker moment coefficients, removing the arbitrary choice of an origin along the axis. During representation learning, decoded joint hypotheses are used to move corresponding three-dimensional anchors, and their motions are compared with those of annotated joints. A differentiable expectation over joint types and axis directions couples attribute reconstruction to the motion it induces. An image-conditioned flow learns the resulting functional latents, while a multi-state-adapted Gaussian model supplies appearance. At inference, recovered part assignments and joints articulate a single set of rest-pose Gaussians without regenerating the object for each state. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility and ACD show stronger part-box agreement, articulated-state geometry and visual similarity than the compared baselines, while joint prediction reduces axis, origin and range errors relative to ArtiLatent. Controlled ablations support the joint-line encoding and show that motion supervision reduces anchor-motion error. Code and pretrained checkpoints will be released in the future.

</details>


### [5] [Foundation4D: Towards a unified perception and reconstruction model for autonomous vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30340)
*NVIDIA,:,Sérgio Agostinho,Maximilian Apodaca,Emmanuel Attia,Guillem Braso,George Dong,Ilya Fedorov,Michele Fenzi,Gang Huang,Jiahui Huang,Shengyu Huang,Bingxin Ke,Laura Leal-Taixé,Kuan-Ting Lee,Mingxuan Liu,Tian Liu,Tim Meinhardt,Aljoša Ošep,Naveen Kumar Rai,Kefei Ren,Cristiano Saltori,Keqiang Wu,Ning Xu,Fei Xue,Jin-Bey Yu,Lei Zhang,Qunjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Foundation4D 是一种统一的前馈模型，可以从多摄像头驾驶视频中估计几何结构、3D 检测、语义占位和高斯重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍一种结合多种任务（几何估计、3D 检测、语义占位和高斯重建）的多摄像头驾驶视频处理方法。

Method: 使用共享主干网络和因子化时空注意机制，有效结合多摄像头和时间的观测数据；视频推理使用姿态图优化来细化相机轨迹并组装对象轨迹；开发标注流水线并发布数据集 PAI-Foundation4D。

Result: 该方法在 Waymo 和 nuScenes 上实现了最低的深度 AbsRel 错误，在 nuScenes 上实现了最高的检测 mAP，在 OpenOccupancy 上实现了最高的占用 mIoU。任务组合研究表明，在几何和检测的同时训练中，可以提高检测性能并降低相机到结构台的平移误差。

Conclusion: Foundation4D 方法在多任务联合训练方面显示出优势，并且可以在没有 LiDAR 的情况下支持 NuRec 的驾驶场景重建。

Abstract: We introduce Foundation4D, a unified feed-forward model for geometry estimation, 3D detection, semantic occupancy, and Gaussian reconstruction from multi-camera driving video. A shared backbone uses factorized spatiotemporal attention to efficiently combine observations across cameras and time. Our video inference pipeline uses pose graph optimization to refine camera trajectories and assembles object tracks across input windows. We also develop a data annotation pipeline and release PAI-Foundation4D, a benchmark with dense depth and semantic occupancy labels for 1,253 driving clips. With benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Foundation4D achieves the lowest depth AbsRel on Waymo and nuScenes, the highest detection mAP on nuScenes, and the highest occupancy mIoU on OpenOccupancy among the compared methods. Our task-combination study shows benefits for both detection and geometry: co-training detection with geometry and occupancy improves top-class mAP by 1.2 points over detection-only training, while co-training geometry with detection reduces camera-to-rig translation error by $13.4\%$ relative to geometry-only training. We further use the predicted geometry and trajectories as priors for lidarless NuRec, supporting reconstruction of driving scenes for simulation.

</details>


### [6] [FiLM-CAM: Keyed Feature Modulation for Conditional-Access Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30347)
*Yash Kulthe,Vishal Asnani,Shruti Agarwal,Andrew Gilbert,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiLM-CAM是一种基于条件访问的图像水印方法，通过密钥调节特征层实现嵌入、解码和删除的授权。该方法通过最大化授权与非授权使用之间的性能差距来确保在正确密钥的条件下可靠提取水印，在错误密钥下则导致提取失败。此外，该方法还设计了可以从图像中估计水印残差，使得在已存在水印的情况下，通过密钥条件下的重新编码及减法即可简易删除水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字版权保护和版权追踪方面，需要一种能够确保授权用户能正确获得或删除水印，而非授权用户获取水印失败的图像水印方法。

Method: FiLM-CAM 方法采用一种基于密钥调节特征层 (FiLM) 的机制来嵌入、解码和移除水印。首先，通过密钥调节嵌入水印的内部表示；然后，在解码时使用相同的密钥来确保准确恢复水印；最后，通过重新编码和减去残差来实现基于密钥的水印移除。

Result: 实验证明，FiLM-CAM 方法可以作为图像水印中的一个有效的、架构无关的条件访问模块 (CAM)，能够在多种图像水印嵌入、解码框架上取得良好的效果。

Conclusion: FiLM-CAM 方法提供了一种新的条件访问图像水印方案，通过将密钥与特征层耦合，确保了只有具有正确密钥的用户才能成功嵌入、解码和移除水印，增强了图像水印的安全性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose FiLM-CAM; a conditional-access image watermarking approach that enforces authorized encoding, decoding, and removal through keyed feature modulation. A secret key conditions a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) mechanism that transforms the internal representation of the watermark signal before embedding, thereby coupling the payload to the key at the feature, rather than pixel level. The same key must be presented to condition the decoder to correctly recover the payload embedded in an image. To enforce conditional access, we train the system to maximize the performance gap between authorized and unauthorized use, ensuring reliable extraction under the correct key while inducing failure under incorrect keys. In addition, we design our encoder to estimate the watermark residual invariant to the presence of pre-existing watermarks. This design enables conditional watermark removal via simple re-encoding and subtraction of the residual using iterative refinement, contingent on knowledge of the secret key. We evaluate FiLM-CAM across multiple watermarking encoder-decoder backbones, demonstrating that keyed feature modulation serves as an effective, architecture-agnostic conditional access module (CAM) for image watermarking.

</details>


### [7] [Latent Space Jam: Layout Guided Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30390)
*Asaf Joseph,Moshe Mandel,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需训练的方法，利用预训练的视频扩散模型通过粗略布局控制对象布局和运动，无需额外计算资源、数据或架构修改。该方法两步生成高质量、布局一致的视频，实验结果表明其在重叠和轨迹准确性以及视觉质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成方法需要训练，消耗大量计算资源。本研究旨在提出一种无需训练的方法，以减少这些需求。

Method: 该方法分为两步：第一步通过提示引导的跨注意力引导时空布局，生成粗略视觉指导；第二步用此指导条件化相同模型生成高质量、布局一致的视频。

Result: 该方法能够实现协调运动并具有强时间一致性，支持多样化的轨迹和对象交互。实验结果显示在重叠、轨迹准确性和视觉质量上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种有效的无需训练的方法，可在多种场景下生成高质量、布局一致的视频。

Abstract: Adding control to video generation often requires training, which demands additional computational resources, data, or architectural changes. We propose a training-free approach that utilizes pretrained video diffusion models to control object layout and motion using coarse layouts. Our method operates in two passes: first, it steers spatiotemporal placement through prompt-guided cross-attention to produce a coarse visual guide; second, this guide conditions the same model to generate a high-quality, layoutconsistent video. The approach enables coordinated motion with strong temporal consistency, supporting diverse trajectories and object interactions without additional training. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms prior work in overlap and trajectory accuracy, as well as visual quality across static, dynamic, and multi-object scene settings.

</details>


### [8] [VisCurate: Evaluating Behavioral Equivalence of Visual Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30405)
*Md Farhan Ishraq,Md Farhan Ishmam,Reaz Hassan Joarder,Md Hasanul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisCurate 是一种新的框架和基准，通过观察执行而不是文本来整理视觉技能库。它在两个真实的未整理库中发现了大量冗余技能，并证明了多项发布的等效性声明是错误的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉技能库通常通过描述、名称或源代码来整理，但无法准确反映技能对图像的实际视觉变化。这些描述性的信息可能会引入冗余、错误标注或有缺陷的技能。

Method: VisCurate 通过将候选技能运行在一个共享的探测电池上，并根据技能之间的六种行为关系进行分类。它还注入了七种类型的缺陷并生成了300个实例，以评估不同验证器的表现。

Result:  visCurate 证实了在真实库中发现的92%的技能是冗余的，而实际只确认了17项等效声明中的10项。相较于文本基准，VisCurate 不接受了任何虚假合并，且恢复了约51.2%真正可以合并的技能对。

Conclusion: 通过行为而非描述来整理视觉技能库是更精确的方法，VisCurate 在实际应用中展现出了比文本描述更好的性能。

Abstract: Modern vision-based agents have shifted toward skill-based problem-solving, where a skill is an executable image-to-image operation, e.g., cropping, thresholding, or edge detection. As these skill libraries grow, they accumulate duplicated, mislabeled, and defective skills. The signals normally used to curate them (a skill's name, description, or source code) say little about how the skill visually changes the image. We present VisCurate, a framework and benchmark that curates visual skill libraries from observed execution rather than from text. VisCurate runs each candidate skill pair on a shared probe battery and assigns one of six behavioral relations, each tied to a library edit that is safe to make. The benchmark injects seven defect types into a clean library at controlled rates, producing 300 reproducible instances with ground-truth relations and repairs. While text baselines accept up to five of six false merges on hard negatives, our verifier accepts none and recovers 51.2% of the pairs that are truly mergeable. On two real, uncurated libraries, VisCurate finds that 92% of the skills a package shares with its downstream copy are redundant, while execution confirms only 10 of 17 published equivalence claims. The curation problem also remains open, as the strongest of the nine automated curators recovers only about half of the repairs. We emphasize that visual skill libraries should be curated by behavior, not by description.

</details>


### [9] [SplatLab: Autonomous Scientific Discovery for 3DGS Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30468)
*Seemandhar Jain,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatLab 是一个自动研究系统，用于解决 3D 超级采样(Gaussian Splatting)的研究挑战。SplatLab 可以在短时间内从头开始进行研究并提出扩展，同时保持高可执行性和记录透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主研究系统存在执行速度慢、模板固定、可执行性差以及缺乏可追踪性的问题，无法在活跃的计算机视觉子领域进行端到端的演示。

Method: SplatLab 的设计包括以下五个关键组件：（i）候选者通过验证的再生产骨干进行插件变异来匹配已发布的基线；（ii）一个级联加权Elo锦标赛评分候选者；（iii）一个持续反驳者在失败时扣分，并将压力测试生存纳入选择；（iv）每个报告的数字都绑定到一个记录的训练运行中，直到任何声明得到验证；（v）一个七个类别的3DGS分类将EVOLVE-BLOCK标记嵌入到与3DGS存储库对齐的结构中。

Result: 与复制的目标基准相比，SplatLab 在 PSNR 目标运行中提高了0.18至3.78 dB，包括在没有先例的天文摄影星云渲染中提高了3.78 dB。研究周期从需要数个月的研究生时间缩短为只需几天的主要自主计算。

Conclusion: SplatLab 成功地减少了 3D 超级采样的研究时间，并提高了研究效率和透明度，显著增强了该领域的自主研究能力。

Abstract: Reproducing a published 3DGS paper takes weeks of expert engineering before any extension can be proposed. Prior autonomous-research systems read papers, draft hypotheses, and write code, but are limited by short fixed templates, low executability, and weak traceability, and none has been demonstrated end-to-end in a live computer-vision subfield with intense activity. SplatLab is an autonomous research system for 3D Gaussian Splatting that addresses these limitations. Given a research prompt, a target-paper URL, or no input at all, it reproduces a published baseline, proposes an extension, trains and ablates across Mip-NeRF 360 and LLFF, writes the manuscript, and binds every reported number to a logged training run. Five design choices distinguish it: (i) candidates mutate plugins from a verified-reproduction backbone matched to published baselines; (ii) a cascade-weighted Elo tournament across eight islands scores candidates on measured GPU PSNR with verbal-gradient feedback; (iii) a standing falsifier subtracts Elo on failure, integrating stress-test survival into selection; (iv) every reported number is bound to a logged training run, and the run cannot terminate while any claim is unbacked; (v) a seven-category 3DGS taxonomy wraps EVOLVE-BLOCK markers in domain-aligned scaffolds transferable across 3DGS repositories. On PSNR-targeted runs, SplatLab's manuscripts beat their reproduced targets by +0.18 to +3.78 dB, including +3.78 dB on astrophotographic nebula rendering, for which no prior 3DGS method exists. The research cycle compresses from months of graduate-student time to days of mostly autonomous compute. Code, all manuscripts, experiment ledgers, and the cross-run paper store are available on the project page.

</details>


### [10] [AVAtt: A Free-Viewing Eye-Tracking Dataset and Benchmark for Visual Attention on Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30481)
*Mohamed Amine Kerkouri,Marouane Tliba,Aladine Chetouani,Alessandro Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了avatt，一个包含绘画的眼动追踪数据集，覆盖了16种艺术运动和5个世纪的画作。研究人员评估了经典和深度感知模型，并展示了艺术感知模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的眼动追踪数据集主要基于自然照片，缺乏对绘画的专门研究。绘画作品往往由艺术家精心设计，引导观众的视线，与照片存在显著差异。因此，本研究旨在创建一个专门针对绘画作品的眼动追踪数据集，以促进相关研究。

Method: 研究团队收集了跨越多个世纪和艺术运动的绘画作品的眼动追踪数据。数据包括单个眼动轨迹及其详细信息（如可视化角度平滑的注视点图、时空兴趣图等），以及丰富的艺术元数据。使用经典和深度感知模型进行基准测试，并开发了44种扫描路径相似性措施，部分措施基于多种CNN、ViT等模型构建。控制偏倚因素后，研究还分析了不同元素对观众 gaze 的影响。

Result: 研究结果显示，艺术感知模型在预测观众注视点方面还有很大的改进空间。此外，研究人员发现，面部比预期更容易受到注视，局部结构能吸引视线，从图像内容衍生的构图规则可以预测人们的注视点，艺术风格影响观众的共识程度。

Conclusion: 该研究进一步验证了现有模型的不足，并为开发新的艺术感知模型铺平了道路。研究结果将有助于深入了解人类视觉系统如何感知艺术作品，并促进相关技术的进步。

Abstract: Saliency and scanpath models are developed almost exclusively on natural photographs, yet paintings are composed by artists to guide the viewer's eye and differ from photographs in texture, colour and abstraction. We introduce \avatt, a free-viewing eye-tracking dataset on \nImages{} paintings spanning sixteen art movements and five centuries, with \nScanpaths{} scanpaths and \nFixations{} fixations from \nParticipants{} participants (\spPerImageMin--\spPerImageMax{} observers per painting). For every painting we release individual scanpaths with fixation timing and eye--screen distance, fixation maps, saliency maps smoothed at one degree of visual angle, temporal saliency videos and rich art metadata. We benchmark classic and deep saliency models against human agreement, revealing substantial headroom for art-aware models, and rank 44 scanpath-similarity measures, including a new language-free family of image-encoder metrics built on ten CNN, ViT and self-supervised encoders. With controls that remove viewing biases, we show that faces are fixated \faceEnrich$\times$ more than expected and within half a second, that local structure draws the eye, that composition rules fitted from image content predict where people look, and that art style shapes how much viewers agree. Dataset, code and a Python package are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Amine-CV/AVAtt.

</details>


### [11] [Unified Trajectory and 3D Pose Forecasting for Multi-Agent Sports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30519)
*Marc Peral,Guillem Capellera,Luis Ferraz,Antonio Rubio,Stella Graßhof,Dan Witzner Hansen,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的联合预测方法，通过在输入级别融合轨迹和姿态，并使用共享因子化注意力模型它们进行了联合建模。此外，通过动态关节的损失重权重策略和频域轨迹平滑项进一步提升了预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在团队运动中，多个交互式参与者的复杂社会动态使得仅从全球轨迹或3D姿态之一不能准确预测未来的人体动作，因此需要一种能够同时建模轨迹和姿态的方法，进而以更准确的预测未来的动作。

Method: 研究开发了一个社会时间编码器，该编码器在输入级上融合了轨迹和姿态，并通过共享因子化注意力机制进行了联合建模。此外，通过为动态关节分配更高的损失权重，防止了解决策导致的退化解决方案，并增加了一个轨迹平滑项以优化轨迹预测效果。

Result: 与之前的研究相比，该方法在WorldPose和3DPW数据集上表现更佳，尤其是在姿态精度方面，表明该联合模型能够应用于除了原始动机的密集互动场景之外的其他情况。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种有效的联合预测方法，表明通过联合建模轨迹和姿态可以提高人体运动的预测准确性。

Abstract: Forecasting future human motion requires jointly reasoning about an agent's global trajectory and 3D body pose, which are tightly coupled, particularly in team sports where multiple interacting players introduce complex social dynamics. We exploit a socio-temporal encoder that fuses trajectory and pose at the input level and models them jointly through shared factorized attention. The model is further enhanced by a per-joint loss reweighting scheme that emphasizes dynamic joints and prevents degenerate solutions, and a frequency-domain trajectory smoothing term that improves trajectory prediction while indirectly benefiting pose forecasting. To ensure fair comparison with prior joint forecasting methods, we identify and correct evaluation biases arising from overlapping sequence construction, adopting instead a non-overlapping protocol and a deterministic, oracle-free strategy for multi-future prediction. Our approach improves over prior joint forecasting methods on both WorldPose and 3DPW, with the largest gains on pose accuracy, demonstrating that the proposed joint formulation generalizes beyond the densely interactive scenarios that motivate it.

</details>


### [12] [Visual Grounding First, Multimodal In-context Learning Follows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30592)
*Minhyuk Seo,Minjae Lee,Chaeeun Lee,Wei Lin,Muhammad Jehanzeb Mirza,Tinne Tuytelaars,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ProCoRe的方法，通过Contrastive Reinforcement Learning在多模态对比数据上增强视觉 grounding，显著提高了多模态上下文学习能力，相比现有依赖ICL数据的方法，在不同基准测试中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在单模态上下文学习（ICL）上表现出色，但是在将这种能力扩展到多模态LLMs（MLLMs）时仍面临挑战。ProCoRe通过增强视觉 grounding来解决这一问题，从而提高MLLMs在ICL上的表现。

Method: ProCoRe的方法是通过对比强化学习生成对比图像配对的描述，并将其分解为命题，优化促进与对应图像对齐的对比奖励，同时抵制与之不匹配的奖励。该方法从未见过标准ICL样例格式，展示了显著的ICL准确度和描述生成ROUGE-L分数的提升。

Result: 与市场上现有的多模态ICL数据依赖的方法相比，ProCoRe能够在不同基准上提高平均多模态ICL分类准确率5.6%，并提高描述生成的ROUGE-L分数13.8%。

Conclusion: 本研究通过ProCoRe的方法有效地提高了MLLMs的多模态ICL能力，并提供了一个测试基准（PICL）来验证LLMs是否真正理解视觉演示，而不是依赖预训练的语义先验。

Abstract: LLMs have shown strong in-context learning (ICL) capability, but extending it to Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) remains challenging. Prior multimodal ICL methods often rely on ICL-specific datasets for additional training or task vector extraction, which can improve performance on the same ICL benchmark used for adaptation. However, they do not generalize well to other benchmarks and often induce forgetting in previously well-solved tasks. For this reason, we identify 'visual grounding' as the key bottleneck in multimodal ICL; MLLMs often fail to attend to task-relevant visual evidence in demonstrations, instead over-relying on textual cues and producing hallucinated outputs. To address this, we propose ProCoRe to enhance visual grounding through contrastive reinforcement learning on multimodal contrastive data for improving multimodal ICL. ProCoRe generates captions for contrastive image pairs, decomposes them into propositions, and optimizes contrastive rewards that encourage alignment with corresponding images while discouraging mismatched ones. Despite never seeing ICL-formatted training examples, ProCoRe improves average multimodal ICL classification accuracy by 5.6% and captioning ROUGE-L by 13.8% over the off-the-shelf Qwen-VL-3-8B model, outperforming ICL-data-dependent state of the arts across benchmarks. Finally, we introduce PICL, a personalized multimodal ICL captioning benchmark that tests whether MLLMs genuinely understand visual demonstrations rather than relying on pre-trained semantic priors.

</details>


### [13] [KinoUAV: A Benchmark for Safe and Stable UAV Vision-Language Navigation with Kinodynamic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30609)
*Chenyao Yang,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Wenhui Diao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KinoUAV 提出了一种新的基于动力学的无人机航路规划模型与环境感知方法，以提高导航的安全性和稳定性，实验结果验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机导航方法没有明确地将动力学建模或安全距离纳入航线生成中，导致绝对航向速率高、频繁碰撞和低导航成功率，影响了安全性和运动稳定性。

Method: KinoUAV 方法引入了一个分层的动力学航路规划管道，通过加速度动作驱动无人机，并使用全局路径规划的航点链生成满足速度、加速度和加加速度约束的分段轨迹，从而减少震荡并提高轨迹稳定性。此外，提出了方向自由空间距离 (DFD) 标签来描述安全运动决策的可用空间。

Result: KinoUAV-Agent 在 KinoUAV 基准上的实验表明，与现有方法相比，它在保持较低绝对航向速率和碰撞率的同时，具有最高的成功率。这证明了动力学建模在确保无人机安全稳定导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: KinoUAV 方法和相关的 KinoUAV-Agent 将有效提高无人机在复杂环境中的自主导航能力和安全性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have recently emerged as important remote sensing platforms, and UAV navigation has shown great potential in remote sensing applications. However, existing methods do not explicitly incorporate kinodynamic modeling or safety distances into trajectory generation, resulting in high absolute yaw rates, frequent collisions, and low navigation success rates, which limit safety and motion stability. To address this challenge, we introduce KinoUAV, a kinodynamic benchmark comprising a dataset and KinoUAV-Agent. First, we develop a hierarchical kinodynamic trajectory generation pipeline that drives UAV through acceleration actions. It uses waypoint chains from global planning and generates trajectories segment by segment under velocity, acceleration, and jerk constraints, reducing oscillations and improving trajectory stability. Furthermore, we introduce directional free-space distance (DFD) labels into the constructed dataset to describe the available clearance for safe motion decisions. The proposed KinoUAV-Agent predicts DFDs and uses them to generate safe actions, decoupling perception from action decision-making. This design enables autonomous obstacle avoidance and improves navigation safety. The benchmark incorporates the proposed Cruise-Seek task, in which the UAV first enters the target region and then searches for the target, reducing the difficulty of target search and improving navigation success. Experiments on KinoUAV show that KinoUAV-Agent outperforms existing methods, achieving the highest success rate while maintaining lower absolute yaw rates and collision rates. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of kinodynamic modeling for safe and stable UAV navigation.

</details>


### [14] [GSHeadRelight: Fast Relightability for 3D Gaussian Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30619)
*Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Jianzhu Guo,Xiaoqiang Liu,Pengfei Wan,Di Zhang,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种使用泛化3D高斯模型的新方法，用于人像的重新照明和新颖视角合成，仅需野外图片即可达到最先进的重新照明效果，并显著提高了渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D感知人像重新照明方法要么依赖于昂贵的数据，要么结果不佳，研究旨在克服这些限制，提出一种新的光度模型，基于统一的神经光照传输表示，能够对入射光线作出线性响应。

Method: 利用最近进展中的生成3D高斯模型，设计一种光照模型，该模型基于统一的神经光照传输表示，能够线性响应入射光。方法的实现依赖于广泛可用的野外图像数据而非昂贵的Light Stage捕获数据。

Result: 研究提出的方法在人像重新照明结果上取得了最先进的水平，并且渲染速度比之前的3D感知人像重新照明研究快了12倍。

Conclusion: 本研究为基于图像学习的3D感知人像重新照明提供了新的解决方案，该方法具有更好的3D一致性、实时性能和对未知主体的泛化能力。

Abstract: Relighting and novel view synthesis of human portraits are essential in applications such as portrait photography, virtual reality (VR), and augmented reality (AR). Despite recent progress, 3D-aware portrait relighting remains challenging due to the demands for photorealistic rendering, real-time performance, and generalization to unseen subjects. Existing works either rely on supervision from limited and expensive light stage captured data or produce suboptimal results. Moreover, many works are based on generative NeRFs, which suffer from poor 3D consistency and low real-time performance. We resort to recent progress on generative 3D Gaussians and design a lighting model based on a unified neural radiance transfer representation, which responds linearly to incident light. Using only in-the-wild images, our method achieves state-of-the-art relighting results and a significantly faster rendering speed ($\times12$) compared to previous 3D-aware portrait relighting research. Project page: http://www.geometrylearning.com/GSHeadRelight/.

</details>


### [15] [Refining Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation via Regional Semantics and Visual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30623)
*Aijing Yu,Zhengbo Wang,Lijun Sheng,Jian Liang,Xiao-Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究改进了CLIP模型以适用于密集预测任务，通过调整注意力层提取更具区域语义信息的特征，并引入可优化的特征提取器提高预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在图像级别的开放词汇分类任务中表现出色，但难以应对像素级的密集预测任务。因此，需要对CLIP进行调整以更好地进行区域和文本之间的对齐。

Method: 作者通过修改CLIP的注意力层来提取更具区域语义信息的特征，并引入视觉原型以优化文本嵌入为基础的预测器。同时，使用可优化的特征提取器，并结合指数移动平均更新策略来进一步提升预测质量。

Result: 在五个语义分割数据集上的广泛实验中，展示了此方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究改进了CLIP模型，提升了其在密集预测任务中的性能，并为未来相关领域的研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) has shown superior performance in the image-level open-vocabulary classification task, benefiting from its powerful alignment of visual features and text embeddings. However, dense prediction tasks require models to perform pixel-level predictions, necessitating modifications to CLIP for region-text alignment. In this paper, we adjust the attention layers within the vanilla visual encoder of CLIP. With a novel attention calculation method for selective attention features, the model retains more regional semantic information. To further mitigate the negative impact of the modality gap, we introduce additional visual prototypes to refine the original predictor solely based on text embeddings. Specifically, patch features with confident predictions are selected to form an additional auxiliary memory-based classifier for more accurate prediction. We further integrate an optimizable feature extractor with an exponential moving average updating strategy to enhance prediction quality. Extensive experiments on $5$ semantic segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [16] [FedBST: Bilateral Self-Training for Unsupervised Federated Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30634)
*Aijing Yu,Kuangpu Guo,Jian Liang,Xiao-Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的双侧自我训练框架FedBST，该框架通过利用确定性和不确定性数据来提高联邦学习中的无监督视觉-语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自训练方法在处理数据分布严重不均衡时会忽略不确定样本，引入偏斜的决策边界。FedBST通过充分利用高置信度数据和不确定数据，解决了这一问题。

Method: FedBST采用了一种新的自我训练策略，即不确定性感知置信度惩罚（UACP），针对不确定样本惩罚过度自信的预测，以隐式提高不确定性。同时，在推理时使用了视觉-文本集成策略，结合了局部可视原型和文本原型，以轻量级的方式适应局部分布。

Result: 实验结果显示，FedBST方法在标准联邦聚合协议下比现有方法取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: FedBST在无监督联邦学习中表现出了良好的性能提升，具有广阔的理论和应用研究价值。

Abstract: Unsupervised federated learning with Vision-Language Models (e.g., CLIP) offers a promising solution for label-free classification, yet its performance often degrades under severe non-IID shifts. A pioneering method, FedCoPL, typically relies on self-training strategies that exclusively prioritize high-confidence predictions for pseudo-labeling. However, this selective approach neglects the information of ambiguous samples (often referred to as ``waist" samples), leading to the underutilization of data and biased decision boundaries. To address this limitation, we propose FedBST, a bilateral self-training framework that effectively exploits both high-confidence and ambiguous data. Specifically, for local optimization, we introduce uncertainty-aware confidence penalization (UACP). UACP targets ambiguous samples by penalizing overconfident predictions to implicitly promote higher uncertainty, thereby preventing erroneous pseudo-labeling. At inference, we employ a visual-text ensemble strategy. This approach aggregates uncertainty-aware local visual prototypes and ensembles them with textual prototypes, achieving lightweight adaptation to local distributions via prototype-based calibration. Experiments demonstrate that FedBST achieves consistent improvements over existing methods with standard federated aggregation protocols.

</details>


### [17] [CODI: Improving Self-Supervised Vision Transformers with Cross Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30639)
*Siran Dai,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Yang Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为Cross Distillation (CODI)的方法，通过将教师类令牌分布混入每个教师 patch-token 目标中，为遮蔽的 patch 提供独立于注意力的语义修正，从而改进了密集和图像级表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现，尽管自监督的 Vision Transformers 可以学习强大的图像级表示，但在密集预测中，patch 级表示往往不可靠。文章探索了图像级表示与密集预测之间的不匹配，并提出了 CODI 方法来改进此问题。

Method: CODI 方法通过将教师类令牌分布混入每个教师 patch-token 目标中，使遮蔽的 patch 获得独立于注意力的语义修正。该方法不改变模型结构，从而保持标准的自监督学习架构。

Result: 在与预训练模式对齐的密集预测和图像级基准测试中，CODI 显著提高了性能。

Conclusion: 通过 CODI 方法，作者展示了如何通过细微调整提高自监督 Vision Transformer 的泛化能力和密集预测任务的表现。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers learn strong image-level representations, but their patch-level representations can be unreliable for dense prediction. We study this mismatch and find that the class token concentrates global semantics on a small number of representative patches, while dense prediction errors often occur on low-attention regions. We provide a local gradient analysis showing that, along the [CLS] attention readout, image-level self-distillation gradients are weighted by [CLS]-to-patch attention mass. To provide masked patches with global semantics without changing the architecture, we propose Cross Distillation (CODI), which mixes a small amount of the teacher class-token distribution into each teacher patch-token target. CODI gives every masked patch an attention-independent semantic correction while preserving the standard self-distillation architecture. Experiments on dense and image-level benchmarks show that CODI improves both dense and image-level performance under aligned pretraining settings. Code is available at https://github.com/EldercatSAM/CODI.

</details>


### [18] [Asking Less, Adapting Better: Feedback-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30643)
*Zixu Wang,Huaquan Zhang,Shoukai Xu,Jinwu Hu,Zhiquan Wen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为FETA的新方法，一种反馈高效且优化可靠的测试时适应方法，用于解决Vision-and-Language Navigation（VLN）中在未知环境中导航性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的VLN代理在未知环境中往往表现不佳，因为面临着分布变化的问题。现有的测试时适应方法虽然有潜力解决这一问题，但其反馈利用效率低且优化粒度过粗的问题促使作者提出FETA。

Method: FETA通过引入一个分层样本选择策略，首先过滤出那些策略熵波动大的轨迹，然后从每一选中的轨迹中依据熵梯度显著性标准选出关键动作，只对这些动作查询单次二元反馈。一个能反映不一致性的熵优化目标用于在这个查询步骤更新策略，其中反馈误差较大的样本所赋予的权重更大。

Result: 实验结果表明在多个基准数据集上，FETA能达成领先效果，同时能以更少的人工干预实现适应，并具备对噪反馈的鲁棒性以及持续适应能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了FETA方法，该方法在提升反馈利用效率和优化准确性方面表现出色，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow instructions and navigate to target locations. However, current VLN agents often suffer severe performance degradation in unseen environments due to distribution shifts. Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising paradigm to bridge this gap, yet existing methods that rely on unsupervised entropy minimization can propagate errors through the trajectory, causing cascading failures in navigation. Recent methods mitigate this by incorporating human feedback, but they apply trajectory-level supervision uniformly across all steps without localizing the actions is truly informative, leading to inefficient feedback utilization and mismatched optimization granularity. To address these limitations, we propose FETA, a Feedback-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation method that improves both feedback efficiency and optimization reliability. To reduce unnecessary human interventions, we introduce a hierarchical sample selection strategy to localize informative samples. Specifically, we first filter trajectories whose normalized policy entropy exhibits large fluctuations. Within each selected trajectory, we further select the critical action via an entropy-gradient saliency criterion and query a single binary feedback on its correctness. A discrepancy-aware entropy optimization objective then updates the policy only at the queried step, with adaptive scaling that assigns larger relative weights to samples with greater prediction-feedback discrepancy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FETA achieves state-of-the-art performance with fewer human interventions, while remaining robust under noisy feedback and continual adaptation settings.

</details>


### [19] [One-shot federated unsupervised domain adaptation with Smoothed Knowledge Distillation and teacher refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30646)
*Ali Abedi,Q. M. Jonathan Wu,Ning Zhang,Farhad Pourpanah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SKD-ETR 是一种新颖的一轮联邦无监督领域适应框架，通过平滑知识蒸馏 (SKD) 和基于指数移动平均 (EMA) 的教师精炼 (ETR) 方法，减少通信轮数，提高模型鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦无监督领域适应方法通常需要多轮通信，依赖复杂的聚合策略，并且难以处理嘈杂的伪标签和不一致的来源知识。为此，作者提出了 SKD-ETR，这是一种结合平滑知识蒸馏和指数移动平均教师精炼的新框架。

Method: SKD-ETR 框架结合了平滑知识蒸馏和指数移动平均教师精炼。SKD 方法通过使用源模型的集成生成的平滑软伪标签来训练目标领域的学生模型，从而减少过度自信、缓解噪声并提高鲁棒性。ETR 方法通过指数移动平均将每个教师模型的参数向学生模型进行插值，从而将目标领域的知识转移到教师。这种双向精炼在不增加通信成本的情况下提高了伪标签质量和学生模型的泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，SKD-ETR 实现了与传统方法相当的性能，但在通信和计算效率方面更具优势，并且在嘈杂的监督情况下更为稳健。

Conclusion: 综上，SKD-ETR 提供了一种有效的一轮联邦无监督领域适应方法，能够提高模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Federated unsupervised domain adaptation (FUDA) addresses the challenge of adapting models to an unlabeled target domain using decentralized source domains while preserving data privacy. However, existing FUDA methods typically require multiple communication rounds, rely on complex aggregation strategies, and often struggle with noisy pseudo labels and inconsistent source knowledge. To address these challenges, we propose SKD-ETR, a novel one-shot FUDA framework that combines Smoothed Knowledge Distillation (SKD) and Exponential moving average-based Teacher Refinement (ETR). SKD trains a student model on the target domain using smoothed soft pseudo labels generated by the ensemble of source models. This reduces overconfidence, mitigates noise, and improves robustness. ETR further refines each source model by interpolating its parameters toward the student via exponential moving average (EMA), thereby transferring target-domain knowledge back to the teachers. This bidirectional refinement enhances pseudo-label quality and student generalization without additional communication overhead. SKD-ETR eliminates complex aggregation by initializing the student model randomly and performing a single-round distillation process. Extensive experiments on OfficeHome, Office-Caltech, and DomainNet demonstrate that SKD-ETR achieves competitive performance while being communication- and computation-efficient, and robust under noisy supervision. The code for this paper is available at https://github.com/A-Abedi/SKD_ETR.

</details>


### [20] [Residual Calibration via Local Feature-Space Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30652)
*Xihao Wang,Chengyao Yu,Ruixing Ming*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LAPE的后嵌入概率调整框架，通过利用邻近校准样本的非参数局部证据来细化预测，同时保持高效和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后嵌入校准方法主要依赖于基于得分的转换，这些方法虽然提高了边际校准，但对于局部特征空间中的空间变异性校准效果较差。因此，为了克服这一不足，提出了LAPE框架。

Method: LAPE通过将全局校验输出作为稳定的锚点，并结合利用邻近校准样本的非参数局部证据来微调个体预测。此外，通过Cascade Feature Augmentation Fusion (CFAF)扩展LAPE，使其适用于多视图输入场景。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够提升各种现有基线的校准质量，并在不同的应用场景中保持高效和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LAPE框架能够有效地对预测进行后嵌入校准，并通过利用特征空间几何信息和重用校准数据来改进校准性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often struggle to provide reliable confidence estimates, making post-hoc calibration essential for reliable decision-making. Existing post-hoc methods largely rely on score-based transformations, which improve marginal calibration but often fail to capture spatially varying miscalibration in local regions of the learned feature space. To address this gap, we propose Local Adaptive Probability Estimation (LAPE), a post-hoc residual calibration framework. Specifically, LAPE treats the global calibrator output as a stable anchor and refines the individual prediction by leveraging non-parametric local evidence from neighboring calibration samples. As a plug-and-play refinement module, LAPE effectively utilizes the information about feature-space geometry and can reuse the calibration data that are used to construct the global calibrator. Furthermore, we extend LAPE to the setting with multi-view inputs through Cascade Feature Augmentation Fusion (CFAF). Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods can improve the calibration quality of various existing baselines and remain efficient and robust across different settings.

</details>


### [21] [Class-Grounded Batik Captioning under Scarce Text Annotation: Label Retention and Source-Specific Error Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30661)
*Novanto Yudistira,Muhammad Dzaka Aufa Fadhillah,Irawati Nurmala Sari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用LoRA适应的DINOv2分类器和Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型进行布佐伊图片的分类和描述任务。通过不同的条件和测试集，分析模型适应性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏专家文本标注的情况下，利用类标签作为监督信息对文化遗迹图片进行描述是一条有效途径。

Method: 采用了LoRA适应的DINOv2分类器和Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型，通过一个包含三种条件和两种提示的评估准则进行实验。

Result: 总体准确率达到96.10%，在公共来源图像上达到98.33%，但在本地图像上仅为75.76%。宏观F1分数和支持类别的平衡准确率分别达到0.8493和0.8302。参考基准的描述指标和图像文本兼容性评分给出不同的模型排名。

Conclusion: 研究表明，类标签可以保留监督信息，但在缺乏文本注释的情况下，分类和描述的质量会受到影响，尤其是在本地图像上的表现较差。

Abstract: Class labels offer compact supervision for describing cultural-heritage images when expert text annotations are scarce. We investigate a batik-captioning cascade that integrates a LoRA-adapted DINOv2 classifier with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, adapted using LoRA and loaded in 4-bit NF4. A $3\times2$ evaluation combines three captioner conditions with two inference prompts to examine adaptation and predicted-class injection. Across 150 caption-evaluation images, all three injected conditions achieve a ground-truth class-label mention rate of 0.9067. The classifier's separate test yields 96.10% pooled accuracy, with 98.33% on 300 public-source images and 75.76% on 33 local images. Supported-class macro-F1 and balanced accuracy on the local subset are 0.8493 and 0.8302, respectively. The classifier and captioner use separate partitions, and their training--evaluation overlap remains unquantified. Differences in label semantics and training support also affect interpretation of the source-specific accuracy gap. Reference-based caption metrics and image--text compatibility scores produce different model rankings. Qualitative examples show both retention of correctly predicted identities and propagation of classification errors into fluent descriptions. These findings characterize how class conditioning preserves label information and transfers upstream errors into batik captions under scarce text annotation.

</details>


### [22] [AgentDreamer: Agentic 3D Motion Reasoning for Text-Controllable Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30679)
*Mijeong Kim,Suho Park,Junha Park,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentDreamer是一种通过3D运动规划将文本指令与模拟动作对齐的代理引导优化框架。该框架利用模板函数参数化时间片段中指定的运动先验，优化不同的MPM模拟器参数以实现最优运动，进而提高文本与物理行为的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视频的指导方式无法实现高质量的物理模拟与文本指令的精准对齐，需要一种新的方法来改进。

Method: 通过代理将文本指令转化为结构化语义运动先验，并与可微MPM模拟器协同优化，以实现物理上合理的运动。

Result: 在实验中，AgentDreamer能够生成更接近所要求物理行为的模拟，文本与物理行为的对齐度优于基于视频的指导。

Conclusion: AgentDreamer通过引入模板函数参数化先验，并通过信息论度量改进了文本与模拟动作的对齐质量，为物理模拟提供了新的方法。

Abstract: We propose AgentDreamer, an agent-guided optimization framework that aligns text instructions with simulated motion through explicit 3D motion planning. Given a text instruction and scene geometry, an off-the-shelf agent translates the instruction into a structured semantic motion prior, specifying what should move, in which direction, and in what temporal order. The agent represents this prior using parametric functions over a series of temporal chunks, yielding dense 3D trajectories. We then optimize the parameters of a differentiable MPM simulator such that its rollout best matches the prior while remaining constrained by the scene dynamics. This allows the agent to provide semantic guidance from language, while the simulator grounds it in physically feasible motion. We further introduce Motion-IG, an information-theoretic metric for text-motion alignment based on masked text recovery. By comparing the likelihood of recovering masked motion-related content with and without the simulated motion, Motion-IG measures the information contributed by the motion beyond that available from the remaining text and scene context. Experiments show that AgentDreamer improves text alignment over video-based guidance, producing simulations that more closely follow the requested physical behavior.

</details>


### [23] [Zero-Shot Domain Generalization via Domain Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30680)
*Shiming Chen,Shuhuang Chen,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DoP方法，通过在文本和视觉模态中学习提示，实现无源数据的零样本领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于源域数据，收集大规模的单源或多源数据集往往不可行，限制了领域泛化的实际应用。

Method: DoP方法建立在大型的视觉语言模型（如CLIP）上，通过学习文本和视觉模态中的提示来克服测试时的领域变化。

Result: 在流行的领域泛化基准数据集（例如PACS、VLCS、OfficeHome和DomainNet）以及ImageNet上，DoP方法显著优于现有的领域泛化方法。

Conclusion: DoP方法在无源数据的零样本领域泛化方面具有较强的效果。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to improve
  the generalization of the model to any unseen domains. Targeting this goal, existing DG methods mainly focus on learning domain-invariant features or prompts from the given source domains. However, these methods rely on source-domain data, and collecting and annotating large-scale single- or multi-source datasets is often infeasible, limiting the practical applicability of DG. To tackle this challenge, we propose {\underline{Do}}main \underline{P}rompt Learning (DoP), a method built on a large vision-language model (e.g., CLIP) for zero-shot domain generalization, which recognizes unseen classes in unseen target domains without access to source-domain data. DoP essentially learns the target domain knowledge via prompt both in text and visual modalities to overcome the domain shift in text-visual modalities under the test-time adaptation formulation. Specifically, DoP first takes text domain learning (TDL) to simulate various distribution shifts by learning diverse style tokens implicitly in the text space with inter-style diversity loss and intra-style consistency loss. Subsequently, DoP employs visual domain learning (VDL) to further refine the target-domain-relevant style token generated by TDL by making use of the test visual data with self-supervised learning. Extensive experiments on popular DG benchmark datasets (e.g., PACS, VLCS, OfficeHome, and DomainNet) and ImageNet show that our DoP consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art DG approaches. Codes are available at: https://github.com/shiming-chen/DoP.

</details>


### [24] [LoRA Superposition across Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30691)
*Keigo Watanabe,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，独立在不同预训练模型上训练的LoRA可以相加并应用于所有预训练模型，这种做法称为LoRA超叠加，在多个ViT-B/16 CLIP预训练模型和图像分类任务上，相加后的LoRA显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在不同预训练模型上独立训练的LoRA是否可以相加并应用于所有模型，提高模型性能。

Method: 实验在七个ViT-B/16 CLIP预训练模型和八个图像分类任务上进行。使用相加的方法将各模型的LoRA相加，并与其他方法（如剔除一个模型后的相加）进行对比。

Result: 相加后的LoRA在所有模型-任务配对上都提高了性能，平均提高了30.21个百分点，几乎与单独训练的LoRA的性能相当。剔除一个模型后的相加方法仅提高了0.06个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，LoRA超叠加可以显著提升模型性能，且不会受到特定预训练模型的影响，提出了一种组件级的非干扰假设，认为冻结的预训练模型会在不进行显式路由或模型间对齐的情况下选择其自身的组件。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is usually tied to the pretrained backbone on which it is learned. We ask whether LoRAs trained independently for the same task on different pretrained backbones can be summed and applied unchanged to all contributing backbones. We call this construction LoRA superposition. Across seven ViT-B/16 CLIP backbones and eight image-classification tasks, the exact sum improves all 56 backbone-task pairs and achieves a mean accuracy gain of 30.21 percentage points over the unadapted backbone. The exact sum achieves a mean accuracy only 0.28 percentage points below that of individually trained solo LoRAs. In contrast, a leave-one-model-out (LOMO) sum produces a mean gain of only 0.06 percentage points over the unadapted backbones. Backbone-specific solo LoRAs are nearly orthogonal in parameter space, while pairwise cross-backbone responses are strongly concentrated on the diagonal. These results support a component-wise non-interference hypothesis, under which the frozen backbone acts as an implicit selector of its own component without explicit routing or cross-model alignment.

</details>


### [25] [ParaLin++: Accelerating Parallel Diffusion Integrator via Intrinsic Partially Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30704)
*Jianrong Lu,Zhiyu Zhu,Hui Liu,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了无训练的步长缩短方法在不同评估标准下的表现，发现这些方法在常规的标准下几乎无质量损失，但在更复杂的基准上会有明显的质量下降。研究者提出了一种新的并行采样器Paralin++，它在保持全步长离散化的同时加速了求解器迭代，实验结果表明Paralin++在多个模型和数据集上能够保持接近损失无质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型的不断改进，如何在不牺牲生成质量的前提下加速采样过程成为了一个重要的研究问题。现有的无训练的步长缩短方法虽然在某些评价指标下表现良好，但在更复杂和现实的标准下表现不佳。

Method: 研究者首先系统地评估了现有的质量度量标准，然后通过对比实验分析了不同方法在不同基准下的表现。在此基础上，研究者提出了一种新的并行采样器Paralin++，该采样器在保持全步长离散化的同时加速了求解器迭代。

Result: 实验结果表明，Paralin++在Stable Diffusion-v2、LSUN Church、FLUX.1-dev和Ming-Flash-Omni 2.0等多个模型和数据集上能够保持接近损失无质量。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明，在复杂的基准下，帕拉林++可以在不降低生成质量的同时加速采样过程。未来的工作可以进一步优化这种并行采样方式，以便在更广泛的生成模型中应用。

Abstract: Training-free few-step samplers are often described as nearly quality-lossless under conventional FID/CLIP-style evaluation. It remains unclear whether that conclusion transfers to more realistic and complex generation-quality benchmarks. We first systematize the quality metrics used to evaluate training-free sampling acceleration for diffusion and flow-matching models.
  We find that representative training-free step-shortening methods often remain nearly lossless under FID/CLIP-class metrics, yet can suffer substantial quality degradation when evaluated with semantic-composition, dense-instruction, and long-text-rendering benchmarks such as GenEval, DPG-Bench, and LongTextBench. Parallel sampling is a notable exception: by retaining the full-step discretization and removing only the solver's temporal dependency, it can preserve near-lossless quality while accelerating sampling. Building on this observation, we propose \textit{ParaLin++}, a step-parity parallel sampler that preserves the full-step discretization while accelerating the solver iterations. Experiments across Stable Diffusion-v2, LSUN Church, FLUX.1-dev, and Ming-Flash-Omni 2.0 show that ParaLin++ preserves the full-step Euler quality profile at $2.6\times$--$4.8\times$, $1.68\times$ on FLUX, and $1.21\times$ on Ming. Project page: https://paralinplus.github.io/.

</details>


### [26] [PhyEvolve: Multi-Agent State-Evolution Planning for Physics-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30717)
*Yang-Hao Zhou,Lixing Tan,Zhiqi Li,Guang Yang,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyEvolve是一种多代理框架，通过结构化的状态演化规划增强自然语言提示，实现了视频生成中的物理演化表达和连续性，优于现有模型7.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文本到视频生成模型在理解物理演化方面仍有不足，难以生成具有连续物理状态演化的视频。

Method: PhyEvolve框架由三个代理组成：导演、物理演化师和编曲师。导演确定场景并将物理事件与观察变化分离；物理演化师利用场景证据识别物理机制并构建因果链接的状态转换；编曲师利用这些转换增强原始提示。

Result: PhyEvolve在四个主要基准测试中表现出色，平均优于最先进的代理基线7.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 明确的因果状态演化规划通过自然语言提示增强提高了生成视频中的物理一致性和状态连续性。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-video generation have substantially improved visual fidelity, yet existing models still struggle to understand the physical evolution implicit in user prompts, making it difficult to generate videos with continuous physical state evolution. Here, we propose PhyEvolve, a multi-agent framework that enhances natural-language prompts through structured state-evolution planning. PhyEvolve consists of three agents: a Director, a Physical Evolver, and a Composer. Given a user prompt and an optional first frame, the Director grounds the scene and disentangles physical events from observation changes. The Physical Evolver uses scene evidence to identify physical mechanisms then constructs causally linked state transitions that unfold throughout the video, whether visible or not. The Composer utilizes these transitions to enhance the original prompts. Without retraining or model modification, PhyEvolve works directly with existing generators through prompt replacement. Extensive experiments on four major benchmarks show that PhyEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the cutting-edge prior agentic baseline by 7.9 percentage points on average. These results demonstrate that explicit causal state-evolution planning improves physical consistency and state continuity in generated videos through natural-language prompt enhancement.

</details>


### [27] [Learning from Next-Frame Prediction: Towards Unified and Scalable Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30724)
*Yifeng Yao,Jinghan Li,Zhihao Zhang,Yajiao Xiong,Yang Jin,Kun Xu,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VANE 提出了一个基于嵌入空间中下一帧预测的自监督视觉预训练框架，改进了模型和数据规模，特别是 EPIC-KITCHENS-100 的行动预见，超越了 V-JEPA 2.1，展示了嵌入空间中下一帧预测的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自监督视频预训练主要集中在从上下文恢复丢失的内容，而 VANE 集中在从过去预测未来，旨在填补这一差距。

Method: VANE 使用因果编码器总结过去观察并预测整个下一帧的表示，使用固定的语义分词器嵌入空间，强调场景内容与结构，并通过轻量级辅助生成解码器重建未来的帧补丁。

Result: VANE 表现出了随模型和数据规模增强的特点，在大多数视觉建模基准上实现了 SOTA，特别是在 EPICKITCHENS-100 动作预见上，超越 V-JEPA 2.1 获得了 7.1 分的 mCRecall@5。

Conclusion: VANE 在嵌入空间中下一帧预测方面改进了视觉世界建模，证明了其有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Video provides a natural source of supervision: what happens next serves as a learning signal for visual world modeling. Yet masked video pretraining focuses on recovering missing content from surrounding context rather than predicting the future from the past. Autoregressive methods instead predict forward one token at a time, but have yet to match joint-embedding methods in representation quality. This gap motivates us to revisit the granularity and space of future prediction. In this work, we introduce VANE, a self-supervised visual pretraining framework built on next-frame prediction in embedding space. A causal encoder summarizes past observations for a state predictor that infers the representation of the entire next frame, making temporal evolution the primary learning signal. This prediction is carried out in a fixed embedding space provided by a frozen semantic tokenizer, emphasizing scene content and structure. To retain fine-grained spatial detail alongside semantic content, a lightweight auxiliary generative decoder reconstructs future-frame patches conditioned on the predicted representations. To unify visual modeling across images and videos, we construct a local-to-global crop sequence for still images, allowing the same encoder to predict newly revealed regions from earlier crops under the same objective. Experiments show that VANE improves with model and data scaling, achieving SOTA on most anticipation, recognition, and dense perception benchmarks among unified encoders. Especially on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 action anticipation, it surpasses V-JEPA 2.1 by 7.1 points in mean-class recall@5. These results demonstrate that next-frame prediction in embedding space is effective and scalable for visual world modeling.

</details>


### [28] [SmolVLA-MoE: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Multi-Task Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30732)
*Shiyang Zhang,Miao Yang,Zhengda Li,Yang Shao,Ruifang Zhai,Hui Peng,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmolVLA-MoE通过引入稀疏的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）框架，用轻量级的专家FFN和令牌级路由器代替密集的FFN层，使得每个令牌能够自适应地选择最合适的专家来生成动作。这种设计不仅提升了模型的多任务处理能力，还在保持高效推理的同时显著提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级VLA模型使用单一密集专家处理所有任务，当计算资源受限时，其多任务能力受限。因此，提出了SmolVLA-MoE框架以增强模型的多任务处理能力。

Method: 引入稀疏的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）框架到VLA模型中的FFN层作为行动专家，并且使用令牌级路由器选择最适合行动生成的专家，同时引入一个共享专家并使用可调权重参数来平衡预训练能力和特定任务专长。通过稀疏激活，SmolVLA-MoE在推理时仅激活单个专家FFN。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中的四个任务套件上进行评估，SmolVLA-MoE的平均成功率达到了84.25%，超过了基线模型SmolVLA（82.75%）和OpenVLA-7B（76.5%），同时保持了类似的推理效率。

Conclusion: SmolVLA-MoE通过增强模型的多任务处理能力并在保持高效推理的同时提高了任务成功率，展示了在轻量级VLA模型中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in multi-task robotic manipulation by leveraging visual and language understanding for action generation. However, existing lightweight VLA models employ a single dense action expert to handle all tasks, which limits their multi-task capability under constrained computational budgets. To address this issue, we propose SmolVLA-MoE, a novel framework that introduces sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) into the FFN layers of the action expert in VLA models. SmolVLA-MoE replaces dense FFN layers with multiple parallel expert FFNs and a token-level router, enabling each token to adaptively select the most suitable expert for action generation. We further introduce a shared expert with a tunable weight parameter to balance the inheritance of pre-trained capabilities and task-specific expert specialization. Through sparse activation, SmolVLA-MoE activates only a single expert FFN during inference, with a total activated parameter count of approximately 500M including the frozen VLM backbone, while benefiting from approximately 800M total stored parameters. We evaluate our method on four task suites of the LIBERO benchmark. SmolVLA-MoE achieves an average success rate of 84.25%, outperforming SmolVLA (82.75%) and OpenVLA-7B (76.5%), while maintaining comparable inference efficiency. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/dudu435/smolvla-moe.

</details>


### [29] [Where Did the Light Come From? Diagnosing Cast-Shadow Reasoning in VLMs and Borrowing the Missing Shadow Prior from Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30740)
*Ryugo Morita,Shunsuke Kitada,Stanislav Frolov,Brian Bernhard Moser,Ko Watanabe,Riku Takahashi,Jinjia Zhou,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究引入了ShadowBench数据集，用于评估语言-视觉模型（VLMs）对阴影方向的理解能力。实验证实VLMs在读取可见阴影方向方面表现良好，但在推断缺失阴影方向和物理一致性方面表现较差。整合图像生成模型（IGMs）提高了VLMs的阴影方向推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种方法来评估视觉-语言模型对阴影方向的理解，并探索提高视觉-语言模型对光照理解的方法。

Method: 构建一个基于真实照片的ShadowBench数据集，其中包括正确阴影、阴影移除和人类标注的方向错误状态。利用该数据集评估了19个开放的VLMs和先进的自定义模型。通过引入IGMs来提高VLMs的阴影方向推理能力。

Result: VLMs在读取可见阴影方向方面表现较好，但推断缺失阴影方向和判断物理一致性方面表现差。使用IGMs后VLMs的性能有了显著提升。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型在理解阴影方面有改进空间，通过增强图像生成模型的作用可以提高其光照理解能力。

Abstract: Shadows are observable consequences of latent physical causes, arising from the interaction between illumination, object geometry, and scene structure. A model that understands shadows should be able not only to read a shadow's direction, but also to infer where a shadow should fall when it is absent, and to judge whether a shadow is physically consistent with the scene. We introduce ShadowBench, built from real photographs, which poses these three questions to vision-language models (VLMs). From each scene, we build a correct-shadow, a shadow-removed, and a human-annotated wrong-direction state. Evaluating 19 open VLMs (2B to 78B) and frontier proprietary models, we find that VLMs read a visible shadow's direction well above chance and improve with scale, yet infer the absent direction only weakly and fall to chance at judging physical consistency even under a bias-free two-alternative test that removes the yes-bias of single-image naturalness questions. Only Gemini-3.1-pro exceeds chance on validity, and even then only modestly and far below humans. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) does not help. The missing ingredient is not reasoning but knowledge that image generation models (IGMs) already hold. Routing a VLM through an IGM lifts its inference well above its direct attempt and helps it flag the wrong-direction shadows it otherwise accepts. Cast-shadow direction is a single facet of illumination understanding, but the sharp gap it reveals between VLMs and IGMs suggests image-generation or reconstruction objectives as a route toward illumination-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [30] [FilmFlow: Agentic System for 3D Virtual Film Cinematography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30753)
*Yiming Zhang,Jiarui Guo,Haojia Wei,Yifei Liu,Yifan Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FilmFlow 是一个将自然语言指令或参考视频转化为可执行的摄像机轨迹的框架，它通过导演代理和其他三个专业代理（解释者、侦察兵、批评家）的协作，生成动态3D环境中的摄像机轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前难以将摄像机运动转化为可控、可执行的摄像轨迹，FilmFlow框架旨在简化这一过程，使视觉讲故事更为直观。

Method: FilmFlow框架通过导演代理构建可执行摄像计划，该计划通过同时控制平移、旋转和构图的原子操作来组合高级动作。该导演代理由三个专业代理支持：解释者提取场景相关的摄像意图，侦察兵选择初始视角，批评家评估渲染结果并提供视觉反馈以改进计划。

Result: FilmFlow框架在150个Blender场景组成的CinemaBench基准测试中，以及在文本指令和视频参考指令生成摄像机轨迹的任务中，表现最佳，用户反馈最高。

Conclusion: FilmFlow框架展示了有效的空间推理、时间协调和视觉组成能力，为可执行3D摄影提供了强有力的支持。

Abstract: Complex camera motion is central to visual storytelling, yet translating cinematographic intent into controllable, executable camera trajectories remains challenging. We introduce FilmFlow, an agentic framework that converts natural-language instructions or reference videos into explicit camera trajectories for dynamic 3D environments. To generate these trajectories, a Director agent constructs executable camera plans by composing high-level motions from concurrent atomic operations that jointly control translation, rotation, and framing. The Director is supported by three specialized agents: an Interpreter that extracts scene-grounded cinematographic intent, a Scout that selects initial viewpoints, and a Critic that evaluates rendered results and provides visual feedback for iterative plan refinement. We also introduce CinemaBench, comprising 150 Blender scenes from self-created and licensed sources, with five camera instructions per scene for 750 text-directed tasks and 60 reference videos associated with a subset of scenes. CinemaBench supports evaluation of both text-directed camera generation and video reference-directed camera generation. Across a diverse set of evaluated baselines, FilmFlow achieves the strongest benchmark performance and the highest user preference, demonstrating effective spatial reasoning, temporal coordination, and visual composition for executable 3D cinematography.

</details>


### [31] [Evaluation of Front-End Feature Matching for Lunar Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30774)
*Guillem Casadesus Vila,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种经典和学习特征检测-匹配组合在月球车视觉里程计中的效果，尽管SuperPoint在部分低纹理场景中表现出色，但SIFT在合成数据中表现更好，研究发现经典检测器对光照敏感，而SuperPoint变化较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于月球表面光照条件恶劣且纹理信息较少，现有的特征匹配技术并不完全适用于月球车导航。为了优化视觉里程估算和相对导航性能，本文通过不同数据集评估了多个经典和基于学习的特征检测-匹配组合的表现。

Method: 本文在九个合成、月球测试场和地球模拟数据集上对比了十一种经典和学习特征检测-匹配组合，并通过光线追踪来评估其产生的航迹误差。

Result: 在仅旋转度量下，SuperPoint配对在两个月球测试场和一个模拟站点上优于SIFT与比率测试FLANN匹配，但在合成数据上效果接近；在联合旋转和平移方向评分时，SIFT配对在四个合成数据集上超过了SuperPoint配对，因此合成数据不能完全代表真实月球车图像的表现。SuperPoint配对在立体视觉里程估计中不比SIFT更精确，但是在所有八个顺序数据集中都能持续跟踪；根据光照条件的不同，经典检测器的效果会受到影响，而SuperPoint则相对变化不大。

Conclusion: SuperPoint与LightGlue是最快的组合（每帧16毫秒），但DINOv2的检索建议仍需要几何验证。合成结果无法完全揭示真实月球车图像上的相对性能，而经典特征检测器对光照敏感，增加了误差。

Abstract: Lunar rovers will primarily rely on cameras for localization until navigation satellite services are deployed, and will continue to need them afterwards for relative navigation, hazard detection, and surface operations. Visual odometry (VO) and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) mostly estimate motion by matching features between frames. However, feature matchers are developed on urban, indoor and driving scenes, planetary studies compare them on orbital registration or within complete SLAM systems, and real lunar imagery with reference poses is scarce, so matching between rover frames on low-texture, harshly lit terrain, and its effect on VO, has not been compared. We benchmark eleven classical and learned detector-matcher combinations on nine synthetic, lunar-testbed, and terrestrial-analogue datasets, and a feature tracker, scoring pairwise pose accuracy against baseline and the trajectory error of the resulting VO. Under a common rotation-only metric, the SuperPoint pairings outperform SIFT with ratio-tested FLANN matching on both lunar testbeds and one analogue site, with smaller or unresolved differences on synthetic data. When rotation and translation direction are scored jointly, this SIFT pairing exceeds both SuperPoint pairings on four synthetic datasets, so synthetic results alone do not establish relative performance on real rover imagery. In stereo VO, the SuperPoint pairings are not consistently more accurate than SIFT, but only they keep tracking on all eight sequential datasets. Pairings with classical detectors depend on lighting while SuperPoint changes little: a low spotlight makes them more accurate than diffuse light on the same testbed views, consistent with more contrast and detected keypoints. SuperPoint with LightGlue is the fastest learned combination (16 ms per frame), and DINOv2 retrieval proposals still need geometric verification.

</details>


### [32] [DeXplicit: Making Physical State Explicit in Dexterous Manipulation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30781)
*Adam Lee,Mutian Xu,Yukang Cao,Shulin Tian,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeXplicit 是一个通过先预测物体三维空间中的运动和变形，再渲染视觉后果的世界模型，相较于现有的方法，它在物体保真性的 IoU+ 上提高了 47%，速度提高了 57 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将动作条件下的动力学、相机运动和视觉合成结合在一个视频生成模型中，或者是通过点轨迹来表示动力学，没有明确的物体结构。这种隐式的表示方法使得预测未来的动作和变形变得困难，同时视觉合成需要从像素中学习复杂的物理世界动力学，增加模型的复杂性和训练难度。

Method: DeXplicit 使用一个轻量级的三维动力学网络来预测由未来的手部轨迹驱动的物体中心运动和变形，这些预测为基于的视频生成提供了一个强骨架。视频合成中，物体的外观通过预测的三维运动传输，并投影到未来视角中，这对于视觉合成来说是一个有利的条件，因为它可以使渲染器集中在完成不可见的新内容上，而不需要从像素中隐式地学习世界动力学。

Result: 与 PointWorld 以及 HOT3D、OakInk2 和 PhysTwin 中的其他方法相比，DeXplicit 的移动点误差降低了 27-40% 的相对值，物体保真性 IoU+ 提高了 47%。使用两步因果生成设计，从我们用于双向配置的 14B 模型中，视频合成速度提高了 57 倍。

Conclusion: DeXplicit 通过将三维空间中的物体运动和变形明确地预测出来，而不是仅仅通过点轨迹或在像素级别上学习动力学，提供了一种更直接和有效的策略来解决复杂的三维物体运动预测问题。这种方法有助于提高物体保真度，同时大大提高了视频合成的效率。

Abstract: Before acting on an object, a robot needs to anticipate how its hand motion will change the physical world and what the resulting scene will look like. Existing approaches typically entangle action-conditioned dynamics, camera motion, and visual synthesis within a video generation model, or represent dynamics as point trajectories without explicit object structure. We introduce DeXplicit, a dexterous manipulation world model that instead predicts how the world evolves explicitly in metric 3D before rendering its visual consequences. Given an initial scene and a future hand trajectory, a lightweight 3D dynamics network predicts object-centric motion and deformation, providing structured and geometrically grounded future world states. These predictions then serve as a strong scaffold for video generation: appearance is transported through the predicted 3D motion and projected into future views, allowing the renderer to focus on completing uncertain and newly visible content rather than implicitly learning world dynamics from pixels. This explicit-3D-first decomposition also enables efficient causal generation, requiring only two denoising steps per block while conditioning on generated history.
  Across HOT3D, OakInk2, and PhysTwin, DeXplicit reduces moved-point error by 27-40% relative to PointWorld and improves object fidelity IoU+ by 47% on HOT3D under a shared renderer. Our design with two-step causal generation achieves a 57x speedup from our 14B bi-directional configuration in video synthesis.

</details>


### [33] [WovenAnchor Matcher: Specialized Intra- and Inter-Image Context Modeling for Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30799)
*Zhiyang Li,Ruijiang Jin,Thibaut Klenke,Yusuke Sekikawa,Nakamasa Inoue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WovenAnchor Matcher (WAM) 是一个基于角色专业化上下文聚合的半稠密匹配框架，通过 WovenMamba 和 AnchorCrossAttention 两个互补的操作器分别进行图像内的水平-垂直状态空间传播和图像间的跨注意力上下文聚合，提高了基于 MegaDepth 数据集的姿态精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的半稠密局部特征匹配方法在聚合上下文信息时未能充分区分图像内和图像间聚合的角色差异。深入分析不同类型的上下文聚合需求，提出了 WovenAnchor Matcher (WAM)，旨在同时优化图像内部和跨图像的匹配准确性。

Method: WAM 引入了两个互补的操作器：WovenMamba 和 AnchorCrossAttention。WovenMamba 通过水平-垂直状态空间传播进行图像内传播，保证了垂直方向上的特征在水平上下文中的更新，有利于形成有序的二维感受野。AnchorCrossAttention 则基于图像对中的局部代表锚点进行跨图像上下文收集，通过跨注意力机制有效筛选相关和噪音特征。

Result: 在 MegaDepth 数据集上，WAM 的姿态 AUC@5° 达到了 66.1%，相较于 JamMa 基线，提高了 2.0 个 AUC 点，实验证明了图像内部传播和图像间检索通过角色特定设计带来的准确性提升。

Conclusion: WAM 通过设计特定的上下文聚合方法，显著提高了半稠密特征匹配的准确性，并展示了两种不同角色操作器的有效性，便于进一步研究和应用。

Abstract: Semi-dense local feature matching commonly aggregates contextual information before coarse-to-fine correspondence estimation. However, intra-image and inter-image aggregation serve different roles: the former should propagate spatial evidence within each image while retaining structured two-dimensional dependencies, whereas the latter should gather information from plausible corresponding regions while limiting noise from unrelated locations. We propose WovenAnchor Matcher (WAM), a semi-dense matching framework based on role-specialized context aggregation. WAM introduces two complementary operators. WovenMamba performs intra-image aggregation through horizontal-then-vertical state-space propagation, allowing vertical updates to operate on horizontally contextualized features and thereby encouraging a structured two-dimensional receptive-field bias. AnchorCrossAttention performs inter-image aggregation by using cross-attention to gather context from plausible corresponding regions across the image pair. It performs this exchange over local representative anchors that aggregate feature evidence within each window. On MegaDepth, WAM achieves 66.1 pose AUC@5$^\circ$, improving on the JamMa baseline by 2.0 AUC points under the same evaluation setup. Ablations show that removing or replacing either specialized component reduces accuracy, providing evidence that intra-image propagation and inter-image retrieval benefit from role-specific aggregation designs. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/mmaiLab/WovenAnchorMatcher.

</details>


### [34] [DRILL: Training World Models to Improve Policies, Not Predict Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30814)
*Zhaolu Kang,Tailong Luo,Lei Wei,Guangyuan Dong,Sihan Liu,Yingjie He,Siheng Wang,Shuaibo Li,Rongchao Zhang,Zhen Tian,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRILL框架，旨在通过最大化策略的下游回报来优化世界模型，而不是仅仅预测像素。DRILL-VWI通过重新加权预测误差来实现这一目标，而DRILL-IMG则通过内层更新的梯度来实现。实验证明，DRILL比现有方法更优，特别是在视觉语言动作任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型通常旨在预测像素级的一帧内容，但这并不是它们的主要作用。作者认为，应该通过构建能产生有用学习信号的预测模型来提升策略，而不是仅仅提高像素预测的准确性。为此，作者提出了DRILL框架。

Method: DRILL框架由两个实例组成：DRILL-VWI和DRILL-IMG。DRILL-VWI通过重新加权预测误差，即根据算法访问、群体相对价值优势和模型置信度来重新加权预测误差。DRILL-IMG则使用了部分Hessian向量乘积来对内层更新的梯度进行求导。

Result: 通过在多种操纵模拟器和视觉语言动作（VLA）模型上的实验，DRILL-IMG方法在两种VLA基准模型上均取得了显著的性能提升，平均成功率提高了13.1%，相比基线方法提高。此外，DRILL-IMG能够与自监督学习方法协同工作，而不仅仅与之竞争。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DRILL框架标志着在视觉语言动作任务中，通过构建有效生成学习信号的世界模型，提升策略学习的有效性。作者指出，今后可以考虑通过策略梯度信号来训练视频世界模型，而不是仅仅关注像素级预测。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to reduce environment interaction when post-training vision-language-action (VLA) policies with reinforcement learning. Yet they are typically trained to predict pixels one step ahead, even though their role here is not merely to render visually faithful videos but to produce learning signals from imagined rollouts that improve the policy. We show that this mismatch is substantial: lower pixel MSE can produce worse policy gradients, and a world model trained for policy utility can yield better downstream policies despite 17% higher pixel MSE on the next frame. We propose the Downstream-Return Imagination Learning Loop (DRILL), a bilevel framework that updates the world model to maximize the policy's return after an inner GRPO update on imagined rollouts. DRILL has two instantiations. DRILL-VWI is a closed-form surrogate, requiring no additional simulator rollouts, that reweights prediction errors by policy visitation, the group-relative GRPO advantage, and model confidence. DRILL-IMG is a full meta-gradient method that differentiates through the inner update using truncated Hessian-vector products. We show that DRILL-VWI recovers the local one-step component of the DRILL-IMG meta-gradient under score-compatibility and small-residual assumptions. Across five manipulation simulators and two VLA backbones, DRILL-IMG raises mean success rate by 13.1 points over WMPO and 7.9 points over RLVR-World, and complements RLVR pretraining rather than competing with it. More broadly, we believe video world models for VLA reinforcement learning can be productively trained by the policy gradients they produce, not only by pixel-level prediction.

</details>


### [35] [Loop to Look Better: Learning Adaptive Depth for Question-Guided Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30824)
*Wencheng Ye,Anning Hu,Hongye Fang,Tianyi Wang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopLook 是一种轻量级的循环感知框架，增强了预训练的 Vision-Language 模型，通过逐步精炼视觉注意力，并根据任务难度自适应分配深度，从而提高模型表现，同时仅轻微增加时间到首个输出时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有 VLM 通常在一个任务无关的编码器中一次性获取视觉标记，导致了推理过程中视觉证据无法及时精炼的瓶颈问题。因此，研究者提出 LoopLook 框架来解决这一问题。

Method: LoopLook 在视觉编码器的中间到后期阶段进行循环回访，并在每次循环中注入问题表示以指导视觉精炼。此外，引入了自适应终止机制和 token 智能组合的机制来决定并优化循环深度。

Result: LoopLook 在多种基础模型和基准测试中，表现出显著的性能提升，相比于基线在特定基准测试上收益高达 8.6% 和 8.1%。

Conclusion: LoopLook 通过将循环机制引入视觉感知，使得视觉感知从一次性视角转变为可适应性循环细化的过程，有效地解决了视觉证据的精炼瓶颈，同时保持了较低的时间开销。

Abstract: Visual perception should be iterative and task-directed, with representations refined in response to the question and the need for further processing. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly support extended reasoning, their visual tokens are still typically acquired in a single, task-agnostic encoder pass. This creates an evidence-acquisition bottleneck: models may reason longer without refining the visual evidence needed to answer the question. We propose LoopLook, a lightweight encoder-side recurrent perception framework that equips pretrained VLMs with adaptive visual depth. LoopLook recurrently revisits a middle-to-late window of the vision encoder, injecting question representations at each loop to guide visual refinement. A learned stopping mechanism adaptively determines the recurrent depth, while a token-wise Mixture-of-Looks (MoL) combines representations from different loops, allowing each visual token to selectively integrate information acquired across recurrent steps. Across four backbones and eight benchmarks, LoopLook consistently improves performance over the baseline, yielding gains of up to 8.6% on MMStar and 8.1% on HallusionBench. Our analysis shows that LoopLook progressively refines visual attention and adaptively allocates depth according to task difficulty. It also better preserves task-relevant visual information under reduced input resolution, while increasing time to first token (TTFT) by only 5.1-6.5%. By bringing recurrence from reasoning back into perception, LoopLook transforms visual perception from a one-shot glimpse into an adaptive process of recurrent refinement.

</details>


### [36] [One Evaluation Is Enough: Inversion-Free Image Editing with Trajectory Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30827)
*Lifan Jiang,Taiyang Chen,Senlin cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECT（Endpoint-Calibrated Transport）是一个无需图像推断、无需轨迹集成、无需二次时间观测或迭代优化的闭合形式、无训练编辑器，只需一个噪声级别，两个文本条件打包到一个除噪批次中，和一次潜在更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的实时图像编辑方法通常依赖于图像反转和迭代采样，而高效的一步编辑器通过在多个噪声级别估计并平均编辑场来稳定较大的更新，以提高效率。论文作者质疑是否可以通过单一时域场评估来实现可靠的无反转编辑。

Method: ECT方法利用了维数保存（VP）一步模型中的先前未充分利用的双重性，通过综合已知的剩余VP角度上的切线来获得名义上的一步运输，并通过约束投影使用端点位移来修正其长时段外推。这种方法被称为Endpoint-Calibrated Transport (ECT)。

Result: ECT在报告的基准设置中提供了极其短的编辑时间内稳定的编辑质量改进。

Conclusion: ECT是一种闭合形式的、不需训练的编辑器，具有直接应用于预训练的ε预测VP一步生成器的特点。

Abstract: Real-image editing commonly relies on inversion and iterative sampling, while efficient one-step editors often stabilize a large update by estimating and averaging editing fields at multiple noise levels. We ask a stricter question: is a single temporal field evaluation sufficient for reliable inversion-free editing? Our answer rests on a previously underused duality in a variance-preserving (VP) one-step model: one source-target query at a shared noisy state simultaneously determines a local angular semantic tangent and a clean endpoint displacement. We integrate the tangent over the analytically known remaining VP angle to obtain a nominal one-step transport, and use the endpoint displacement to correct its long-horizon extrapolation through a constrained projection. This transport-first, correction-second construction yields Endpoint-Calibrated Transport (ECT), a closed-form, training-free editor requiring only one noise level, two text conditions packed into one denoiser batch, and one latent update. ECT requires no image inversion, trajectory integration, second temporal observation, or iterative optimization, and can be directly applied to pretrained epsilon-prediction VP one-step generators. Across the reported benchmark settings, ECT delivers stable gains in editing quality within an extremely short editing time.

</details>


### [37] [RECAP: Looking Once Is Not Enough for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30834)
*Zhaolu Kang,Tailong Luo,Chenxin Li,Yingjie He,Zhenyu Yu,Fengyu Zhou,Jiachen Qian,Lei Wei,Shuang Chen,Jiachen Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Rongchao Zhang,Zhengtao Yao,Hoi Leong Lee,Guansu Wang,Kaiyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过视觉回顾技术解决了长时间链式推理中图像证据与生成过程脱节的问题，提出RECAP方法改进了视觉语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
