Motivation: 针对视觉语言模型在推理过程中图像证据与生成过程脱节的问题，研究希望通过引入视觉回顾技术来增强模型的视觉理解能力，提升其在视觉推理任务中的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 研究通过增加视觉回顾信号，提出了一种RECAP训练方法，利用视觉回顾条件的优势估计、动态折扣和门控价值预测来传播最终奖励，使模型能够更好地利用重要视觉推理步骤的信息。这种方法在多个模型上进行了测试，包括Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3-8B和LLaVA-OV-7B。

Result: RECAP方法在HallusionBench基准测试中取得了+6.1--+6.5的改进，同时保持了文本推理能力。进一步的实验还表明，这种方法在更大规模的模型、中文零样本基准测试、视觉扰动和人类评价中都表现出了稳健的改进。

Conclusion: RECAP方法有效解决了视觉语言模型推理过程中的时间对齐问题，显著提高了模型的视觉推理能力，尤其是在大规模模型和汉语环境中表现出色。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves vision-language models (VLMs), but it also exposes a temporal grounding failure: as generation unfolds, models may drift from image evidence while reinforcement learning still provides only a final-answer reward. We study this gap through visual revisit, a temporally localized reactivation of image evidence during reasoning. Across three VLM backbones, revisit peaks are predictive of correctness and causally linked to performance: masking them degrades accuracy more than masking non-peaks, while injecting revisit-like peaks into failed rollouts partially restores correct answers. Control analyses show that this signal is not explained by raw attention strength, uncertainty, logit margin, or hidden-state norm. We propose RECAP, a GRPO-compatible training method that turns detached revisit traces into a credit-assignment signal. RECAP uses revisit-conditioned advantage estimation, dynamic discounting, and gated value prediction to propagate final rewards through visually important reasoning steps, without extra rewards, additional rollouts, or inference-time changes. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, InternVL3-8B, and LLaVA-OV-7B, RECAP consistently improves over GRPO and VPPO, yielding $+6.1$--$+6.5$ HallusionBench gains over the SFT base while preserving reasoning and text-only performance. Results on larger LoRA-tuned models, Chinese zero-shot benchmarks, visual perturbations, and human evaluation further demonstrate robust improvements in image-grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [CAVC: Convergence-Adaptive Audio-Visual Cache for Modality-Asymmetric Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30854)
*Lifan Jiang,Yibo Zhao,Senlin cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAVC（Convergence-Adaptive Audio-Visual Cache）通过在音频和视频处理之间实现异步缓存策略，实现了在保留音频视觉同步的同时，提高了1.10-1.35倍的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散的音频视觉生成方法在每个时间步骤上都会进行联合去噪和跨模态注意力计算，这并非总是必要的。CAVC提出了一种无需训练的模态异步缓存框架，旨在提高效率的同时保持视觉细节和音频同步。

Method: CAVC采用了一种夹心式计算分配策略：在检测到音频去噪器输出进入缓存可容忍阶段后，缓存并重复使用音频去噪器输出，以绕过中间阶段的音频去噪器和跨模态注意力计算，视频分支则继续独立的空间-时间细化；在计算的末尾进行几个联合步骤，以减少累积的跨模态漂移并提高同步性。

Result: 在多个公开基准测试中的实验表明，CAVC实现了1.10-1.35倍的墙钟加速，提高了多种视觉动态和时间统计特性，并保持了竞争力的音频保真度和音频视觉对齐。

Conclusion: CAVC显示了一种有效的方法，能够在保持视音频同步的同时显著提高性能，是一项对基于扩散的音频-视觉生成模型有益的创新。

Abstract: Diffusion-based audio-visual generation methods typically perform joint denoising of audio and video latents throughout the entire sampling trajectory, repeatedly executing modality-specific denoising branches and cross-modal attention at every timestep. However, we observe that such uniform joint computation is not always necessary. The key is a modality-asymmetric recoverability: audio-side drift introduced by mid-stage caching can be substantially reduced by only a few terminal joint denoising steps, whereas video-side degradation caused by caching leads to persistent loss of motion and temporal details. This asymmetry suggests that inference acceleration should selectively cache recoverable audio-side computation while preserving full video refinement. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Convergence-Adaptive Audio-Visual Cache (CAVC)}, a training-free modality-asymmetric caching framework. CAVC adopts a sandwich-structured computation allocation: after detecting that audio denoiser outputs enter a cache-tolerant regime, it caches and reuses the audio denoiser output to bypass audio denoising and cross-modal attention in the middle stage, while the video branch continues independent spatio-temporal refinement; a few joint steps are then restored at the end to reduce accumulated cross-modal drift and improve synchronization. Experiments across multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that CAVC achieves 1.10--1.35x wall-clock acceleration, improves several visual dynamics and temporal statistics, and maintains competitive audio fidelity and audio-visual alignment.

</details>


### [39] [Direct Forcing: A Single-Stage Training Recipe for Few-Step Autoregressive Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30861)
*Haojun Xu,Jie Huang,Xin Lu,Mingchen Zhong,Zihao Fan,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Direct Forcing 提出了一种单阶段框架，通过参考视频同时学习因果延续和目标视频分布，该方法不需要初始化阶段或在线扩散评分监督，能够在250次更新内训练一个四步自回归生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的训练管道将因果适应和分布匹配分开，Direct Forcing 意图提供一种整合方式，直接从参考视频中学习。

Method: 这种方法使用参考视频作为配对示例和自我生成抽样的目标群体，引入了因果延续对齐和生成抽样分布匹配，动态梯度平衡调整这两种梯度贡献的相对比例。

Result: Direct Forcing 在 250 次更新内从双向 Wan2.1-1.3B 开始训练了一个四步自回归生成器，并获得了 84.63 的 VBench 总分和 86.01 的质量评分，消融研究进一步展示了动态梯度平衡和不同特征层的表现偏好。

Conclusion: Direct Forcing 良好的性能证明了结合参考视频进行因果延续和目标分布学习的有效性，特别是对于自回归视频生成任务。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generation enables low-latency streaming, yet prevailing training pipelines separate causal adaptation from distribution matching. Recent representation-based text-to-video methods retain this separation by starting from ODE-initialized checkpoints, even when the target distribution is defined directly by reference videos. We introduce Direct Forcing, a single-stage framework that uses reference videos both as paired examples of how a history continues and as a target population for self-generated rollouts. Causal Continuation Alignment learns paired spatiotemporal correspondence under reference histories, while Rollout Distribution Matching uses maximum mean discrepancy to align the representation distributions of self-generated and reference videos. Together, these complementary signals allow a pretrained video model to learn causal continuation and the target video distribution in a single training stage. Dynamic Gradient Balancing adapts the relative gradient contributions of continuation alignment and distribution matching during training. Starting from bidirectional Wan2.1-1.3B, Direct Forcing learns a four-step autoregressive generator in 250 updates without a separate initialization stage or online diffusion-score supervision, achieving 84.63 VBench Total and 86.01 Quality. Ablation studies further demonstrate the benefits of dynamic gradient balancing and reveal distinct representation preferences: deeper features benefit continuation alignment, whereas intermediate features yield better performance for rollout distribution matching.

</details>


### [40] [Cross-Spectral Feature Fusion for Multispectral Document Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30863)
*Yasas Savinda,Maheshi Buddhinee Dissanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过使用多分支条件生成对抗网络(cGAN)处理500、600、700和1000纳米的SMADI波段，并在编码器深度L=3处使用空间和通道自softmax门融合可见光、近红外和结构特征流，同时利用选定的可见光-近红外波段对辅助边缘图提供定向的结构指导，研究提出的方法可以显著提升多光谱文档二值化效果，尤其是在F1、p-FM和IoU指标上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光谱文档二值化面临挑战，特别是在处理具有光谱依赖性退化现象的历史文档时。现有方法如固定融合和通用注意力机制在复杂情况下的效果不佳。因此，本研究旨在探索适应性交叉光谱特征融合和定向结构指导如何改进多光谱文档二值化。

Method: 研究采用了多分支条件生成对抗网络(cGAN)处理多光谱数据，并在编码器第三层（L=3）融合可见光、近红外和结构特征。同时引入自softmax门机制、辅助边缘图作为定向结构指导。

Result: 实验结果显示，F3 + NIR 边缘导向配置的整体性能最佳，F1 得分为0.7916，p-FM 为0.8044，IoU 为0.6654。该方法相较于固定中值平均baseline在F1、p-FM和IoU上分别提高了0.0269、0.0360和0.0321，并且这些提升在Holm校正后依然显著。此外，平均NRM和DRD也有所下降，但这些改变经过Holm校正后不具有统计学意义。

Conclusion: 研究证明了定向交叉光谱结构指导在调节多光谱融合中的有效性，提升了主要前景重叠性能，同时保留了完整的四个波段观测数据。这为进一步改进和应用多光谱文档二值化提供了可能的新方向。

Abstract: Historical document binarization remains challenging when faded ink, bleed-through, and background degradation exhibit wavelength-dependent behavior across visible and near-infrared observations. This study investigates whether adaptive cross-spectral feature fusion and targeted structural guidance can improve multispectral document binarization beyond fixed fusion and generic attention mechanisms.
  Four registered SMADI bands at 500, 600, 700, and 1000 nm are processed using a multi-branch conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN). Visible, NIR, and structural feature streams are fused at encoder depth L = 3 using spatial- and channel-wise softmax gating, while auxiliary edge maps derived from selected VIS-NIR band pairs provide targeted structural guidance. Nine controlled fusion and structural configurations are evaluated using five-fold document-level cross-validation, DIBCO-standard metrics, and Holm-corrected statistical testing.
  The F3 + NIR edge-guided configuration provides the strongest overall performance among the evaluated models, achieving mean F1 = 0.7916, p-FM = 0.8044, and IoU = 0.6654. Relative to the fixed Mid AVG baseline, it improves F1 by 0.0269, p-FM by 0.0360, and IoU by 0.0321, with all three gains remaining statistically significant after Holm correction. Mean NRM and DRD are also reduced, although these reductions are not statistically significant after correction.
  Targeted cross-spectral structural guidance provides an effective mechanism for regulating intermediate multispectral fusion, improving primary foreground-overlap performance while retaining the complete four-band observation.

</details>


### [41] [Verus-Insight: Towards Explainable Artifact Detection in AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30866)
*Zhuoran Zhang,Yang Shi,Qixun Wang,Tengfei Liu,Yuqi Tang,Xuanyu Zhu,Yizhuo Jia,Jingyun Hua,Shixuan Liu,Xianghua Ying,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了Verus-Insight，一种通过结构化过程进行生成物检测的方法，包括检测规划、证据验证和证据聚合。该方法在人类标注的基准上进行了训练，展示了在细微生成物检测和合成视频检测方面优于其他方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的检测器虽然能够识别生成物，但有时无法识别细微的生成特征，或不能准确地识别出证据。因此需要一种更有效的生成物检测方法。

Method: 研究者将生成物检测分解为三个互补能力：检测规划、证据验证和证据聚合。Verus-Insight通过全周期的人类监督和渐进式能力训练获得改进。

Result: Verus-Insight在多种基准测试中取得了良好的性能，尤其是在细微生成物分析和合成视频检测方面较其他方法有了显著提升。

Conclusion: Verus-Insight提供了一种新的方法来提高生成物检测的准确性，并通过Verus-Bench评估了生成物检测的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made synthetic videos increasingly realistic, placing greater demands on reliable AI-generated video detection. Existing artifact-based detectors typically identify generation artifacts as evidence for source prediction, yet they may either fail to recognize subtle artifacts or reach the correct source decision without faithfully identifying the evidence underlying it. In this work, we systematically investigate how to design and train effective artifact detectors and formulate artifact detection as a structured process comprising three complementary capabilities: Inspection Planning, which determines what should be examined; Evidence Verification, which verifies whether the inspected content exhibits genuine generation artifacts; and Evidence Aggregation, which integrates the verified evidence into the final source decision. Building on this formulation, we introduce Verus-Insight, trained with human-anchored full-process supervision and progressive capability training to strengthen both fine-grained artifact detection and video-level detection. We further introduce Verus-Bench, a benchmark with fine-grained human annotations of perceptible artifacts for evaluating artifact detection. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that Verus-Insight achieves strong performance in both fine-grained artifact analysis and AI-generated video detection, with substantial gains in recovering human-perceived artifacts. Further analyses reveal that the three capabilities are complementary yet distinct, and that Verus-Insight more reliably converts relevant inspection targets into supported artifact evidence without increasing spurious artifact claims.

</details>


### [42] [HimmBO: High-Dimensional Interactive Model-Merging Bayesian Optimization for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30875)
*Jinyuan Mao,Zhihao Shen,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HimmBO 是一种两阶段方法，能够处理大规模的适配器集合并减少生成图像的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大规模适配器集合时受限于昂贵的图像生成，且交互次数有限。HimmBO 方法旨在解决此问题，提升偏好驱动的适配器融合的效率。

Method: HimmBO 分为筛选和优化两个阶段。筛选阶段通过预生成图像进行层级集估计以恢复活跃适配器集，无需生成新图像；优化阶段在低维子空间内优化合并系数。

Result: 相比现有方法，HimmBO 在模拟用户图像匹配实验中达到了更高的最终相似度，所需图像生成次数减少了十分之一，性能随适配器集合增长到1080个仍保持稳定，而竞品方法性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: HimmBO 通过有效筛选和优化策略，显著降低了大规模适配器集的偏好驱动合并成本，提高了效率和稳定性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has become a common way to customize text-to-image models, leading to community collections of over 100K lightweight adapters. Adapters sharing a base model can be merged with continuous coefficients. This turns a large collection into a high-dimensional continuous design space, which recent work explores interactively using preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO). However, existing approaches are limited to a few dozen preselected adapters and require expensive image generation in every interaction round. We introduce HimmBO, a two-stage method that scales preference-driven adapter merging to much larger collections while reducing image-generation cost. Its screening stage recovers the active adapter set through level-set estimation over pre-generated images, requiring no image generation. The refinement stage then optimizes merge coefficients in the recovered low-dimensional subspace. Both stages use a style-aware kernel, allowing preference information to transfer between visually similar adapters. In simulated-user image-matching experiments, HimmBO achieves higher final similarity than all comparisons while reaching a given similarity with under one tenth of the image generations required by prior work. Its performance remains stable as the collection grows to 1080 adapters, while competing methods degrade substantially.

</details>


### [43] [When Annotation Patterns Are Learned: Skin-Tone Dependence in Dermatology Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30901)
*Y. Li,M. Bigliardi-Qi,S. Bedros,P. Bigliardi,N. Papanikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究审计了广泛使用的皮肤科概念数据集SkinCon中不同肤色之间的可靠性差异，发现不同类型的皮疹在不同肤色上的标注频率显著不同，这可能导致基于这些标注的模型对某些皮疹的诊断出现偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤疾病的诊断依赖于可靠的皮肤病变概念标注，然而现有数据集在不同肤色上的标注可能存在偏差，这可能影响诊断准确性。因此，研究旨在审计广泛使用的皮肤病概念数据集SkinCon在不同肤色下的可靠性差异。

Method: 研究采用了元分析的方法，对同一疾病内去除近似重复的样本在不同肤色下的概念标注进行了比较分析，并训练了概念模型验证标注模式。

Result: 研究发现，红斑在深肤色上标注频率远低于浅肤色，而色素沉着和色素减退的标注频率则较高。概念模型在训练和测试阶段均再现了这一模式。基于这一模式的模型可能导致忽略潜在的红斑诊断。

Conclusion: 研究指出，概念标注应在每个肤色亚组内进行审计，以确保基于这些标注的解释准确可靠。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) let clinicians inspect and correct skin disease diagnoses through clinical concepts, but only if the concept annotations are reliable. We therefore audit the reliability of SkinCon, a widely used dermatology concept dataset, across skin tones. Within the same disease and with near duplicates removed, erythema is annotated far less often on dark than on light skin, with an odds ratio of 0.17. In contrast, hyperpigmentation and hypopigmentation are annotated more often, with odds ratios of 6.2 and 4.5, respectively. We refer to this pattern as tone-dependent concept annotation. An evidential concept model trained on these annotations learns the same pattern and reproduces it on unseen images. Such a model may agree with potential erythema omissions rather than contradict them. Consistent with this, three label error detectors underperform a disease-only baseline at finding erythema that dermatologist captions mention but SkinCon labels as absent. The three detectors are based on confident learning, training dynamics, and the model's own evidential uncertainty. Concept annotations should thus be audited within each subgroup before explanations built on them can be trusted.

</details>

*Dominik Engel,Yousef Rajeh,Leon Sick,Ivan Viola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过将CLIP等视听特征提升至3D高斯重构，提出了一种新的自然语言查询驱动的开放词汇分割方法。该方法通过对固定提升特征场进行处理，部分减去共享场景方向，并对文本原型进行归一化，从而改善了多类别和单查询分割任务的表现，而无需重新训练或重新编码特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在不同场景下的特征分布差异较大，而CLIP等模型对语义概念的表示虽然广泛，但在具体场景中的特征却可能表现出强烈的方向性集中。因此，需要一种方法来更好地利用这些提升的特征进行开放词汇的分割。

Method: 本研究提出了一种基于场景条件的比较方法，通过部分减去共享场景方向并归一化文本原型，改进了固定提升特征场与文本的比较方式。具体而言，方法包括：1) 部分减去共享场景方向；2) 归一化文本原型；3) 使用软聚类进行多类别预测；4) 使用CSLS方法结合余相关性边界进行单查询预测。

Result: 该方法在多种提升方法和多个数据集上测试，结果表明，改进的特征场比较方式可以有效减轻特征方向集中问题，提高类间特征分离度，从而在多类别和单查询分割任务中提升了平均交并比。

Conclusion: 通过场景条件化比较，该研究展示了提升表示在开放词汇分割任务中的潜力，证明了无需重新训练、重编码或重新提升特征即可提升现有提升表示的使用价值的准确性。

Abstract: Lifting vision-language features such as CLIP into 3D Gaussian reconstructions enables open-vocabulary segmentation through natural-language queries. Although CLIP represents a broad range of semantic concepts, the lifted features of individual scenes can exhibit strong directional concentration, while raw cosine similarity does not account for the scene-specific feature distribution. We show that substantial segmentation gains can be obtained by changing how a fixed lifted feature field is compared with text. Our method partially subtracts a shared scene direction from visual features and text prototypes and whitens text prototypes using a fixed reference vocabulary of generic terms. It then uses soft clustering for multiclass prediction and CSLS with a relevance margin against the reference vocabulary for single-query prediction. Beyond reducing directional concentration, scene centering improves class-relative feature separation in every evaluated scene. Across seven lifting methods and four datasets, the resulting query-time calibration consistently improves mean intersection over union in both multiclass and single-query segmentation. These gains require no retraining, source-image re-encoding, or feature re-lifting, demonstrating the value of scene-conditioned comparison for making better use of existing lifted representations.

</details>


### [45] [Hierarchical Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Alignment via Dynamic Granularity Discovery and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30912)
*Pengfeng Song,Shaozhong Zhang,Pengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HFCMA 是一种分层细粒度跨模态对齐框架，通过动态粒度发现（DGD）将原始块和标记聚合为语义紧凑的视觉区域和文本短语，并通过熵正则化的最优运输问题进行区域短语匹配，引入背景偏差校正、文本重要性加权和原型一致性正则化，以增强细粒度评分的局部对齐信息，改善微妙的语义差异区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像-文本检索方法在构建语义单元和全局匹配一致性方面存在局限性，导致难以精确区分语义相似的候选对象。

Method: HFCMA 使用动态粒度发现（DGD）将原始块和标记聚合为语义紧凑的视觉区域和文本短语，然后通过熵正则化的最优运输问题进行区域短语匹配。此外，HFCMA 还引入了背景偏差校正、文本重要性加权和原型一致性正则化，以减少背景噪声和局部语义漂移。

Result: 在 Flickr30K 和 MS-COCO 数据集上的实验表明，HFCMA 能够有效地结合动态语义单元构建与基于最优运输的结构化匹配，从而在双编码器检索框架下产生细粒度评分，提供结构化的局部对齐信息，增强对候选对象的区分。

Conclusion: HFCMA 通过分层的跨模态对齐和高级匹配策略，显著提高了图像-文本检索的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-grained image-text retrieval requires a model not only to assess global semantic consistency between an image and a text, but also to model visual evidence for local textual semantics, including objects, attributes, quantities, actions, and relations, so as to distinguish semantically similar candidates. Existing methods commonly use fixed image patches, detected regions, or text tokens as the basic matching units and then aggregate local interactions through max pooling, cross attention, or similarity accumulation. Such strategies remain limited in semantic unit construction and global matching consistency. This paper proposes a hierarchical fine-grained cross-modal alignment framework, termed HFCMA. The framework progressively transforms low-level patch and token representations into adaptive visual regions and textual phrases, and then performs structured region-phrase matching within a global retrieval scoring process. Specifically, HFCMA first adopts dynamic granularity discovery (DGD) to aggregate raw patches and tokens into semantically compact visual regions and textual phrases. It then formulates region-phrase matching as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem, thereby estimating a structured alignment matrix under global marginal constraints. To reduce background noise and local semantic drift, HFCMA further introduces background debiasing, textual importance weighting, and prototype consistency regularization. Experiments on Flickr30K and MS-COCO show that HFCMA effectively combines dynamic semantic unit construction with OT-based structured matching under a dual-encoder retrieval framework. The resulting fine-grained score provides structured local alignment information for candidate ranking and improves the discrimination of subtle semantic differences.

</details>


### [46] [Watch and Learn: Teaching Visual In-Context Learning by Removing Textual Shortcuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30913)
*Nimrod Shabtay,Asaf Yehudai,Avihu Dekel,Eli Schwartz,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究指出视觉语言模型在基于视觉的上下文学习方面表现不佳，并构建了MIME数据集，通过去除文本捷径提高模型表现。DTAD方法进一步提高了模型对示范图片的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在上下文学习方面表现不如纯文本模型，特别是基于视觉的上下文学习（vICL）方面更加薄弱。

Method: 构建MIME数据集与DTAD方法。MIME数据集提供视觉示例但不提供直接规则说明，使模型需要从视觉中推断信息。DTAD方法在训练时随机遮蔽示范标签，促使模型更好地理解视觉示例。

Result: 在三个视觉语言模型中，MIME数据集的训练显著提高了vICL准确率，DTAD方法则进一步提高了模型对示范图片的注意。同时，通过MIME和DTAD方法，提高了LLaVA 1.5 13B的多图vICL准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，基于视觉的上下文学习数据应成为视觉语言模型训练中的标准组成部分，以提升模型的视觉推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with in context learning compared to their textual counterparts. Our preliminary study shows that VLMs rely on textual shortcuts and underutilize the visual demonstrations. We therefore hypothesize that VLMs are insufficiently trained to perform visual in-context learning (vICL). To this end, we construct MIME, a task-agnostic vICL training set without textual shortcuts. We ensure the instruction does not state the rule linking the demonstration images to their answers, so the model must infer it from the visual demonstrations. Fine-tuning on MIME improves vICL accuracy by 8.1 to 18.0 points across three VLMs, preserves single-image performance, and improves multi-image and text only in-context learning. In contrast, stating the rule in the training data sharply reduces this gain. We further propose DTAD (Demonstration Text Attention Dropout), which randomly withholds demonstration labels during training so that the VLM must infer the rule from the demonstration images. While achieving comparable vICL accuracy to fine-tuning on MIME, DTAD yields more consistent attention to the demonstration images. On TrueMICL, Qwen3-VL-8B trained on MIME matches frontier models such as Claude Opus 5, and surpasses them with DTAD. Finally, adding MIME to the instruction-tuning mix of LLaVA 1.5 13B more than doubles its vICL accuracy without hurting single-image abilities, whereas a same-size multi-image set improves it only slightly. These results suggest that vICL data should be a standard component of VLM training.

</details>


### [47] [Making MLLM Latents Matter: Factor-Aware Predictive Learning for Cinematic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30926)
*Songlin Yang,Zhe Wang,Xiaotong Zhao,Xi Wang,Ran Zhang,Shuai Li,Taiyi Wu,Songlian Li,Eric Liu,Alan Zhao,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAPL 提出了一种新的方法来利用 MLLM 的潜在表示，通过互补的学习信号增强扩散变换器（DiT）的生成能力，实现了对角色、场景、动作和摄像机的多因素控制以及对有限输入的时空推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在利用大模型语言的先验知识进行生成时缺乏对角色、场景、动作和摄像机的多因素控制能力，以及时空推理水平有限。

Method: FAPL 通过两个阶段训练扩散变换器（DiT）。第一阶段，仅潜在变量生成器地基训练 DiT，增强了其利用大模型语言潜在表示的生成能力；第二阶段，基于配对视频目标和信息观测目标，分别进行多因素控制训练和时空推理训练。

Result: 在三个关键任务（稀疏到视频、多因素编辑和多镜头生成）中，FAPL 在性能上优于基线方法，证明了大模型语言特征使用、时空控制和多因素协调方面的贡献。

Conclusion: FAPL 提出了一种有效的方法，通过强化扩散变换器在使用语言大模型先验方面的表现，同时还保留了完整观测重建的补充训练模式，提升了在影视视频生成任务中的能力。

Abstract: Cinematic video generation requires both temporal inference from limited inputs (i.e., sparse rather than frame-by-frame guidance) and factor-aware control of character, scene, action, and camera. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer useful priors for both capabilities, but their representations are not automatically actionable for video generation: a Video Diffusion Transformer (DiT) may bypass them through native conditioning pathways, and using them does not ensure either factor-aware control or generation-compatible temporal prediction. We propose Factor-Aware Predictive Learning (FAPL), which establishes effective MLLM latent use as a shared prerequisite and then targets the two capabilities with complementary learning signals. First, latent-only generator grounding trains the DiT with a frozen MLLM and connector, strengthening the DiT's use of MLLM latents for generation. Second, for factor-aware control, paired videos specify a target-factor change and non-target preservation, and factor-span replacement trains the DiT to realize this relationship in its output. Third, for temporal inference, text-only and text-plus-complete-video observations share a video target, and the trained DiT is then frozen while the MLLM and connector are optimized through it. This final stage trains limited-input representations for generation while retaining complete-observation reconstruction as a complementary training mode. Extensive experiments demonstrate our consistently better performance on three cinematic video generation tasks (i.e., sparse-to-video, factor-aware editing, and multi-shot generation) over baselines and validate FAPL's contributions to MLLM feature use, temporal control, and factor coordination.

</details>


### [48] [ICGF-CXRMate: Image-Conditioned Clinical Context Compression and Gated Fusion for Chest X-ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30928)
*Vu Minh Tran,Thuc Huynh,Son Thanh Luu,Doanh C. Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ICGF-CXRMate 提出了一种用于提高胸部X光报告生成质量的图像条件化临床背景压缩和门控融合框架。通过使用可学习的问题查询从病患数据序列中压缩信息，并通过门控交叉注意力将这些信息选择性地加入视觉流中。与 CXRMate-ED 相比，ICGF-CXRMate 在多个报告质量指标上提高了性能，并减少了平均提示长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将异构的临床信息与视觉标记连接，导致模型接触到冗余或与图像无关的上下文，影响报告质量。因此，需要一种新的方法来改善这一点。

Method: ICGF-CXRMate 通过以下步骤工作：首先，学习问题查询根据当前的胸部X光表示进行条件化；接着，它对病患数据序列进行注意力机制，从中压缩出固定数量的临床上下文标记；然后，通过门控交叉注意力选择性地将压缩标记整合进视觉流；最后，压缩标记被保留到解码提示中，作为直接的临床路径。

Result: ICGF-CXRMate 在 MIMIC-CXR 和 MIMIC-IV-ED 数据集上的实验证明了报告质量的提高，并缩短了平均提示长度。与其他版本相比，它在特定报告质量指标上表现更优，并成功保留了压缩临床标记的直接价值。

Conclusion: ICGF-CXRMate 通过有效的图像条件化和门控融合策略有效地改进了胸部X光报告的生成，同时减少了对模型可能无用的信息依赖。

Abstract: Auxiliary patient data can improve chest X-ray report generation, but directly concatenating heterogeneous clinical information with visual tokens produces long, variable-length decoder prompts and may expose the model to redundant or image-irrelevant context. We propose ICGF-CXRMate, an image-conditioned clinical context compression and gated fusion framework. Learnable queries are first conditioned on the current chest X-ray representations and then attend to the patient-data sequence, compressing it into a fixed number of clinical context tokens. Gated cross-attention selectively integrates these tokens into the visual stream, while the compressed tokens are retained in the decoder prompt as a direct clinical pathway. Experiments on linked MIMIC-CXR and MIMIC-IV-ED examinations show that ICGF-CXRMate improves report quality while reducing the average prompt length. In Stage~2, it improves RadGraph-F1 from 24.00 to 28.86, CXR-BERT similarity from 56.86 to 62.80, and BLEU-4 from 5.14 to 7.96 compared with CXRMate-ED, while reducing the average prompt length from 373.89 to 288.37 tokens, corresponding to a 22.9\% reduction. After self-critical sequence training, ICGF-CXRMate outperforms CXRMate-ED on six of eight report-quality metrics in Stage~3. Ablation studies confirm the complementary contributions of image-conditioned compression, gated fusion, and direct retention of the compressed clinical tokens.

</details>


### [49] [Attention-guided classification and tumor localization of combined hepatocellular-cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30944)
*Xiaowen Liang,Hélène Cazier,Aurélie Beaufrère,Miguel Albuquerque,Valérie Paradis,Cédric Wemmert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用肝细胞癌（HCC）和胆管细胞癌（CCA）的整个切片图像（WSI），使用Hematoxylin-Eosin-Safran（HES）染色训练分类模型，旨在区分这些亚型并为在联合肝癌图像中定位不同类型的肿瘤提供潜在解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于cHCC-CCA的临床行为难以预测且无法与HCC或CCA的临床行为区别，对其进行精确诊断和表型特征识别面临挑战。因此，本文旨在通过训练分类模型帮助准确区分不同类型的肝肿瘤。

Method: 作者使用染色的WSIs训练分类模型。模型通过分析WSIs中的特征来区分HCC、CCA和cHCC-CCA。

Result: 尽管具体结果未在摘要中明确提及，但该研究提供了一种用于精确诊断及识别cHCC-CCA的潜在方法。

Conclusion: 通过提高不同类型肝癌的形态学表征与识别能力，研究提出了一个有潜力改善cHCC-CCA诊断的方法。

Abstract: Combined hepatocellular-cholangiocarcinomas (cHCC-CCA) are a type of cancer that share components of two other primary liver cancers: hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and cholangiocarcinoma (CCA). Accurate diagnosis of cHCC-CCA, of which the behavior may resemble that of either HCC or CCA, remains a challenge due to strong intratumoral heterogeneity. Since the prognoses differ between liver cancer types, it is necessary to improve their morphological characterization and recognition in order to accurately identify cHCC-CCA. In this work, we use whole slide images (WSIs) of the two main types of liver cancer (HCC and CCA) and cHCC-CCA, stained with Hematoxylin-Eosin-Safran (HES), to train a classification model that can distinguish between these subtypes and provides a potential solution for localizing different types of tumors in the combined liver cancer images.

</details>


### [50] [GraphCAD: Geometry-Aware Diffusion on Edge-Adjacency Graphs for CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30974)
*Wenhao Zheng,Xianggen Liu,Li Yue,Jiancheng Shi,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GraphCAD的几何感知生成框架，重点在于Edge-Adjacency Graph (EAG)，并采用了混合图扩散公式联合建模离散和连续成分。作者还构建了EAGBench数据集，以此展示了GraphCAD在几何保真度和分布匹配方面的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型在处理计算机辅助设计（CAD）中的拓扑连接和空间几何关系时存在局限性，而GraphCAD通过引入一种基于Edge-Adjacency Graph (EAG)的新颖图形表示方法，旨在解决这一问题，从而提高生成CAD模型的质量。

Method: GraphCAD采用混合图扩散公式联合建模EAG的离散和连续成分，并引入了一种几何感知的去噪机制，将节点属性转换为空间点集，通过SE(3)-等变基础进行编码，并将其与节点表示融合，从而将生成过程与物理几何相结合。此外，作者还开发了一个大型工程CAD模型数据集EAGBench，以支持该研究。

Result: 实验结果表明，GraphCAD能够生成具有超过50个面的复杂CAD模型，并在几何保真度和分布匹配方面优于基于序列的基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种创新的生成框架GraphCAD，通过有效的图形建模和数据集构建，显著提高了CAD模型生成的质量和技术水平。

Abstract: Generative modeling for Computer-Aided Design (CAD) remains fundamentally challenged by the need to capture the intricate interplay between discrete topological connectivity and precise spatial geometry inherent in Boundary Representations (B-reps). Existing generative approaches largely formulate CAD construction as a sequence modeling problem, but consequently lack an explicit topological inductive bias. In this work, we present GraphCAD, a geometry-aware generative framework that centers modeling on the Edge-Adjacency Graph (EAG), a novel graph-based CAD representation tailored for generative tasks. GraphCAD adopts a hybrid graph diffusion formulation to jointly model the discrete and continuous components of EAG and introduces a geometry-aware denoising mechanism that renders node attributes into spatial point sets. These point sets are encoded by an SE(3)-equivariant backbone and fused into the node representations, thereby grounding the generative process in physical geometry. To support this study, we construct EAGBench, a large-scale dataset of engineering CAD models. Extensive experiments show that GraphCAD can generate complex CAD models with more than 50 faces and achieves state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and distributional alignment compared with sequence-based baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Diagnostic Loss: Asymmetric Supervision for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30980)
*Ignacy Kolton,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Szymon Płotka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Diagnostic Loss (DLoss)的方法，用于在医学图像语言模型中提高诊断准确性。DLoss直接作用于解码器token概率，鼓励支持诊断的概率集中在至少一个位置，同时抑制不支持诊断的概率在所有位置的意外升高。这种改进在保持常规语言生成性能的同时提高了诊断的准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学视觉-语言模型在训练中倾向于优先考虑文本一致性而非诊断性关键词，这可能导致模型遗漏关键诊断或引入错误诊断。

Method: 该研究引入了一种名为Diagnostic Loss (DLoss)的可微、架构无关的目标函数。DLoss通过不对称聚合与参考报告支持的诊断和不支持的替代诊断相关的概率来工作。它在报告的token位置上进行不对称聚合，以鼓励至少在一个token位置上支持的诊断获得足够的概率，同时在所有token位置上抑制不支持的诊断的意外概率激增。

Result: DLoss在多个3D医学视觉-语言模型架构中得到了培训和评估。在保持常规语言生成性能的同时，DLoss提升了诊断准确性和一致性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了对放射报告生成进行不对称序列级监督的价值，这种方法可以在不修改现有模型的视觉骨干、语言解码器或推理流程的情况下提升诊断的准确性。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation requires models to do more than produce fluent clinical narratives: they must explicitly state supported diagnoses while avoiding unsupported ones. However, existing medical vision-language models (MedVLMs) are typically trained with token-level maximum likelihood objectives, which prioritize text agreement over diagnostically critical terms. As a result, models can miss key diagnoses and introduce wrong ones. We identify this as an objective misalignment between conventional language modeling and the asymmetric diagnostic requirements of radiology reporting. To encode these requirements, we introduce Diagnostic Loss (DLoss), a differentiable, architecture-agnostic objective that operates directly on decoder token probabilities. DLoss separately aggregates probability mass over vocabularies associated with diagnoses supported by the reference report and with unsupported alternatives. Using asymmetric pooling across token positions in the report, it encourages supported diagnoses to receive sufficient probability in at least one position while suppressing spurious probability spikes for unsupported diagnoses across all positions during training. The loss can be applied to existing 3D MedVLMs without modifying their vision backbones, language decoders, or inference procedures, and introduces no additional inference-time computation. We train and evaluate DLoss on 50,000 abdominal CT scans across several 3D MedVLM architectures on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution report-generation benchmarks. Across these settings, DLoss improves diagnostic faithfulness while maintaining competitive performance on conventional language-generation metrics, showing the value of asymmetric sequence-level supervision for radiology report generation. The code for DLoss is available at https://github.com/gmum/DLoss.

</details>


### [52] [ResGVC: Residual Coding for High-fidelity Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31039)
*Ding Ding,Daowen Li,Ying Chen,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResGVC 提出了一种残差编码框架，通过传递残差信号并直接在像素空间进行去噪，从而减少解码器侧的生成过程，实现更好的视觉质量和与前最先进的方法相比，分别在 LPIPS 和 PSNR 上获得了 63.7% 和 83.8% 的 BD 率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GVC 方法依赖解码器侧的扩散模型进行重建信号的降噪，而 ResGVC 提出了通过编码原始图像、分解误差段并引入残差编码器等创新方法，旨在减少信息损失并提高去噪效果。

Method: ResGVC 的方法首先通过学习视频编解码器对原始图像进行编码，然后将编解码器重建与原始图像之间的失真分解成残差段，每个残差段在去噪步骤中获得。残差段进一步通过包含扩散变换器作为熵模型的新颖残差编解码器进行压缩，引入了在整个去噪过程中的显式源约束，避免了潜变量瓶颈导致的不可逆信息损失。

Result: 实验结果显示，与最新的 SOTA 方法相比，ResGVC 在 LPIPS 和 PSNR 评分上分别实现了 63.7% 和 83.8% 的 BD 率降低，同时提供了更高质量的视觉效果。

Conclusion: ResGVC 通过直接在像素空间进行去噪、引入显式的源约束以及基于扩散变换器的熵模型，改进了视频压缩的效果。

Abstract: Generative video compression (GVC) methods usually employ decoder-side diffusion models to denoise reconstructed signals based on generative priors. Besides, most of these methods perform denoising in latent space rather than directly in pixel domain. In this paper, we propose ResGVC, a residual coding framework for GVC that guides denoising toward original pictures by transmitting residual signals. ResGVC first encodes the original pictures within a learned video codec, and then decomposes the distortion between the codec reconstruction and the original pictures into a sequence of residual segments, where each residual segment is acquired at each denoising step. Residual segments can further be compressed by a novel residual codec with a diffusion transformer as an entropy model. Unlike prior methods, ResGVC introduces explicit source constraints throughout denoising rather than relying on decoder-side generation alone. The diffusion-based entropy model also enables more effective distribution modeling capability based on the powerful generative priors. Moreover, denoising directly in pixel space avoids the irreversible information loss caused by latent representation bottlenecks. Experimental results show that, compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, ResGVC achieves 63.7% and 83.8% BD-rate reductions in terms of LPIPS and PSNR, respectively, while also delivering superior visual quality.

</details>


### [53] [A General-Purpose Dual-Path Interaction Framework for Multi-View Medical Image Segmentation: Alleviating Data Volume Dependency in Small-Sample Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31065)
*Shuhuan Wang,Yuanyuan Cui,Pengju Wang,Huimin Guo,Ling Yue,Yin Gu,Yanchao Zhang,Wei Feng,Yunhe Liu,Wei Qian,Mingjun Xu,Shuang Liu,He Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DPIF 提出了一种适用于任意编码解码分割网络的通用双路径交互框架，通过跨视图解剖互补性减少注释依赖，提高在小样本情况下多视图医学图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半监督和弱监督方法依赖特定的网络架构，而传统的多视图辅助分割方法需要严格配对样本，这限制了它们在临床应用中的使用。为此，提出了 DPIF，以解决这些问题。

Method: DPIF 通过 Sinkhorn 最优传输实现视图间分布对齐，建立全局语义对应，无需样本级配对，并使用条件互信息指导特征解耦，在共享语义空间中实现双向知识转移，抑制视图特定噪声。最后，使用持久同调拓扑正则化编码解剖不变量作为未监督先验，减轻过拟合。

Result: 实验表明，使用 DPIF 改进的模型在与单一视图基线模型相当的数据量下性能更优。大部分双路径变体在 40%-60% 的数据下达到与单一视图模型在完整数据集下相似的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种通用的大规模多视图医学图像分割解决方案，为数据稀缺场景提供了有效的增强方法。

Abstract: The performance of medical image segmentation depends on a large amount of annotated data, and most segmentation models experience a significant drop in performance in low-sample-size scenarios. Existing semi-supervised and weakly supervised methods rely on specific network architectures, while traditional multi-view-assisted segmentation methods require strictly paired samples, which limits their clinical applications. To address these issues, we propose DPIF, a general-purpose plug-and-play dual-path interaction framework compatible with arbitrary encoder-decoder segmentation networks, which leverages cross-view anatomical complementarity to reduce annotation reliance. DPIF first performs Sinkhorn optimal transport-based cross-view distribution alignment to establish global semantic correspondence without sample-level pairing. It then adopts conditional mutual information-guided feature decoupling to enable bidirectional knowledge transfer only within the shared semantic space, suppressing view-specific noise. Finally, persistent homology-based topological regularization encodes anatomical invariants as unsupervised priors to mitigate overfitting. Experiments were conducted using 24 baseline models on a private multicenter echocardiography dataset and two public datasets. The results show that DPIF-enhanced models outperform single-view baseline models when trained on comparable amounts of data. Notably, most dual-path variants trained on 40\% - 60\% of the data achieve performance comparable to that of single-view models trained on the full dataset. This demonstrates that DPIF provides a general-purpose and scalable solution for data-scarce multi-view medical image segmentation problems.

</details>


### [54] [I$^2$-KAN: Interpretable Intra- and Inter-Modal Multi-Pathway Representation Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31087)
*Xiaoting Xie,Feng Qiao,Xianwei Zhang,Hailong Chen,Kaiwen Zheng,Joemon M. Jose,Xuri Ge,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种称为I²-KAN的新框架，该框架明确了模态内和模态间信息，并通过任务解耦线性门控去噪瓶颈和结构化的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德网络融合，增强了可解释性，实验显示其在多个数据集上表现优异，验证了孤立模态和交互模态信息的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的融合方法难以区分预测效果是单一模态本身所带来的，还是多个模态交互的成果。为了解决这一问题，研究提出了一个新框架来支持模态内和模态间的信息保持，并支持任务相关的表示学习。

Method: 提出了一个解释性的多路径框架I²-KAN，分别处理模态内和模态间信息。每个路径通过任务解耦线性门控去噪瓶颈（TDL-GDB）进行优化，然后通过结构化的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德网络融合（KAN）来保持路径关联，支持多级解释。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI和CH-SIMS v2数据集上的广泛实验表明，I²-KAN框架具有很强的性能，并且消融分析和解释性分析显示，孤立的模态不一定具有低预测效用，通过与其他模态的交互，模态的预测效用可以得到保留。

Conclusion: I²-KAN框架强调了模态嵌入交互效用的重要性，其性能和解释能力证明了多模态信息交互依赖性的关键特征，提示在评估多模态效能时不仅要考虑单一模态的效用，还要考虑其相互作用下的效果。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to infer sentiment by integrating linguistic, acoustic, and visual information. However, many fusion methods integrate modality-specific representations and cross-modal interactions into joint representations, making it difficult to determine whether predictive utility arises from a modality itself or from its interactions with others. More fundamentally, a modality's standalone predictive utility may not faithfully reflect its interaction-conditioned predictive utility. To address this issue, we propose I$^2$-KAN, an interpretable multi-pathway framework that explicitly maintains intra-modal and pairwise inter-modal information in separate structural pathways. Each pathway is refined by a Task-Decoupled Linear-Gated Denoising Bottleneck (TDL-GDB) to learn compact and task-relevant representations, followed by structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network fusion that retains explicit pathway associations and supports multi-level interpretation. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS v2 demonstrate strong overall performance. Ablation and interpretability analyses consistently show across the three benchmarks that low standalone predictive utility does not necessarily imply low interaction-conditioned predictive utility: a modality that appears weak in isolation can remain informative through pairwise interactions. These findings highlight the interaction-dependent nature of multimodal utility and suggest that modality utility should be characterized from both standalone and interaction-conditioned perspectives.

</details>


### [55] [DRAFT: Decoding and Reconstructing 3D Room Layouts from fMRI and Camera Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31108)
*Chi-Wei Lee,An-Kai Chen,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究首次使用仅通过参与者观看室内走动的已知摄像机轨迹和fMRI信号，而无需任何视觉帧，重建隐藏场景的3D布局，尽管存在视觉变化快速与fMRI信号模糊之间的时间不匹配问题。研究开发了一种名为DRAFT的方法来融合视觉皮层早期信息和摄像机动画几何自由空间，并提出布局r评估指标来衡量预测深度与射线深度之间的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能磁共振成像（fMRI）解码器能够从活动中重建图像和3D对象，但由于视角的快速变化与fMRI图像模糊之间的同步挑战，对于从移动视角恢复房间的度量几何布局仍面临困难。本文旨在解决这个开放性挑战，尝试仅通过fMRI信号和摄像机轨迹恢复场景的3D布局。

Method: DRAFT方法结合了视觉皮层早期信息和摄像机动画几何自自由空间数据，通过融合这些数据来重建隐藏的3D房间布局。

Result: 当使用DRAFT方法时，预测的房间布局与射线投射深度之间的Pearson相关性达到了0.480，尤其是在与七个fMRI解码器相比，表现出更优的性能。此外，DRAFT方法亦作为一个插件，提升7个解码器的布局Pearson相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究证实，大脑活动和摄像机运动可以恢复看不见房间的度量布局，这是向重建人们所见的3D空间迈出的第一步。

Abstract: While functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) decoders reconstruct images and 3D objects, recovering the metric geometry of rooms from a moving viewpoint remains an open challenge. We introduce, to our knowledge for the first time, the task of reconstructing 3D layouts of held-out scenes using only the known camera trajectories and the fMRI signals of participants watching indoor walkthroughs, without any visual frames. The task is hard because of the temporal mismatch between rapid visual changes and blurred fMRI signals. Our method, DRAFT (Decoding and Reconstructing 3D room lAyouts from fMRI and Trajectories), extracts a compact layout representation from the early visual cortex and fuses this neural code with the geometric free space derived from camera motion. To evaluate it, we propose layout r, the Pearson correlation between predicted and ray-cast log depths. On ten held-out scenes (20 participants), DRAFT reaches a layout r of 0.480, outperforming the camera path alone (0.446) and seven fMRI decoders (at most 0.429). DRAFT also works as a plug-in that improves all seven decoders without retraining, raising their layout r from 0.171-0.429 to 0.487-0.493. Brain activity and camera motion can thus recover the metric layout of unseen rooms, a first step toward reconstructing the 3D spaces people see.

</details>


### [56] [Where Do Long Captions Fail? Position-Aware Diagnosis and Reinforcement Learning for Detailed Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31111)
*Yuanze Hu,Zhichao Yang,Junwei Jing,Xin Yang,Wenxuan Liu,Tinghai Zhang,Jia Fu,Hongzhi Zhang,Xiangyu Wu,Ruiming Tang,Tingting Gao,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的评估框架PAGER，通过位置感知的方式识别并优化图像描述中的事实出现位置，从而提高生成描述的准确性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉语言模型在生成长描述时存在中间部分事实缺乏支持，尾部部分又变得过于模糊的两个问题。现有方法主要侧重于事实内容本身，而忽视了这些内容出现的位置。

Method: 研究者提出了一种新的位置感知评估框架PAGER，它将密集的人工参考信息分解成原子视觉声称，验证生成的描述是否符合这些声称，并根据支持、虚假幻觉和通用内容四分位数进行合成。同时，还提出了一种GRPO训练目标，带有针对支持的具体中段证据、承载证据的尾部继续、声称覆盖率和内容感知长度控制的独立信号。

Result: 实验结果显示，PAGER在位置上的视觉接地和成对描述偏好方面表现更好，同时保持了竞争力的整体描述质量。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新的方法来改进视觉语言模型生成的长描述的质量，并证明其有效性。

Abstract: Detailed image captioning is usually framed as an amount problem: a model should mention more visual facts while avoiding hallucination. However, autoregressive VLMs generate captions as ordered sequences, and later generations may be produced under weaker or noisier visual grounding. We therefore argue that long-form captioning should be studied not only by what facts are mentioned, but also by where they appear. We introduce a position-aware rubric representation that decomposes dense human references into atomic visual claims, verifies generated captions against those claims, and induces a quartile-wise composition of supported, hallucinated, and generic content. This lens reveals two qualitatively different failures in long captions: models tend to produce unsupported specificity in the middle, while the final portion often escapes into vague, non-evidential language rather than simply accumulating more hallucination. A same-reference human control does not exhibit these patterns; additional human audits examine the visual validity of the induced labels. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose PAGER (Position-Aware Grounded Evidence Reinforcement), a GRPO training objective with separate signals for supported fine-grained middle evidence, evidence-bearing tail continuation, claim coverage, and content-aware length control. The result is a closed loop: the same ordered claim representation identifies where long captions fail and supplies the position-specific reward needed to repair those failures. Experiments on detailed-caption benchmarks show that PAGER improves position-wise grounding and pairwise caption preference while preserving competitive global caption quality.

</details>


### [57] [Through a PRISM, Clearly: Step-Sequence Reasoning for Mistake Recognition in Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31117)
*Olga Loginova,Oleksandr Bezrukov,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PRISM的方法，用于从视频中识别程序性错误。该方法将视频段落与语言对接，并使用轻量级的隐马尔可夫模型将其描述映射到程序状态，通过参考对齐和语义检查识别步骤内容和顺序的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在通过自动化从视频中识别程序性错误，提高工业生产和产品工艺流程的准确性和效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于语言对接和隐马尔可夫模型的方法来识别视频中的程序性错误。该方法首先对齐视频片段并进行语义检查，然后使用轻量级的隐马尔可夫模型将描述映射到程序状态。此外，引入了四种编辑错误类型：插入、删除、替换和转换。通过不同的描述形式（原始描述、语义角色和行为对象）和不同的解码策略，评估了模型性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用轻量级隐马尔可夫模型进行编码控制可以在所有六个匹配的接地对比中提高Any-F1得分，尽管收益在不同数据集上的变化较大。语义角色标记在所有六个匹配的接地对比中均提高了Any-F1得分，但在单独的配置选择中并未实现统一的提高。PRISM结合了语言对接和解码策略，展示了有效的程序性错误推理接口。

Conclusion: 本文展示了语言对接作为程序性错误推理的有效接口，并对语义角色和行为对象表示及解码策略的作用进行了特征分析。

Abstract: Procedural mistake recognition requires identifying deviations from valid multi-step activities. We study post-completion recognition from videos with benchmark-provided step boundaries. We introduce PRISM (Procedural mistake Recognition via Interpretable Semantic Modeling), which grounds video segments in language and maps their descriptions to procedural states using a lightweight hidden Markov model trained on correct executions. Reference alignment and semantic checks identify errors in step content and order. To support evaluation across heterogeneous benchmarks, we map dataset-specific annotations to four edit-based error types: insertion, deletion, substitution, and transposition. Across three benchmarks, we compare gold and VLM-generated descriptions in raw, semantic-role, and action-object forms. Decoder controls show consistent gains from the trained local decoder over nearest-candidate matching, with additional, dataset-dependent benefits from sequence decoding. Semantic role labeling improves Any-F1 in all six matched grounded comparisons, although the gains vary across datasets and do not translate into uniform improvements under separate configuration selection. These results establish language grounding as an effective interface for explicit procedural mistake reasoning and characterize the contributions of representation and decoding.

</details>


### [58] [WildDepth-v2: A Synchronized RGB--LiDAR--Radar Dataset and Benchmark for Metric Depth, Motion, and Tracking of Wildlife](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31123)
*Muhammad Aamir,Sangyun Shin,Naoya Muramatsu,Matthew Wijers,Amir Patel,Andrew Loveridge,Andrew Markham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WildDepth 2数据集，包含了37条RGB-LiDAR-4D雷达序列，提供每点Doppler速度和误差预算，首次实现了用于野生动物记录的成像雷达影像。该数据集通过隐藏测试集和不同类型的运动追踪，对动物和背景像素分别进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在的基于单目视觉的方法缺乏元数据和野生动物数据集的元数据。因此，需要开发一种能够提供准确深度信息的数据集来支持动物距离和运动的度量单位下的评估。

Method: 通过将RGB与LiDAR和4D雷达数据结合，创建WildDepth 2数据集。引入了37条序列，每条序列包括19,716帧，提供每点Doppler速度和误差预算。同时，设计了一个隐藏测试集用于距离估计和稀疏LiDAR深度完成的评估，并通过雷达校准和融合不同方式促进动物和背景像素的三维轨迹的计算。

Result: 使用WildDepth 2数据集，实现了：1. 相比逐场景LiDAR校准，对象级雷达校准提高了单目深度的精度（从0.198降低到0.120）；2. 通过每次轨迹一次的雷达缩放，3D位置误差减少了四倍，同时减少了距离抽样检测函数偏差；3. 融合雷达多普勒速度到LiDAR，可提升动动物LiDAR累积误差43%，并且估算径向速度，尽管略有低估。

Conclusion: WildDepth 2在一定程度上解决了单目视频缺乏度量尺度和野生动物数据集缺乏元数据的问题，增强了动物距离和运动的测量能力，具有较高的准确度和效率，有助于相关研究的发展。

Abstract: Measuring animal distance and motion in metric units underpins abundance estimation, morphometrics, and movement ecology, yet monocular video lacks metric scale and wildlife datasets lack metric ground truth. We present WildDepth 2, extending the synchronized RGB-LiDAR WildDepth dataset in three directions. (i) Imaging radar: 37 RGB-LiDAR-4D-radar sequences (19,716 frames) with per-point Doppler velocity and a measured error budget, to our knowledge the first imaging-radar wildlife recordings. (ii) Benchmarking: a frozen, checksummed hidden test set for RGB-only distance estimation and RGB+sparse-LiDAR depth completion, evaluated separately on animal and background pixels. (iii) Motion and tracking: metric 3D trajectories obtained by lifting 2D tracks using LiDAR, radar, or monocular depth. Across 48,723 animal frames, object-level radar calibration improves monocular depth over scene-level LiDAR calibration without requiring LiDAR at inference (AbsRel 0.120 vs. 0.198), although animal-level LiDAR remains more accurate (0.052). Across 431 tracks, once-per-track radar scaling reduces 3D position error four-fold (2.20 to 0.58 m) and distance-sampling detection-function bias from -13% to -2%, matching once-per-track LiDAR calibration. Transferring radar Doppler onto LiDAR produces motion masks 3.7 times denser than radar alone at 72% precision, reduces moving-animal LiDAR accumulation error by 37%, and estimates radial speed (r = 0.60), albeit with approximately 50% underestimation.

</details>


### [59] [Pos4CHD: A Positive-Free Mask-Guided Counterfactual Synthesis Framework for Fetal Congenital Heart Defect Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31139)
*Wenjie Xuan,Yapeng Li,Hang Su,Chen Cheng,Juhua Liu,Sheng Zhao,Ziming Huang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个无阳性样本的胎儿心脏缺陷生成框架Pos4CHD，通过使用语义掩模和生成模型合成高保真伪阳性样本，提升了胎儿CHDs诊断任务中的零样本分类、病灶检测和解剖学分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于罕见的胎儿先天性心脏病（CHDs）病例和隐私问题导致阳性病例难以收集，现有的带有正样本的手动合成方法难以广泛应用于CHDs的诊断系统开发。

Method: 研究开发了FE-Gen，一种针对胎儿心脏超声的语义掩模引导生成模型，它减轻了直方图漂移和提升了图像-掩模空间对齐。基于FE-Gen，Pos4CHD采用了掩模到图像生成范式进行CHDs合成。该方法首先通过操纵语义掩模模拟胎儿心脏结构异常，然后渲染出解剖上一致的图像。该框架不仅在虚构样本上表现良好，还在零样本分类和病灶检测任务上实现了性能提升。

Result: 该框架在自建数据集上进行了广泛实验，经专家评估，合成图像具有较高视觉保真度（-12.97 FID）和解剖一致性（+3.96% DSC）。并在多种CHDs诊断任务上展示了显著改进，包括零样本分类、病灶检测、解剖学分割等。

Conclusion: Pos4CHD框架通过仅使用正常样本即可生成高质量的虚假阳性样本，为CHDs的诊断和研究提供了新的解决方案，特别是在数据稀缺的情况下。

Abstract: The scarcity of positive cases remains a major bottleneck in developing automated diagnostic systems for fetal Congenital Heart Defects~(CHDs), particularly for rare subtypes. Although normal cases are accessible with effort, positive cases are hard to collect due to low incidence rates and privacy concerns, causing severe class imbalance or absence of positive training samples. Existing works resort to synthesizing positives with conditional generative models, but they are task-specific and require positive training samples, hindering application for fetal CHDs. To this end, we propose a positive-free mask-guided counterfactual generation framework for fetal echocardiography, namely Pos4CHD, which enables realistic, high-quality synthesis of pseudo-positive samples from semantic masks, with only normal samples at hand. Technically, we first develop FE-Gen, a mask-guided generative model tailored for fetal heart ultrasound synthesis that mitigates histogram drift and enhances image-mask spatial alignment. Building on FE-Gen, Pos4CHD employs a mask-to-image generation paradigm for CHD synthesis. It first manipulates semantic masks to simulate fetal-heart structural anomalies and then renders anatomically consistent images. Extensive experiments on in-house datasets, together with expert evaluations, demonstrate the visual fidelity~($-12.97$ FID) and anatomical consistency~($+3.96\%$ DSC) of synthesized images. Further validations across diverse real-world CHD diagnosis tasks exhibit promising results and consistent increments, including zero-shot classification~(achieving $93.25\%$ ACC on EbA and $72.92\%$ ACC on TAPVC), lesion detection~($+6.95\%$ mAP50 on VSD), and anatomy segmentation~($+10.49\%$ DSC on fetal heart) compared with baselines. Codes and models are publicly available on \href{https://github.com/DREAMXFAR/Pos4CHD}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [60] [GM-GReFEL: Geometry-Guided Spatiotemporal State-Space Learning for Robust Dynamic Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31140)
*Yudhistira Arditya Pratama,Yi-Zeng Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GM-GReFEL 提出了一种基于空间-时间状态空间网络的方法，利用几何感知决策阶段解决无约束视频中的面部表情识别问题，相较于其他方法，在 DFEW 和 MAFW 数据集上表现竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面部表情识别技术在无约束视频中的应用受限于自注意力成本随片段长度呈二次增长以及人为标注数据中存在的噪声。因此提出了 GM-GReFEL 方法来解决这些问题。

Method: GM-GReFEL 采用 Modulated GroupMamba 骨干网络，并通过 Channel Affinity Modulation 门控机制增加组之间的混合。在模型顶部引入 GReFEL 可靠性模块，根据预测的不确定性调整分类器输出和可训练几何锚点投票之间的权重。模型通过静态-动态权重转移进行初始化，并使用 Triplet Loss 进行训练。

Result: 在五折交叉验证中，GM-GReFEL 在 DFEW 数据集上的 UAR/WAR 分别达到 66.57%/76.46%，在 MAFW 数据集上达到 45.66%/62.27%，优于其他方法；在 FERV39k 数据集上的性能具有竞争力，平均为 42.30%/52.66%。

Conclusion: GM-GReFEL 经验证实了其在面部表情识别任务中的有效性，特别是在处理无约束视频中的应用。此外，其在 DFEW 数据集上接近近期大规模预训练模型的表现，但在 MAFW 数据集上略逊一筹。

Abstract: Recognizing facial expressions in unconstrained video suffers from two coupled problems: self-attention grows quadratically in cost with clip length, and crowdsourced labels are noisy. We present GM-GReFEL, a spatiotemporal state-space network with a geometry-aware decision stage. Its Modulated GroupMamba backbone replaces a spatiotemporal Vision Transformer. Six channel groups are scanned separately, four over the frame plane and two over time, so the cost grows linearly with sequence length ($\mathcal{O}(N)$); after each layer, a Channel Affinity Modulation gate mixes the groups again. On top of the backbone, a GReFEL reliability module turns the normalized entropy of each prediction into a mixing weight between the classifier output and a vote from trainable geometric anchors, so that facial geometry counts for more when the network is unsure. The model is initialized by static-to-dynamic weight transfer and trained with a Triple Loss. In five-fold cross-validation, the best fold scores 66.57\%/76.46\% UAR/WAR on DFEW, 0.3 points or less below the strongest compared method, and 45.66\%/62.27\% on MAFW, ahead of all compared methods; on FERV39k the mean over five splits, 42.30\%/52.66\%, is competitive. The five-fold means are 64.07\%/74.54\% on DFEW, on par with recent large pre-trained models, and 38.90\%/54.37\% on MAFW, below the best of them. A paired $t$-test on DFEW indicates a modest benefit of temporal modeling (+1.61 UAR points, one-sided $p=0.031$). Attention stays on plausible facial regions, and latency is close to a ViT-B/16 baseline (47.4 vs.\ 46.8 ms per clip). Code: https://github.com/ardiytama/GroupMamba-DFER.

</details>


### [61] [RankAlign: Vision-Language Representation Alignment via Rank Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31145)
*Enzhe Zhao,Marco Fiorucci,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RankAlign的秩基归一化策略，用于解决无配对的视觉-语言匹配中的类别级对应关系恢复问题，该方法能够提升且更加稳定地恢复类别级对应关系，即使在标签噪声和领域迁移的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨模态关系匹配方法直接比较同模态的相似性核，但不同模态中相同的语义结构可能会通过不同的数值参数化表示。现有方法面临挑战，特别是如何在未配对的情况下有效恢复类别级别的跨模态对应关系。

Method: RankAlign通过秩基归一化策略，将每个同模态相似性核转换为序数关系表示，然后进行排列匹配，同时保持对应空间和匹配求解器不变。该方法保证了相似值严格递增重新参数化下的不变性，并保留用于匹配的序数结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，RankAlign方法能够提高且更加稳定地恢复类别级别的跨模态对应关系，即使在存在标签噪声和领域迁移的情况下。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种有效的方法来解决无配对条件下的视觉-语言跨模态对应关系恢复问题，并在多种视觉和语言编码器与数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: While the Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that vision and language models converge toward compatible latent geometries, recovering class-level correspondences between unpaired modalities without cross-modal supervision remains challenging. Existing relational matching methods compare within-modality similarity kernels directly, although the same semantic structure may be represented through different numerical parameterizations across modalities. We propose RankAlign, a rank-based normalization strategy for class-level unpaired vision-language matching. RankAlign transforms each within-modality similarity kernel into an ordinal relational representation before permutation matching, while leaving the correspondence space and matching solver unchanged. The resulting representation is invariant to strictly increasing reparameterizations of similarity values while preserving the ordinal structure used for matching. We provide exact-recovery and stability guarantees that characterize correspondence recovery under both exact and partial ordinal agreement. Experiments across diverse frozen vision and language encoders and multiple datasets demonstrate improved and more stable class-level correspondence recovery, including under label corruption and domain shift.

</details>


### [62] [WildFakeToolBench: Benchmarking AI-Generated Image Detectors with Web-Sourced Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31152)
*Mingjie Li,Yukun Jiang,Yicong Tan,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究介绍了一个包含156个图像处理工具的基准套件，这些工具源自220个公开来源，共142个领域，旨在评估AI生成图像的潜在风险。发现某些工具能够绕过最先进的检测器，显示了需要依据这类野外信息建立更有效的AI图像检测器评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成图像的流行，恶意行为者可能利用这些生成的工具来规避检测器，无需专门知识或本地GPU执行。因此，为了评估这种风险，研究者开发了一个基准套件。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含156个工具的基准套件，每种工具都保留了其源证据并通过语法、执行和输出失真检查。在105个生成图像上，研究者评估了六个最先进的检测器，测试它们的防护能力。

Result: 研究发现，某些工具能够实现25.25%-100%的攻击成功率，这超过了六种选定的图像处理基线。这种有效性是稀疏和检测器特定的，即便覆盖候选工具，也不一定能成功绕过检测器。这对评估基于野外信息建立AI图像检测器的鲁棒性提供了支持。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，依赖于线上信息的AI生成图像的风险具有潜在威胁，需建立一种基于这些风险来评估AI图像检测器更有效的框架。

Abstract: As AI generated images are getting popular these days, discussions on such generations are widely existed in current public forums, software repositories, and photography tutorials. Moreover, with the help of LLM-assisted code translation, malicious actors can adopt such information to evade detectors without specialized knowledge or local GPU execution of the resulting tools. To evaluate such risks, we introduce \textsc{WildFakeToolBench}, a benchmark suite of $156$ executable image-processing tools derived from $220$ public sources across $142$ domains. Each tool retains its source evidence and passes syntax, execution, and output-distortion checks without using feedback from the evaluated detectors. On $105$ generated images from five generators, we evaluate six state-of-the-art detectors with images processed by the tools in our collected bench to assess their effectiveness against such in-the-wild risks. The strongest individual tools achieve attack success rates of $25.25\%$--$100\%$ across detectors, exceeding the strongest of six selected image-processing baselines for each detector. Evaluating subsets of an eight-tool pool yields worst-case coverage above $80\%$ for every detector. Effectiveness is sparse and detector-specific: high coverage across candidates does not imply reliable one-shot evasion. These findings not only highlight the potential of such web-sourced risks, but also show the potential on establishing AI-image detector robustness evaluation framework based on those in-the-wild information.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Watching and Searching: Benchmarking Agents for Video-centered Deep Research Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31158)
*Jiahao Meng,Qize Yang,Yuxuan Wang,Yunfei Chu,Wensi Dai,Jin Xu,Yunhai Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种以视频为中心的深度研究报告生成任务，通过给定的视频和用户请求，生成互动报告并链接到相关的视频片段。这个任务有助于用户定位相关信息、验证声明、复现展示的操作以及基于视频内容做决策。OmniDR-Bench 提供了一个评估框架和基准数据集，涵盖音频视频关联、多模态证据利用及交互式展示等多维度评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的研究主要集中在基于文本的内容上，缺乏针对视频内容的评估基准。因此，提出了以视频为中心的深度研究报告生成任务，希望通过评估模型提高用户从视频内容中获取信息和做决策的能力。

Method: 该方法首先定义了评估框架 Omni-ARTs，包括音频视频关联、多模态证据利用和交互式展示三个维度。然后构建了一个名为 OmniDR-Bench 的数据集，包含50个中英文任务和来自19个真实领域子类别的6,414项评估标准。通过18种不同配置的代理测试，评估了系统的性能。

Result: 研究结果显示，当前系统在多项评估标准上表现较好，但尚需提升内容的全面性、跨模态的因果和逻辑分析以及引用支持的准确性。OmniDR-Bench 和相关技能配置等资源已公布。

Conclusion: 研究成果展示了当前技术在以视频为中心的研究报告生成方面存在的局限性，并提出了一种新的评估框架和基准数据集，为未来的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Deep research agents synthesize open-web evidence into long reports, but current benchmarks do not evaluate research centered on a source video. We introduce Video-Centered Deep Research Report Generation. Given a video and user request, an agent identifies research questions, retrieves external multimodal evidence, and produces an interactive report linked to relevant video moments. This task has direct practical value because it helps users locate relevant moments, verify claims, reproduce demonstrated procedures, and make informed decisions from video content. We present a skill-based workflow and OmniDR-Bench for this task. For evaluation, we propose Omni Atomic Rubric Trees (Omni-ARTs). Each tree contains three dimensions, 23 shared directions, and task-specific atomic binary criteria. The directions test audiovisual grounding, multimodal evidence use, and interactive presentation. Audited Rubric Construction (ARC) builds these trees through independent evidence collection, automated checks, behavioral calibration, and iterative human review. OmniDR-Bench contains 50 Chinese and English tasks across 19 realworld subcategories. It includes 6,414 criteria. Across 18 agent configurations, the best system scores 63.3 out of 100. Current agents still need broader content coverage, stronger causal and logical analysis across modalities, and more accurate citation support. We further analyze models, harnesses, skill configurations, video categories, and research trajectories. The code and data are released at https://github.com/marinero4972/OmniDR-Bench.

</details>


### [64] [HiSTEVE 1.0: Harnessing Human-Interface VLA Agents for Open-World Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31162)
*Zecong Tang,Zian Guan,Yuncheng Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiSTEVE 是一种层次化的虚拟操作代理，结合了结构化记忆、VLM 计划器和任务完成状态检测器，能够在没有高阶规划的情况下自主执行复杂任务，如 Minecraft 中制作铁镐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Minecraft 等开放世界游戏中实现既是具有长期自主性的模拟角色，同时又能提供本体感官控制的目标，一直是一个挑战。现有的代理通常要么无法执行长期的任务，要么无法实现复杂的人机交互。

Method: HiSTEVE 采用了一种层次化的虚拟代理架构，其中包括一个用于任务规划的层次结构和一个根据任务执行情况动态调整的子任务操作机制。它不仅具备结构化记忆，还引入了 VLM 计划器来选择代理能力范围内的子任务，以及任务完成状态检测器来检测子任务的完成情况。该架构还支持了特定领域的归一化分支和独立的鼠标移动令牌嵌入以减少跨界干扰，并通过知识注入、示例学习和强化学习结合环境反馈来训练代理以提高其可靠性和恢复能力。

Result: HiSTEVE 在 MineStudio Bench 上实现了 85.0% 的原子任务性能，平均成功率达到了 37.4%，并能够像人类玩家一样从零开始制作铁镐等复杂任务。

Conclusion: HiSTEVE 是一种创新的虚拟代理，可以在 Minecraft 中自主操作和执行复杂的任务，同时也为其他开放世界的模拟研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Building virtual embodied agents that operate in open-ended games like human players is a long-standing goal requiring both long-horizon autonomy and reliable native-interface control. As a procedurally generated open world, Minecraft provides a natural testbed for studying the integration of these capabilities. Existing agents typically provide either native visuomotor control without long-horizon autonomy or high-level planning executed through environment APIs or predefined skills. To bridge this gap, we propose HiSTEVE, a hierarchical VLA agent with a capability-aware agent harness. At the orchestration level, we equip the harness with structured memory for tracking progress and a VLM planner for selecting subtasks within the VLA's capabilities. To reliably determine subtask completion, we further introduce a policy-coupled task completion state detector that conditions on VLA policy context to signal candidate completion. At the control level, a single VLA executes all subtasks with vision-only perception and native keyboard-and-mouse actions. To mitigate interference between heterogeneous GUI and non-GUI interactions, we introduce regime-specific RMSNorm branches and separate mouse-movement token embeddings. We also train the VLA through world knowledge injection, imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with environment feedback to improve reliability and recovery. HiSTEVE achieves state-of-the-art atomic-task performance, scoring 85.0% on MineStudio Bench and a 37.4% average success rate on OpenHA, while also completing long-horizon tasks such as crafting an iron pickaxe from scratch like human players.

</details>


### [65] [RaSFNet: Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network for LiDAR Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31186)
*Chuang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种Ray-aligned和Scan-aware融合网络(RaSFNet)，用于改进基于投影的点云语义分割方法，改善了几何差异和空间关系的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投影法在聚合点云时会丢失几何差异，局部特征提取限制了跨区域的空间关系建模，并且投影后的邻近性不等同于3D空间中的邻近性。

Method: 提出了Ray-Aligned Geometric Distribution Encoding (RAGDE)模块来描述每个视椎体内的点分布，通过Scan-Topology-Aware Mamba (STAM)块根据LiDAR的方位采样模式组织视椎体特征并捕捉跨扫描区域的长程依赖性，通过Range-Constrained Point-Frustum Fusion (RCPFF)机制实现点和视椎体表示之间的特征交换。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和nuScenes验证集上分别达到了70.2%和77.7%的mIoU得分，表明与对比方法具有竞争力的分割性能。

Conclusion: RaSFNet有效的结合了点云几何和LiDAR扫描特性，展示了在基于投影的语义分割中的优越性。

Abstract: LiDAR point cloud semantic segmentation is a fundamental component of 3D perception for autonomous driving and robotics. Projection-based methods map 3D point clouds into 2D representations for feature extraction using 2D networks. However, aggregating multiple points during projection can obscure geometric differences within each frustum, while local feature extraction limits the modeling of spatial relationships across scanning regions. Moreover, proximity in the projected representation does not necessarily imply proximity in 3D space, and interactions between point and projected features may mix features from different ranges, reducing semantic discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose the Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network (RaSFNet), which incorporates point cloud geometry and LiDAR scanning characteristics into projection-based semantic segmentation. Specifically, the Ray-Aligned Geometric Distribution Encoding (RAGDE) module characterizes point distributions within each frustum using a ray-aligned coordinate system. To model spatial context, a Scan-Topology-Aware Mamba (STAM) block organizes frustum features according to the LiDAR azimuth sampling pattern and captures long-range dependencies across scanning regions. In addition, a Range-Constrained Point-Frustum Fusion (RCPFF) mechanism uses shared range information to guide feature exchange between point and frustum representations while maintaining spatial correspondence. RaSFNet achieves mIoU scores of 70.2% and 77.7% on the SemanticKITTI and nuScenes validation sets, respectively, indicating competitive segmentation performance compared with the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [66] [A Visual Grounding Benchmark for Retinal OCT with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31205)
*Ronald Fecso,José Morano,Botond Fazekas,Paul Widmann-Sedlnitzky,Dorota Himel,Laetita Hinterhuber,Virginia Mares,Andreas Pollreisz,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入RetinaGroundBench基准数据集，探讨了MLLMs在视网膜OCT图像中进行病理定位的能力，并提出了AggIoU评价指标，展示了通过专门策略微调的RetGrounder模型在视觉定位方面取得了显著进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs可以在医学影像中回答临床问题，但需要视觉定位以减少依赖无用的捷径。当前针对视网膜OCT的视觉定位研究较少，故该研究旨在填补这一空白，评估MLLMs的视觉定位能力。

Method: 该研究构建了一个名为RetinaGroundBench的基准数据集，包含815个B扫描图像和4537个边界框的标注，覆盖8种疾病中的13个生物标志物。提出了AggIoU评价指标来评估模型的视觉定位能力。此外，研究者还基于特定策略对模型进行了微调，以提高视觉定位性能。

Result: 在27个开源MLLMs中，表现最好的模型在宏观F1分数上仅为23.7%，RetGrounder模型通过特定策略微调，将定位F1分数提高到了33.9%，相对提高了43%。

Conclusion: 该研究为MLLMs在OCT诊断中的视觉定位能力提供了评估基准，RetGrounder模型展示了一定的潜力，但仍需进一步改进以改善模型的定位指标。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can answer clinical questions about medical images, but trustworthy use requires visual grounding: models should localize the pathology they report rather than rely on spurious shortcuts. Whether MLLMs can visually ground clinical findings in retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT), the most widely used imaging modality in ophthalmology, remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce RetinaGroundBench, the first benchmark for biomarker visual grounding in retinal OCT. It comprises 815 B-scans exhaustively annotated by ophthalmologists with 4,537 bounding boxes covering 13 biomarkers across 8 diseases. Because MLLMs often fragment a single lesion into multiple partial predictions, we propose AggIoU, a metric that evaluates whether the union of overlapping predicted boxes covers the annotated region. Across 27 open-source MLLMs (0.8-38B parameters), we find that visual grounding remains largely unsolved: the best general-purpose model achieves a macro F1 of just 23.7%, with consistently low recall, despite reaching up to 73% balanced accuracy on biomarker presence questions. To narrow this gap, we developed RetGrounder, an MLLM fine-tuned using an OCT-specific strategy integrating grounding and textual information. Despite its relatively small size (8B), RetGrounder improves grounding F1 to 33.9%, a 43% relative improvement. Our benchmark provides a reference point for measuring progress in the field of OCT diagnosis with MLLMs.

</details>


### [67] [Moving Regions, Not Moving Objects: Segmenting What Actually Moves under Camera Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31209)
*Wenhua Jin,Tiancheng Chen,Sheng Tang,Jiahao Li,Yupeng Liu,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MoRSe方法，通过联合解释两帧视频来预测移动区域掩码，从而区分实际场景运动和由摄像机引起的感知运动。MoRSe使用单RGB编码器代替多阶段基础堆栈，在没有使用光流、点轨迹或单独的掩码生成器的情况下进行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法倾向于为整个实例生成掩码，即使只有部分区域真正移动，这掩盖了运动实际上是局部属性的本质。MoRSe旨在解决这一问题，将运动识别细化至区域层面。

Method: MoRSe采用单个RGB编码器直接在两帧间做对比分析来生成移动区域掩码，无需额外的光流计算或点追踪等步骤，简化了推理过程。

Result: MoRSe在多个基准测试中表现优异，优于专门的移动对象分割方法和基于运动阈值的密集4D重建方法，同时显著减少了无动作序列中的误报。

Conclusion: MoRSe通过引入一种新的两帧联合解释机制，有效提升了移动对象分割的精度，适用于复杂动态场景下的目标识别和分割任务。

Abstract: Moving object segmentation is conventionally formulated at the sequence and object level, marking an entire instance as moving once it moves anywhere in a video. Recent instantaneous formulations distinguish moving from stationary intervals, but still generate masks at the object level through frozen segmentation foundation models. Because the mask is derived from an instance-level proposal, motion is typically attributed to the entire instance rather than localized to the regions that actually move. Moreover, such instance-level predictions can produce masks even when no part of the instance is actually moving, revealing a more fundamental issue: motion is not an intrinsic property of an instance; it can only be determined by comparing at least two frames. Building on this premise, we introduce MoRSe, which jointly interprets two frames to predict a moving-region mask, distinguishing actual scene motion from apparent motion induced by the camera. A single RGB encoder replaces multi-stage foundation stacks, so no optical flow, point tracks, or separate mask generator is needed at inference. We release MoRSe-5K, a benchmark of 42 sequences with Static, Dynamic-Static, and Dynamic settings designed to evaluate the model's ability to distinguish and localize moving and stationary regions under different camera and scene motion conditions. MoRSe outperforms both specialized moving object segmentation methods and dense 4D reconstruction methods with motion-thresholded predictions, while producing the fewest false positives on sequences where nothing moves.

</details>


### [68] [GLOW-FM: Efficient Generative Low-Light Image Enhancement with One-Step Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31222)
*Ariel Lapid,Yuval Haitman,Reuven Peretz,Arnon Netzer,Idit Diamant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLOW-FM 提出了一种高效的一步骤图像生成框架，特别适用于低光环境下图像增强。它通过冻结 Tiny-VAE 的潜在空间并利用 Terminal Velocity Matching 提取条件流，实现了一次函数评估下的高质量增强和高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLIE 领域现有的方法虽然能在感知质量上取得显著效果，但迭代采样的高计算成本限制了其实际应用。GLOW-FM 旨在减少计算开销，同时保持高感知质量。

Method: GLOW-FM 使用终端速度匹配 (TVM) 技术实现了一次函数评估下的低光图像增强。它基于冻结的 Tiny-VAE 潜在空间进行条件传输。

Result: GLOW-FM 在多个具有挑战性的现实基准数据集上的实验证明了其在图像质量和跨数据集泛化能力方面达到了业界领先水平，同时仅需一次函数评估和34 GFLOPs的计算量。

Conclusion: GLOW-FM 通过单步推断和紧凑的潜在建模展现了在生成式低光图像增强中的潜力，有望推动该领域的实际部署。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to recover well-exposed and perceptually faithful images from severely degraded observations. Recent diffusion-based generative methods achieve strong perceptual quality by leveraging powerful image priors, but their iterative sampling incurs substantial computational cost, limiting practical deployment. Despite recent efficiency gains in generative LLIE, high-quality enhancement with a single function evaluation and low overall computation remains challenging, particularly under diverse real-world low-light conditions. We introduce GLOW-FM, a one-step latent flow-matching framework for efficient generative LLIE. GLOW-FM performs conditional transport in the latent space of a frozen Tiny-VAE and leverages Terminal Velocity Matching (TVM) to enable enhancement with a single function evaluation. Matched comparisons with alternative one-step objectives support TVM as an effective design choice, and, to the best of our knowledge, this is its first adaptation to image restoration and specifically to LLIE. Extensive experiments on challenging real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GLOW-FM achieves competitive image quality and state-of-the-art cross-dataset generalization while requiring only a single function evaluation and 34 GFLOPs for the small variant. These findings highlight the potential of GLOW-FM to bring generative LLIE closer to practical deployment through single-step inference and compact latent modeling. Code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [69] [Lightweight Frame Compensation for Real-Time Polyp Segmentation under Frame Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31228)
*Weihao Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为LFC的轻量级帧补偿器，通过插在冻结的分割骨干网络前的小巧模块，解决了实时结肠息肉分割中的准确性与延迟问题。LFC在三个结肠息肉视频基准上取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持良好实时性能的同时，提高计算机辅助结肠镜检查中实时结肠息肉分割的精度，主要针对传统帧跳过方法带来的运动模糊和时间一致性差的问题。

Method: LFC是一种轻量级插件模块，能高效融合连续帧的时空注意力，基于伪标签进行训练，不需要专家注释，适用于多种深度学习架构，特别适用于高容量的Transformer架构。

Result: 实验表明，LFC在不同的视频基准数据集上恢复了大部分直接跳帧引起的精度损失，并在某些情况下，能达到按帧分割的精度，同时保持2.05到2.55倍的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: LFC提供了一种轻量级的插件解决方案，解决了实时结肠息肉分割中的准确性与延迟之间的权衡问题，增强了高容量Transformer架构的适应性，并为实际部署提供了便利。

Abstract: Real-time polyp segmentation in computer-assisted colonoscopy is hampered by the difficulty of deploying high-accuracy models under strict latency budgets. Frame skipping, which performs inference every K frames and reuses or interpolates the rest, improves throughput but introduces motion blur and lesion misalignment that degrade temporal consistency. To close this gap, we propose the Lightweight Frame Compensator (LFC), a 77K-parameter plug-and-play module inserted before a frozen segmentation backbone. Operating on a three-frame window, LFC fuses consecutive frames via spatio-temporal attention into a single compensated frame, and is trained only on pseudo-labels generated by a differently seeded model, without any expert annotation. Experiments on three polyp video benchmarks show that LFC recovers most of the accuracy degraded by direct frame skipping and, in several settings, reaches the accuracy of frame-by-frame inference, while retaining a 2.05X--2.55X throughput gain. We further evaluate the temporal stability of three backbones spanning CNN, pyramid Transformer, and global-attention Transformer architectures, revealing a spectrum of sensitivity to frame skipping that arises from how locally or globally each architecture aggregates misaligned input. The compensation gain of LFC follows this spectrum: it is largest for high-capacity Transformer backbones, which achieve the best static accuracy but suffer the most under skip-frame inference. LFC thus offers a lightweight, pluggable, and deployment-friendly route to real-time polyp segmentation.

</details>


### [70] [Reinforcing intra-operative tumour localisation with weak supervision from pre-operative appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31253)
*Sidhart Sami,Ali Sharjeel,Sri Atluri,Miao Zhao,Caroline Roney,Eirini Marouli,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从预手术磁共振（MR）图像中学习肿瘤外观并将其应用于术中CT图像以指导肿瘤定位的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中影像中肿瘤可视化有限，使得利用深度学习进行手术导航变得困难，并且在配对术前和术中数据缺乏的情况下，通过配准传输预手术轮廓也具有挑战性。

Method: 研究中首先训练一个分类器来识别预手术MRI图像中的肿瘤存在。这个分类器被应用到术中CT图像的窗口上，即使存在域间差异，它也倾向于赋予含有肿瘤的窗口更高的肿瘤存在概率。然后使用这些较弱的跨域信号来指导强化学习代理在术中CT图像上定位肿瘤。研究比较了直接转移、少量样本适应和合成转移（在定位前使用未配对的MR和CT创建合成MR）方法。

Result: 在外部肝细胞癌（HCC）队列上，合成转移方法优于其他测试方法和仅基于CT的方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在没有配对MRI-CT训练图像的情况下实现跨模态肿瘤定位。

Abstract: Limited tumour visibility on intra-operative images complicates surgical navigation, including liver hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) targeting on computed tomography (CT) imaging. This makes labelled datasets difficult to curate for deep learning, and transferring pre-operative delineations through registration remains challenging due to intra-operative deformation and lack of paired pre- and intra-operative data. We propose to transfer tumour appearances learned from pre-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images to guide localisation on intra-operative CT through weak supervision. A classifier is trained to classify tumour presence on pre-operative images. This is applied to windows on intra-operative images, and despite the domain gap, it assigns higher tumour-presence probabilities on average to tumour-containing windows. These weak cross-domain signals guide a reinforcement learning agent to localise tumours on intra-operative images. We compare direct transfer, few-shot adaptation and synthetic transfer (trained using unpaired MR and CT), which translates CT into synthetic MR before localisation. On an external HCC cohort, synthetic transfer outperformed other tested methods and CT-only baselines. Our framework enables cross-modality tumour localisation without paired MR-CT training images.

</details>


### [71] [ReCoG: Relational Concept Graph for Training-Free Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31282)
*Changseon Yu,Hyeokjun Kweon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCoG 提出了一个基于关系概念图的检索增强个性化方法，能够更准确地处理混淆度高的概念集合，该项目在多个基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对基于检索的方法在处理混淆度高的概念集合时存在非辨别性问题，提出一种方法来增强个性化性能。

Method: ReCoG 使用一个关系概念图，在图构建阶段利用冻结的视觉语言模型提取概念之间的辨别性线索，然后在推理时根据对应的关线索进行显式的两两对比以消除歧义。

Result: 在引入的 FINGER 基准测试中，ReCoG 显著优于以前的方法，特别是在最难区分的类别中。并在标准个性化基准测试中取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 关系证据在更多场景中被证明是有益的，并且不仅限于细粒度分类任务。
