Abstract: Training-free personalization adapts a frozen vision-language model (VLM) to recognize user-defined concepts from only a few exemplars by retrieving concept entries at inference time. While recent retriever-based methods enrich each concept with descriptive evidence, they largely treat concepts in isolation, so the retrieved information can be plausible yet non-discriminative, especially when the personalized concept set contains highly confusable, fine-grained categories. We argue that personalization is inherently relational: correct recognition depends on the specific distinctions between a concept and its nearest alternatives. To this end, we propose ReCoG, a Relational Concept Graph for retrieval-augmented personalization. ReCoG represents concepts as nodes and augments them with directed edges that explicitly capture discriminative cues between concept pairs, elicited by a frozen VLM during database construction. At inference, ReCoG retrieves a shortlist and resolves ambiguity via explicit pairwise comparisons conditioned on the corresponding relational edges, selecting the concept that consistently wins against competing candidates. We also introduce FINGER benchmark to test personalized recognition on confusable concept sets. Across FINGER, ReCoG substantially outperforms prior methods, with the largest gains in the most confusable regimes. ReCoG also achieves consistently best performance on established personalization benchmarks including captioning and VQA, confirming that relational evidence is broadly beneficial beyond fine-grained settings. Project Page: https://csy-98.github.io/ReCoG/

</details>


### [72] [Retrieval-Augmented Online Textual Memory for Episodic Memory Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31284)
*Raffaele Cali,Giuseppe Lando,Rosario Forte,Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了检索在自传式视频问答中的作用，通过不同的检索单元、查询形式、上下文预算和重排序策略影响多项选择问答效果。最优配置Parent在问题时间仅提供少量文本记忆，但仍能达到与完整记忆和检索基线相当的56.4%准确率。更重要的是，研究表明更好的时间覆盖并不一定意味着更好的答案，增加检索预算虽然能改善时间Hit@K和Recall@K，但也可能引入视觉上合理的干扰项和不明确的标题证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态语言模型在处理长时间的第一人称视频时面临挑战，视觉流需要被压缩进可高效搜索的记忆表示中，同时不能丢失问答所需的关键证据。本研究旨在通过系统研究检索在这些场景中的作用，优化自传式视频问答过程。

Method: 该研究基于经过时间定位的自动生成标题的第一人称视频框注体系，系统研究了检索单元、查询形式、上下文预算和重排序策略对多项选择问答的影响，通过QAEgo4D-Closed数据集进行评估，并将检索作为一个主要变量来研究。

Result: 研究中的最优配置Parent能够高效利用少量标题记忆（匹配精确实时标题片段，然后提供完整的父标题给推理器），在问题时间达到56.4%的准确率，接近完整记忆和检索基线的性能。然而，增加检索预算虽然改善了时间特定度（Hit@K和Recall@K），但仍可能导致视觉上合理的错误干扰和上下文信息不确定性，影响答案的质量。

Conclusion: 研究结果指出现有检索增强的自传式记忆不应仅优化信息检索，也需要考虑上下文的相关性、精确性和回答的区分度。这激励了更结构化的记忆框架，旨在超越原始标题检索，向证据意识的长形式自传式推理发展。

Abstract: Long egocentric videos pose a central challenge for multimodal language models: the visual stream must be compressed into a memory representation that can be searched efficiently while still preserving the evidence needed for question answering. Building on an online textual-memory framework in which first-person videos are converted into temporally grounded captions, this work systematically studies the role of retrieval in episodic-memory video question answering. We evaluate how different retrieval units, query formulations, context budgets, and reranking strategies affect multiple-choice answering on QAEgo4D-Closed, isolating retrieval as the main variable while keeping the underlying generated memory fixed.
  Our best configuration, Parent, matches fine-grained caption subchunks, then provides their full parent captions to the reasoner. It achieves 56.4 % accuracy, matching strong full-memory and retrieval baselines while exposing only a small fraction of the textual memory at question time. More importantly, our analysis shows that better temporal coverage does not necessarily translate into better answers: increasing the retrieval budget improves temporal Hit@K and Recall@K, but can reduce accuracy by introducing visually plausible distractors and uncertain caption evidence. These findings suggest that retrieval-augmented egocentric memory should not be optimized only for evidence recall, but also for the reliability, specificity, and answer-discriminative value of the retrieved context. The study motivates more structured episodic memories that move beyond raw caption retrieval toward evidence-aware representations for long-form egocentric reasoning.

</details>


### [73] [RecallGS: Geometry-Grounded Recall for Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31293)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thien-Phuc Tran,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecallGS 是一种轻量级且无需训练的方法，利用过去的观测历史作为非参数化的几何-语义先验，从而实现高效的3D场景语义分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过额外学习3D网络来恢复从图像推导出的语义特征所缺失的几何上下文，但这增加了训练和运行时的开销。本文提出 RecallGS 方法，致力于利用观测历史中的几何-语义证据，从而简化这一过程。

Method: RecallGS 方法采用召回-建立-融合的流程：CLIP 模型用于检索相关观测；alpha 组合权重确定几何支持的视角；最后将这些视图的语义特征聚合到高斯点渲染的覆盖范围内。

Result: 在 ScanNet 和 ScanNet++ 数据集上，RecallGS 实现了优异的语义分割性能，帧率高达 2.48 FPS，证实了无需额外学习3D分支的情况下可以从观测历史中有效恢复几何-语义上下文。

Conclusion: RecallGS 方法表明几何-语义上下文可以从观测历史中提取，而不必依赖额外的3D网络，这一方法在实时场景理解中具有潜力。

Abstract: Online semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables open-vocabulary scene understanding from streaming observations. However, existing methods recover geometric context missing from image-derived semantic features using an additionally learned 3D network, incurring extra training and runtime overhead. We observe that such geometric-semantic evidence may already exist in the agent's observation history and can be retrieved rather than learned. Based on this insight, we introduce RecallGS (Geometry-Grounded Recall for Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting), a lightweight, training-free mechanism that treats past observations as a non-parametric geometric-semantic prior. RecallGS follows a recall-ground-fuse pipeline: CLIP retrieves relevant observations, alpha-compositing weights identify geometrically supported views, and aggregate their semantic features over each Gaussian's rendered footprint. On ScanNet and ScanNet++, RecallGS achieves strong semantic segmentation performance at 2.48 FPS, demonstrating that geometric-semantic context can be effectively recovered from observation history without an additional learned 3D branch.

</details>


### [74] [PiHand: Latent Hand-Object Interaction Priors for Physics-Based Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31299)
*Niklas Berndt,Xianghui Xie,Enes Duran,Yixiao Wang,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PiHand 是一个能够综合物理特性的手部与物体交互合成框架。通过模拟训练专家策略并使用目标条件 VAE 提取通用手物体交互先验，PiHand 成功实现了一致优于基线的各种精细操控任务，同时支持前所未见的物体和运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互合成方法要么缺乏跨物体的训练迁移能力，要么忽视手物耦合运动，导致物理接触和样本效率低下。PiHand 提出了一种新颖的物理导向交互策略，旨在解决这些问题。

Method: PiHand 采用模拟环境中的专家策略训练，通过目标条件 VAE 网络提炼手物交互的先验知识，并在解码器的潜在空间中优化手部动作以适应不同任务。

Result: PiHand 在 GRAB 和 DexYCB 等数据集上表现优异，不仅能够应对各种不同的手物操控，还能够捕捉手物之间的复杂接触力学关系，提升了自然性和多样性。

Conclusion: PiHand 代表了一个重要的进步，它为精细的手部与物体交互合成问题提供了一个有效的解决方案，展示了在新的手物任务上的优秀泛化能力。

Abstract: Synthesizing physically grounded dexterous hand-object interactions is important for VR/AR, gaming, and robotics, yet remains challenging because subtle contact dynamics, diverse object geometries, and target trajectories must be jointly respected while maintaining natural grasps. Existing methods either track mocap trajectories with limited transfer to novel objects, or rely on hand-only generative priors that fail to capture coupled hand-object motion, sacrificing contact fidelity and sample efficiency. To tackle these challenges, we introduce PiHand, a Physically grounded Interaction-aware Hand motion synthesis framework. PiHand first trains expert policies that track mocap demonstrations in simulation, distills them into a unified hand-object interaction prior using a goal-conditioned VAE, and finally solve diverse dexterous manipulation tasks through bounded residual exploration in its latent space. With only a single reward and no complex curriculum training, this yields general tabletop manipulation policies that generalize to unseen objects and trajectories on GRAB and DexYCB. The same prior transfers to object catching and bimanual handover, two tasks that are rarely seen in its training data. Across all tasks, PiHand consistently outperforms baselines in all metrics. We will release the processed data and model checkpoints.

</details>


### [75] [Data-Driven Vision-Language Models for Remote Sensing: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31304)
*Yue Zhou,Shujun Zhao,Xue Yang,Ruigang Li,Tianwen Zhang,Mengcheng Lan,Chaofeng Chen,Lingfei Ma,Hongjie He,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）优化的遥感（RS）数据集的发展现状系统性回顾，并提供了从数据构建到微调和评估的全面实用指南，旨在构建多样化的高质量数据集，以促进遥感领域向通用人工智能的进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术研究缺乏针对视频-语言模型优化的遥感数据集的系统性调研，无法有效指导高质量RS数据集的构建以及基于数据的模型微调与性能评估。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用文献回顾方法，梳理了RS数据集及其在视觉-语言模型优化中的发展现状，并提供了一整套从数据构建到模型微调和评估的指南。

Result: 本文提供了一种全面的数据集中构建及模型优化方法，旨在推动构建多样化的高质量RS数据集，为特定RS子领域提供专业支持，服务于遥感领域的通用人工智能发展。

Conclusion: 本文的研究有望促进遥感领域通过构建高质量的数据集和有效的模型优化方法，逐步走向通用人工智能的目标。同时，它为该领域提供了有效的指导和技术支持。

Abstract: During the deep learning era, innovations in remote sensing (RS) vision models primarily focused on optimizing network architectures for specific tasks and conducting end-to-end training. However, with the advent of the Vision-Language Models (VLMs) era, constrained by substantial computational resource demands, the mainstream research paradigm has shifted towards adapting general-purpose VLMs using RS vertical domain data for fine-tuning, rather than modifying their core architectures. This transition marks a change in the innovation pathway from model-centric to data-centric. Despite the increasing importance of data, the academic community still lacks a systematic, data-centric survey to guide researchers in constructing high-quality RS vision-language datasets for various application scenarios and in conducting effective model fine-tuning and performance evaluation based on these datasets. To address this gap, this paper presents a systematic review of the development status of RS datasets tailored for VLMs. It further provides a comprehensive practical guide for RS researchers in the large model era, covering the entire pipeline from data construction to fine-tuning and evaluation. This paper aims to encourage the academic community to build more diverse and higher-quality vision-language datasets for specialized RS sub-fields. Ultimately, the goal is to converge these efforts into a comprehensive RS vision-language corpus that offers extensive task coverage and a wide geographical distribution, thereby laying a solid data foundation for advancing towards general artificial intelligence in RS.

</details>


### [76] [Stream4D: Streaming 4D Reconstruction with Training-free Motion Decoupling via Causal KV-Cache Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31320)
*Wei Zhang,Ying Feng,Mengtong Wu,Shiqiang Gong,Huaqi Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream4D 提出了一种无需训练、运动解耦的流式 4D 场景重建框架，能够直接集成到因果 Transformer 后端中，有效解决了动态场景下的漂移和幽灵伪像问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式方法通常假设静态场景，导致长时间序列中的姿态漂移和幽灵伪像，针对此问题，Stream4D 通过利用因果注意机制中的隐含时间运动线索来实现动态和静态区域的解耦。

Method: Stream4D 通过适应性地从多个注意力层和时间窗口中聚合这些线索，并将它们与深度先验融合，生成精确的动力学掩码，解耦静态和动态区域，然后采用软键掩码方案，利用这些动态掩码抑制因果注意力中的动态区域干扰，从而提高相机姿态估计准确性。

Result: 在多种动态场景基准上，Stream4D 显著提高了相机姿态估计和静态场景重建质量，生成可靠的动力学掩码，并有效减轻了长时间序列中的幽灵伪像。

Conclusion: Stream4D 框架继承了因果注意力的效率，并与现有的流式架构兼容，能够有效处理动态场景下的挑战性情况，对4D场景重建具有重要意义。

Abstract: 4D scene reconstruction requires robust handling of dynamic objects, yet existing streaming methods commonly assume static scenes, leading to pose drift and ghosting artifacts over long sequences. We introduce Stream4D, a training-free, motion-decoupled streaming reconstruction framework that can be directly integrated into causal Transformer backbones (e.g., StreamVGGT, Stream3R). Our key observation is that causal attention implicitly encodes rich temporal motion cues within the KV cache. We adaptively aggregate these cues from multiple attention layers and across temporal windows, and fuse them with depth priors to handle challenging cases such as occlusions and textureless regions, producing precise dynamic masks that decouple static and dynamic regions. We then apply a soft key masking scheme that leverages these dynamic masks to suppress interference from dynamic regions in causal attention, thereby improving camera pose estimation accuracy. Stream4D inherits the efficiency of causal attention and remains compatible with existing streaming architectures. On diverse dynamic-scene benchmarks, Stream4D significantly improves camera pose estimation and static-scene reconstruction quality, yields reliable dynamic masks, and effectively mitigates long-sequence ghosting.

</details>


### [77] [MVLoMa: Multi-View Local Feature Matching with Any Geometry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31321)
*Xinyang Lu,Shengzhe Wei,Sheng Tang,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVLoMa 在多个视图中扩展了局部特征匹配方法 LoMa，通过联合描述所有图像并注入跨视图几何上下文来提高匹配效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的局部特征匹配方法主要针对成对图像设计，但在结构从运动和视觉定位中，一参考图像通常需要与多个被检索的或共视的邻居进行匹配。这种方法虽然提高了匹配的质量，但存在计算冗余及信息利用不足的问题，MVLoMa 提出了一种新的适应多视图的 sparse star-graph 匹配器来解决这些局限。

Method: MVLoMa 引入了一种新的方法，用于从多个视图（多个邻居）中提取特征点，并通过一个前向传递过程在视图自注意力和中心-邻居双向跨注意力中进行匹配。

Result: 在 HyperSim、ScanNet++、Mip-NeRF 360 和 Tanks and Temples 25-视图星图基准上，MVLoMa 在三个阈值下都达到了最高的宏观平均相对姿态 AUC，并且比单独评估 24 条中心-邻居边的速度快2.2倍。

Conclusion: MVLoMa 通过改进的多视图几何上下文注入，实现了高效和准确的局部特征匹配，而无需为每个边单独计算，表明它在提高局部特征匹配精度和效率方面有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Local feature matching is typically formulated for image pairs, although structure-from-motion and visual localization commonly match one reference image against many retrieved or covisible neighbours. Applying a pairwise matcher independently to these centre-neighbour edges duplicates reference-side computation and prevents the matcher from exploiting complementary multi-view geometric evidence from the remaining observations. To address these limitations, we introduce MVLoMa, a sparse star-graph matcher that extends LoMa from image pairs to multiple views. MVLoMa first uses a frozen multi-view geometry model to jointly describe all images in a star, then injects the resulting cross-view geometric context into local descriptors. It then matches the centre image to all neighbours in a single forward pass through per-view self-attention and bidirectional centre-neighbour cross-attention. For supervision, we derive positive correspondences only from depth-valid, mutually visible dense warps and explicitly penalize high-confidence matches that violate epipolar geometry. On 25-view star graphs from HyperSim, ScanNet++, Mip-NeRF 360, and Tanks and Temples, MVLoMa obtains the best macro-average relative-pose AUC among the compared methods at all three thresholds (58.65/85.41/90.99). Its matcher is 2.20 times faster than sequential evaluation of the 24 centre-neighbour edges. The same design supports four tested frozen geometry encoders -- DA3, $π^3$, VGGT, and VGGT-$Ω$ -- with DA3-Giant performing best on all reported encoder-ablation metrics. Controlled ablations further isolate the effects of geometry-aware descriptors, visibility filtering, geometric negative supervision, and joint multi-view inference.

</details>


### [78] [WeVisOne-VL: Unified Visual Perception from Objects to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31329)
*Tianming Liang,Junyi Pan,Yuxi Li,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeVisOne-VL 是一种新型的 VLM，通过结构化的下一个标记预测统一了对象级和像素级感知，支持多种视觉任务，并通过轻量级的像素解码器输出实例级像素输出，展示了卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在精细视觉感知方面存在不足，WeVisOne-VL 的提出旨在改进这一问题，提供更可靠的感知系统，适用于真实世界的部署。

Method: WeVisOne-VL 采用了一种特殊的方法：在 VLM 中集成一个轻量级的像素解码器，通过结构调整预测机制，支持对象级和像素级的各种视觉任务。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，WeVisOne-VL 获得领先表现，尤其在对象级和像素级任务上，表现出强大的抗干扰能力。

Conclusion: WeVisOne-VL 的开放源代码旨在促进视觉语言模型向面向真实世界应用的可靠感知系统发展。

Abstract: Existing vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable for fine-grained visual perception. In this report, we present WeVisOne-VL (WeChat Vision All-in-One), a powerful VLM that unifies object-level and pixel-level perception through structured next-token prediction. It supports multiple visual tasks including object detection, referring localization, counting, OCR, segmentation, and matting, enabling localization at any user-specific granularity, from points and boxes to masks and alpha mattes. Architecturally, we integrate a lightweight pixel decoder into the VLM via a special <OBJ> token to generate instance-level pixel outputs. We train the model with a three-stage curriculum SFT followed by mixed-task RL. Across over 30 benchmarks, WeVisOne-VL demonstrates leading performance on both object-level and pixel-level tasks, while maintaining robust rejection for object-absent queries. By releasing WeVisOne-VL to the open-source community, we hope to advance VLMs from broad visual understanding toward truly reliable perception systems for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [79] [A Task-Centric Survey of Long-Document Visual Question Answering: Taxonomy, Evolution, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31385)
*Yufei Tong,Guanjie Cheng,Tianle Wu,Leilei Zhang,Hang Zheng,Peihan Wu,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提供了一个以任务为中心的视觉文档理解综述，专注于长文档的视图问答任务分解为文档解析、证据组织与检索、生成器组织与学习以及评估四个部分，并探讨了技术动机和跨组件连接，对未来发展提出了展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的综述着重于架构和技术方法，忽视了任务瓶颈如何影响技术选择和研究优先级。本文旨在填补这一空白，聚焦于长文档的视图问答任务。

Method: 本文提出了四部分的技术分类框架，并对每一部分的动机、技术响应和技术连接进行了详细阐述。

Result: 研究发现了任务驱动的技术发展路径，从多模态内容理解到充分的高保真证据获取和持续的证据管理。

Conclusion: 未来的研究方向包括确保证据充分性、验证和可纠正的理解，以及预算感知的协调。

Abstract: Visual document understanding is essential for acquiring knowledge from visually rich documents and is attracting growing research interest. Existing surveys emphasize architectures or technical approaches, with limited attention to how task bottlenecks shape technical choices and research priorities. We present a task-centric survey that decomposes long-document visual question answering (VQA) into four components: document parsing, evidence organization and retrieval, generator organization and learning, and evaluation. For each, we develop a fine-grained taxonomy and trace motivations, technical responses, and cross-component connections. We focus on explicit retrieval while covering direct reading. Our temporal analysis traces expanding priorities from multimodal content understanding to sufficient, high-fidelity evidence acquisition and sustained evidence management. We outline future directions in evidence sufficiency, verifiable and correctable understanding, and budget-aware coordination, with an outlook on continual document understanding.

</details>


### [80] [MixPT: Point Transformer with Interleaved Window and Global Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31455)
*Minkyun Seo,Hyungtae Lim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的点云变换器，MixPT，它结合了窗口注意和全局注意机制，以在保持相同序列化模式下的窗口块的同时平衡计算成本和场景级交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的窗口注意机制和全局注意机制在捕获场景的详细局部几何形状和关系方面存在局限性，窗口注意通过限制关注于固定数量的连续令牌来降低成本，而全局注意则会因网络中令牌对数量庞大而导致计算成本增加。

Method: MixPT 通过交替使用窗口注意和全局注意块来平衡计算成本和场景级交互。当场景中的令牌数量较大时，MixPT 将全局注意应用于空间池化令牌，并将剩余更新返回到原始分辨率流中。

Result: 在室内外基准测试中，MixPT 达到了与代表性窗口和全局注意基线相当的准确性，但具有更低的延迟。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了按深度扩展注意力阶段的可行性，通过重复交替排列的窗口和全局注意力块模式。

Abstract: Point cloud transformers aim to capture detailed local geometry and relationships across a scene at a manageable computational cost. Two common approaches are window attention and global attention. They differ in how they enable scene-wide interaction, which refers to information exchange among tokens across a scene. Window attention reduces computational cost by restricting self-attention to groups of tokens called windows; in serialized designs, each window contains a fixed number of consecutive tokens from a spatially ordered sequence. Because each block only directly connects tokens within the same window, information exchange across windows relies on changes in window membership across blocks and hierarchical pooling. Global attention, in contrast, directly connects all tokens within a scene, but computing attention over all token pairs can be costly when the number of tokens is large. To balance computational cost and scene-wide interaction, we introduce MixPT, a point transformer that uses a mix of window and global attention blocks in an interleaved schedule, while retaining a single fixed serialization pattern for its window blocks. For scenes with a large number of tokens, MixPT applies global attention to spatially pooled tokens and returns residual updates to the original-resolution stream. Across indoor and outdoor benchmarks, MixPT achieves competitive accuracy with lower latency than representative window and global attention baselines. We further demonstrate the feasibility of scaling the attention stage in depth by repeating the interleaved pattern of window and global attention blocks.

</details>


### [81] [AnyGameRenderer: Entity-Consistent Generative Rendering Across Games and an Entity-Level Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31466)
*Ruicong Liu,Liangyang Ouyang,Fengbo Lan,Zhixiang Wang,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这项研究提出了一种名为AnyGameRenderer的视频到视频生成渲染器，它可以将粗糙的游戏画面转为高质量视觉效果，包括角色、运动细节和效果，并保持原始布局、摄像机位置及实体位置和时机不变。研究还展示了该模型在多种游戏类型上的一致性表现，并通过实体对应基准测试显示出更好的实体保持效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI游戏生成技术虽能快速创建小型可玩游戏，但游戏画质通常较低，本研究旨在解决此问题，提升AI生成游戏的视觉质量，提供更沉浸的体验。

Method: 研究团队通过LoRA微调与MiniMax-H3模型相结合的方法，利用从不同来源获取的数据进行训练，包括AI生成的游戏渲染数据、Unreal Engine渲染数据和真实游戏录影。

Result: 实验结果表明，AnyGameRenderer在多个游戏类型上具备高度通用性，特别是在保持实体对应方面表现优异。相较于基础模型，实体时间匹配率从50.9%提升至62.9%，遗漏的计数变化率从34.7%降低至21.3%，额外的实体生成和未渲染实体数量减少。

Conclusion: AnyGameRenderer通过视频到视频转译的方式，极大提升了AI生成游戏的视觉质量，未来有望进一步优化游戏生成系统，增强其视觉吸引力。

Abstract: AI coding agents can now build small playable games in minutes, but the results usually look like programmer art: gray blockouts, colored geometric primitives, and flat semantic blobs. We present AnyGameRenderer, a video-to-video generative renderer that turns such footage into high-quality visuals with characters, motion detail, and effects. Throughout, it keeps the layout, camera, and every entity's position and timing intact.
  Our AnyGameRenderer generalizes across a wide range of genres that agents build, such as tower defense, auto-battlers, top-down survivors, turn-based battles, kart racing, and crowd runners.
  AnyGameRenderer takes plain video as its game-state input, with no complex representations such as engine G-buffers, per-object class labels, or low-poly proxies. Gray blockouts, colored primitives, semantic maps, and wireframes all work as input.
  It also preserves entity correspondence: group counts stay correct, and entities appear and disappear on the right frame.
  We implement AnyGameRenderer by LoRA fine-tuning MiniMax-H3 ref2va, a 33B reference-video-conditioned video generation model. Training uses $\sim$10k paired clips from three sources: (i) multi-stream rendering data from 38 agent-made games exported from GEAR, an AI game-creation tool; (ii) two-stream rendering data from Unreal Engine, with programmatically controlled crowds, count changes, and entity scales; and (iii) real gameplay recordings, with pairs reconstructed via 3D-box detection.
  To evaluate the entity correspondence, we propose an entity-level benchmark of 59 held-out cases with 753 entities, scored against ground-truth boxes. Compared with the base model, AnyGameRenderer better preserves entity correspondence: temporal match rises from 50.9\% to 62.9\%, missed count changes fall from 34.7\% to 21.3\%, and fewer entities are hallucinated or left unrendered.

</details>


### [82] [Ask About the Right Moment: Training-Free Object Identity Reasoning for Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31471)
*Di Yang,Xiaochang Li,Muyang Liu,Yufei Wang,Yanhai Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为‘追踪，然后绑定’（Track, Then Bind）的方法，通过将对象身份绑定与时间维持分开处理，显著提升了视频语言模型在涉及物体跟踪问题上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有视频语言模型（Video-LMs）无法保持物体身份在时间上的连续性，研究提出了一种新的策略来改善视频理解效果。

Method: 研究开发了一种无需训练的方法——追踪，然后绑定，该方法利用可提示的视频追踪器来追踪每个候选物体，并通过语言模型验证这些候选对象的身份，之后不再重新解释原始视频。

Result: 通过这种方法，研究解决了所有基准测试任务，与微调后跟踪模型的表现相当，甚至在处理更复杂的测试用例时表现更佳，如硬货币使游戏和TOC-Bench中的问题。

Conclusion: 这一无训练方法展现了在视频对象跟踪任务中的强大潜力，并且能够实现在真实场景下比直接解析视频更高的性能。

Abstract: Many questions about a video concern one physical object. Where does the ball end up after the cups are shuffled? Which card is the queen? How often was the mug covered? Video-language models (Video-LMs) recognise such objects in single frames but do not keep them apart over time. On real shell-game videos, open models from 8B to 235B parameters, their thinking variants, a reinforcement-learned tool agent and three training-free tool agents all stay within ten points of chance. We separate semantic identity binding from temporal identity maintenance. An object's identity should be fixed at a moment when the evidence for it is visible, and carried afterwards by a tracker. \emph{Track, Then Bind} implements this principle without training. A promptable video tracker follows every candidate object and draws it with a track number. A frozen vision-language model answers a static question about the identifying evidence, which binds the target to a track. The answer is then derived from the tracked state in text, without asking the model to reinterpret the original video during read-out. A single configuration covers all benchmarks. With Qwen3-VL-32B it solves both VET-Bench tasks, where a fine-tuned trajectory model reaches 92\% and 90\% and the same VLM voting over 16 samples stays at 30--32\%. It answers 55.4\% of the hardest Perception Test cup games, where the best published model answers 41.5\%, and, using track-derived timelines, 48.6\% of TOC-Bench questions, against 37.1\% for the same model answering directly. Drawing the same tracks on the video and asking the question directly falls 12--19 points short of the agent on real cup games.

</details>


### [83] [MoG-VLM: Efficient Video Anomaly Detection through Motion-Guided Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31478)
*Khaled Waleed Dawoud,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer,Khan Muhammad,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoG-VLM 提出了一种基于运动适应性的异常检测框架，该框架仅在需要时激活密集的多帧 VLM 推理，并通过时空梯度幅度进行内容感知采样，从而在保持检测性能的同时显著提高了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测的方法存在计算开销大或易遗漏局部异常的问题，MoG-VLM 通过运动信息调节 VLM 的推理以更加智能地分配计算资源。

Method: MoG-VLM 采用了一个分层双流设计，仅在具有较高异常可能性的帧周围激活密集多帧 VLM 推理，并引入了时空梯度幅度（TGM）进行内容感知采样。通过这些方法，MoG-VLM 可以在不均匀计算资源的情况下进行有效推理。

Result: MoG-VLM 在四个基准数据集上取得了优异的 AUC-ROC 分数，其中在 XD-Violence 数据集上超越了之前最好的结果，并实现了 1.31 倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: MoG-VLM 通过运动信息调整 VLM 的推理，有效地分配了计算资源，提升了检测性能并加速了推理过程。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for video anomaly detection, but dense frame-level inference makes them impractical for real-time deployment. Existing acceleration commonly relies on uniform sampling, which ignores content dynamics and can either waste computation on static segments or miss localized anomalies. We propose MoG-VLM a motion-adaptive anomaly detection framework that leverages motion purely as a lightweight control signal to decide when VLM inference is necessary. To this end, we introduce Temporal Gradient Magnitude (TGM) which performs content-aware sampling to concentrate computation on informative regions while avoiding redundant processing. We further introduce a hierarchical dual-stream design in which dense multi-frame VLM reasoning is activated only around frames with elevated anomaly likelihood, via anchor-triggered context-window expansion with early termination. A fusion module then reconciles these instantaneous semantic cues with temporal action dynamics. Across four benchmarks, our method achieves 91.91%, 84.30%, 87.88%, and 78.96% AUC-ROC on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, MSAD, and ShanghaiTech, respectively, establishing new state of the art on three benchmarks under the zero-shot, training-free setting. On XD-Violence, we improve over the previous best result by +0.57% while delivering a 1.31x inference speedup. These results demonstrate that content-aware allocation of VLM capacity, processing fewer frames more intelligently rather than all frames uniformly, is a more effective axis of progress than uniformly densifying VLM inference across frames. Code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/kdawoud91/MoG-VLM.

</details>


### [84] [AgentVQA: A Unified Benchmark for Agentic Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31494)
*Advait Gupta,Sachin Konan,Tom Yin,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于评估视觉-语言模型在五个代理领域中的离线基准AgentVQA，旨在解决现有基准数据有限且碎片化的不足。该基准涵盖Web代理、机器人、第一人称视频、游戏和空间规划等场景，包含1000个真实世界任务，结果显示模型在这些场景中的准确率仅为58.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟器和真实环境的在线评估成本高且难以扩展，AgentVQA提供了更快更可靠的离线评估方式，便于跨领域模型对比。

Method: 通过手动从23个源数据集中挑选出1000个具有挑战性的现实世界代理实例，并根据可靠离线评估标准进行调整。

Result: AgentVQA被应用到22个模型中，最高准确率为58.5%。

Conclusion: 尽管现有的VLMs在这些真实场景中表现不佳，但其表现主要受到将当前和不断变化的视觉状态进行准确表示和维持的失败影响，而非任务误解或下游逻辑推理。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can perform a broad range of tasks across diverse settings, yet their performance in agentic contexts remains poorly understood. Although online evaluations in simulators and real-world environments are essential for measuring interactive agent performance, they are costly and challenging to scale. Complementary offline VQA benchmarks can enable faster and more reliable comparisons across agentic domains, yet existing benchmarks remain limited and fragmented. To address this gap, we introduce AgentVQA, an offline benchmark for evaluating VLMs on realistic agentic scenarios across five agent domains: Web Agents, Robotics, Egocentric Videos, Games, and Spatial Planning. We build AgentVQA by manually selecting 1000 challenging, real-world agent examples from 23 source datasets and adapting them for reliable offline evaluation. The resulting benchmark preserves realistic scenarios from interactive environments across diverse domains, and its model rankings track those from corresponding online evaluations. Across the 22 evaluated models, the best achieves only 58.5% accuracy. Our cross-domain analysis shows that errors are driven primarily by failures to ground and maintain accurate representations of the current and evolving visual state, including localizing relevant UI elements, interpreting motion, tracking state changes over time, and inferring 3D spatial relationships, rather than by task misinterpretation or downstream logical reasoning.

</details>


### [85] [Evaluation of Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Software Ensembles for Hippocampal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31505)
*Gabriel Oliveira-Stahl,Anna Schroder,James Moggridge,Hamza A. Salhab,Caroline Micallef,Josephine Barnes,M. Jorge Cardoso,Carole H. Sudre,Matthew Grech-Sollars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究采用五个异质性分割方法的集成，旨在提高海马分割的准确性和效率，并通过不确定性评估减少低置信度分割，最终发现这种方法在阿尔茨海默病分类中有所助益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的海马体分割对于诊断和监测神经退行性疾病（如阿尔茨海默病）和癫痫等疾病至关重要。虽然有许多自动化分割方法，但它们在临床中的应用仍然有限。本研究旨在通过集成多种分割方法以提供可靠的不确定性评估，从而增强临床转化的可行性。

Method: 研究评估了InnerEye、ASHS、FastSurfer、HippoSeg和FreeSurfer五个不同的分割方法，涵盖了两个痴呆症数据集和一个癫痫数据集。通过建立子集合并使用样本方差、互信息和预测熵进行不确定性量化，识别并标记低置信度的分割结果。

Result: InnerEye、FastSurfer和HippoSeg组成的子集既准确又高效，展示了如何在计算成本和性能之间取得平衡。此外，研究发现不确定性评估可以减少不准确的分割，表明其在自动化引导下重要病例专家复核的可能性。研究还发现，集成方法的不确定性与运动伪差严重程度存在显著相关性，并且在阿尔茨海默病的分类中也有一定贡献。

Conclusion: 集成分割方法及不确定性评估展示了在海马体分割中提高准确性和效率的潜力，并为进一步临床应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate hippocampal segmentation can be a useful tool for diagnosing and monitoring neurological conditions such as Alzheimer's disease and epilepsy. While numerous automated segmentation methods exist, their clinical adoption remains limited. Reliable uncertainty assessment can enhance trust and facilitate clinical translation. This study evaluates ensembles of five heterogeneous hippocampal segmentation methods - InnerEye, ASHS, FastSurfer, HippoSeg, and FreeSurfer - across two dementia datasets and one epilepsy dataset. The sub-ensemble containing InnerEye, FastSurfer, and HippoSeg emerged as both accurate and efficient, highlighting the feasibility of balancing computational cost and performance. Additionally, ensemble-derived uncertainty quantification with sample variance, mutual information, and predictive entropy is shown to reduce inaccurate segmentations by flagging low-confidence cases, potentially providing a mechanism for automatically escalating ambiguous cases for expert assessment. Lastly, this study explores the downstream clinical utility of ensemble-derived uncertainty, finding a significant correlation with motion artefact severity and a modest contribution to Alzheimer's disease classification.

</details>


### [86] [Diffusion-Based Synthesis of Complete 3D Biparametric Prostate MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31523)
*Claudia Giardina,Montse Pardàs,Verónica Vilaplana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种条件3D扩散框架，用于在CT分割掩码上合成完整的前列腺双参数MRI（bpMRI），评估显示两种合成路径均可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏大且有代表性的前列腺MRI数据集，该研究旨在通过合成MRI提高深度学习应用中的数据可用性。

Method: 该研究提出了一个条件3D扩散框架，支持顺序合成和联合合成策略，并使用PI-CAI、FastMRI Prostate和PROSTATEx数据集训练了五种不同配置。

Result: 评价结果表明，两种合成路径均能实现合成bpMRI，但联合合成在分布保真度方面具有竞争力，而直接合成ADC和计算高b值DWI则优于从合成扩散加权图像中推导。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个有效的条件3D合成框架，能够提高深度学习在前列腺bpMRI领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic biparametric MRI (bpMRI) may help alleviate the limited availability of large and representative prostate MRI datasets for deep learning applications. In this work, we present a conditional 3D diffusion framework for anatomy-conditioned synthesis of complete prostate bpMRI from anatomical masks. The framework supports sequential synthesis, in which T2w and diffusion modalities are generated by separately trained models, and joint synthesis, in which all bpMRI modalities are generated simultaneously within a single model. Five configurations were trained using PI-CAI, FastMRI Prostate, and PROSTATEx datasets to investigate different conditioning schemes, target modalities, and synthesis strategies. Evaluation using distribution-based metrics, paired-image similarity, and qualitative assessment demonstrated the feasibility of both synthesis pathways. Joint synthesis achieved competitive distributional fidelity while generating complete bpMRI, whereas direct synthesis of ADC and computed high-b-value DWI outperformed their derivation from synthetic diffusion-weighted images.

</details>


### [87] [LACToS: Local Anomaly Detection via Conditional Typicality of Superpixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31538)
*Jihoon Hong,Alireza Kheirandish,Rohan Sanda,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LACToS 是一种基于统计典型性且通过超级像素分组和计算效率代理来估算典型性而变得可行的局部 OOD 检测方法，其在医学影像、人脸和X射线安全检查等三个不同的应用领域展现出比现有方法更稳健的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在工业缺陷检测等特定上下文中表现优秀，但在其它场景尤其是内类分布为多模态时，性能显著下降。因此，LACToS旨在解决这一问题。

Method: LACToS 方法包含了两方面创新：(i) 将像素分组为超像素；(ii) 使用高效的代理来估算典型性。这些创新使得方法在处理复杂的内类分布时更具稳健性。

Result: LACToS 在医学影像、人脸和X射线安全检查等领域的基准测试中表现出了比现有方法更稳健的性能。

Conclusion: LACToS 是一种针对复杂内类分布和多模态数据集的局部 OOD 检测方法，具有广泛的适用性和更高的稳健性。

Abstract: Local anomaly detection is the task of identifying spatially confined anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) regions within an otherwise in-distribution (ID) image. Most existing methods are designed for a specific context, with a strong focus on industrial defect detection, where they achieve excellent performance on well-established benchmarks. However, we observe that these methods can exhibit significant performance drops in other contexts, especially when the inlier distribution is multimodal. To account for this inlier complexity, we propose LACToS, a principled local OOD detection method based on statistical typicality and made tractable through two innovations: (i) grouping pixels into superpixels and (ii) using a computationally efficient proxy to estimate typicality. We evaluate LACToS across three diverse applications spanning medical imaging, human faces, and X-ray security screening. LACToS performs more robustly than prior methods across these benchmarks. The code is released here: https://github.com/voilalab/LACToS.

</details>


### [88] [Squaring-the-Sphere: Distortion-Free and Seamless Panorama Generation via CubeAtlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31565)
*Xuhui Zuo,Chenjie Cao,Tengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Yisu Zhang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了名为Squaring-the-Sphere的全景图像生成框架，使用一种新颖的表示CubeAtlas，在保持透视几何的同时组织所需的跨面关系，使预训练的扩散变换器能够在单个过过程中生成完整的全景图像，同时降低注意计算量，并实现了无损视场细节增强和高分辨率生成，从而提高了几何保真度、跨视图一致性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景图像表示方法在局部视角保真度和全局球面连续性之间存在权衡，本文旨在克服这种权衡，以提高全景图像生成的质量。

Method: 该方法提出了一种名为CubeAtlas的新表示，通过展开所有立方体面以单个紧凑画布的形式，保留立方体的透视几何，同时组织全景组装所需的跨面关系。此外，通过使用布局感知稀疏注意力掩码，保持所需的全景连续性交互，同时去除几何上无关的令牌对。

Result: 该方法在多项基准测试中展示了更好的几何保真度、跨视图一致性和高分辨率视觉质量，相比其他方法提高了全景图像生成的质量。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了其提出的Squaring-the-Sphere框架的有效性，并展示了其在全景图像生成中的优势。

Abstract: Generating 360-degree panoramas requires both local perspective fidelity and global spherical continuity. Existing representations face a trade-off: equirectangular projection (ERP) enables seamless panorama modeling but suffers from severe distortion and a domain gap to pre-trained image generators, whereas cubemaps preserve perspective geometry but often produce inconsistent faces and visible seams. We present Squaring-the-Sphere, a panoramic image generation framework built on a novel representation CubeAtlas, which unfolds all cubemap faces into a single compact canvas. By arranging orientation-aligned, rotated polar regions alongside the equatorial faces, this representation preserves the perspective geometry of cubemaps while organizing the cross-face relationships required for seamless panorama assembly. It further enables a pretrained diffusion transformer to model the complete panorama jointly in a single pass, without altering the backbone architecture or positional encoding. While CubeAtlas has a token count comparable to ERP, its structured layout makes many dense attention interactions unnecessary. We therefore introduce a layout-aware sparse attention mask that preserves the interactions required for panorama continuity while removing geometrically irrelevant token pairs. As a result, CubeAtlas achieves better quality than ERP modeling with lower attention computation. Furthermore, we introduce a training-free super-resolution technique and a fusion scheme that enhance fine details in distortion-free perspective space before achieving the final ERP panorama. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves geometric fidelity, cross-view consistency, and high-resolution visual quality over previous methods.

</details>


### [89] [CellTrack-Bench: Microscopic Cell Tracking Benchmark for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31566)
*Ilan Theodoro,Jordão Bragantini,Loïc A. Royer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究旨在评估视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在细胞追踪任务上的性能，创建了一个新的基准CellTrack-Bench，包含了多种成像模态的标准细胞追踪数据集，从中评估了多种预训练和开源模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在文本任务上的优异表现，研究者致力于探索它们在涉及高度专业化领域的科学问题上的潜力，尤其是在细胞追踪等需要时空感知的任务中。

Method: 研究者构建了一个名为CellTrack-Bench的新基准测试集，涵盖四个成像模态的15个标准细胞追踪数据集，共1,537个问题。他们评估了多种前沿的封闭源代码模型和开源模型，并分析了模型的失败模式。

Result: 该研究提供了对VLMs在细胞追踪中的泛化能力和具体失败模式的评估结果。

Conclusion: 研究者通过CellTrack-Bench提出了视觉-语言模型的一个具体应用领域，并提供了代码及模型输出，展示了VLMs在细胞追踪中的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models have shown outstanding performance on text-first tasks such as coding, math, and writing. Recent efforts are shifting models' training to include scientific problems that often live in highly specialized domains beyond text, such as protein structure and biological images. However, in these scenarios, their general-purpose accuracy compared with specialized algorithms remains in question. One such domain is cell tracking, which requires spatiotemporal awareness of cell dynamics. To evaluate this, we created a new benchmark, CellTrack-Bench, which assesses Vision-Language Models' (VLMs) cell-tracking-related performance against specialized trackers. The full benchmark draws from 15 standard cell-tracking datasets across four imaging modalities and comprises 1,537 problems corresponding to 9,041 cell-linking decisions. We selected a hard subset requiring greater reasoning effort by identifying failures shared across distinct algorithmic trackers. With this proposed dataset, we: (i) evaluated different environment harnesses for VLMs; (ii) benchmarked 9 frontier closed-source models and 13 open-weight models; and (iii) provided a detailed analysis of their failure modes. Code, the stored model outputs, and a pipeline that regenerates the benchmark from the source datasets are available at https://github.com/royerlab/celltrack-bench.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Best-of-$k$: Prior-Consistent DAPS for Ambiguous Nonlinear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31592)
*Merve Kocabas,Benjamin A. Burns,Andreas Geiger,Kent Gauen,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PC-DAPS是为了解决非线性逆问题中的模糊测量问题而设计的，通过保持单一采样运行中的多个候选解决方案，减少先验不一致的样本，并在非线性逆问题中提高样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的采样器在重建过程中依赖于局部近似，这在模糊测量的情况下会导致测量指导偏向一个与先验支持模式接近但本身不受先验支持的模式。PC-DAPS旨在通过在单一采样运行中维持多个候选解决方案来解决这一问题。

Method: PC-DAPS是Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS)的一个变体，它逐渐减少退火噪声，根据候选解的先验一致性来修剪并替换较弱的候选解，从而剔除先验不一致的样本。

Result: 该方法在非线性逆问题中减少了先验不一致的样本总数，提高了样本质量。同时，它在保持线性逆问题样本质量及多样性的情况下，仍然能显著提升样本质量。

Conclusion: PC-DAPS提供了一种新的采样机制，能够有效改善模型在非线性逆问题中的表现，尤其是在模糊测量的情况下，仍然能够发现真正符合先验的信息。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become strong priors for solving inverse problems. However, nonlinear inverse problems such as phase retrieval remain challenging when multiple measurement-consistent solutions are distinguishable only by the prior; we call such measurements ambiguous. Existing diffusion-based samplers rely on local approximations during reconstruction. For an ambiguous measurement, these approximations can allow measurement guidance to favor a measurement-consistent mode that is close to a prior-supported mode but is itself not supported by the prior. Evaluation often masks this type of failure by reporting the best-of-$k$ runs, where the best is selected by comparison to the ground truth. We introduce Prior-Consistent DAPS (PC-DAPS), a variant of Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) designed to address this failure case by maintaining multiple candidate solutions within a single sampling run. As annealing noise decreases, PC-DAPS prunes and replaces weaker candidates according to their prior consistency, without any ground-truth access. PC-DAPS reduces the incidence of prior-inconsistent samples and improves sample quality on nonlinear inverse problems while preserving sample quality and diversity on linear inverse problems.

</details>


### [91] [F2G-Pose: Geometry-Aware Foundation Feature Lifting for Direct RGB-D Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31623)
*Jinyu Zhang,Haitao Lin,Jiashu Hou,Xiangyang Xue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: F2G-Pose通过单一前向传递从单个RGB-D观测值直接估计类别级别的姿态、度量尺寸和相机帧中完成的形状，其性能超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前RGB-D对象姿态估计方法在可靠性和效率之间的权衡问题，以及克服因类别间差异、对称性和部分观测带来的挑战。

Method: F2G-Pose采用了一种直接、单次通过的实时框架，构建了从密集视觉基础特征到部分点云的映射，使用DGCNN进行局部几何编码，并通过Mixture-of-Experts Transformer结构集成物体级结构。同时，它通过密集的几何监督促进度量一致性。

Result: 在SOPE和ROPE数据集上实现了最先进的精度，在仅进行姿态推断时运行速度达到28 FPS，进一步展示了在HouseCat6D和真实物体制作中的相机帧几何和HANDAL的物体转移性能。

Conclusion: F2G-Pose在效率和可靠性方面表现出色，同时提供了实时性能，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies category-level RGB-D object pose estimation, which recovers an object's 3D rotation and translation without instance-specific CAD models or reference views. This task is highly ambiguous due to intra-class variations, symmetries, and partial observations. Current solutions struggle to balance reliability and efficiency: methods based on intermediate structures are prone to error propagation, while diffusion models suffer from high inference latency and complex post-hoc candidate extraction. To address this, we present F2G-Pose, a real-time direct, single-pass framework that predicts category-level pose, metric size, and camera-frame completed shape from a single RGB-D observation in one feed-forward pass. F2G-Pose lifts dense visual foundation features to partial point clouds, encodes local geometry with DGCNN tokenization, and integrates object-level structure with a Mixture-of-Experts Transformer. Joint size prediction and camera-frame shape completion encourage metric consistency through dense geometric supervision. With its task-specific network trained only on pose-annotated synthetic SOPE data and its frozen RADIO backbone externally pretrained, F2G-Pose achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SOPE and ROPE while running at 28 FPS for pose-only inference, and further demonstrates strong camera-frame geometry on HouseCat6D and real-object transfer on HANDAL. Analyses of latency, robustness, shape completion, and GenPose++ candidate behavior further demonstrate the efficiency and reliability of F2G-Pose, with real-world manipulation experiments demonstrating deployment potential under segmented RGB-D inputs. The project page, code, and pretrained models are made available at https://jrryzh.github.io/F2G-Pose/.

</details>


### [92] [HiP3R: Hierarchical Pose Graphs for Long-Horizon 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31635)
*Zunnan Xu,Shangchen Zhou,Yihang Luo,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiP3R提出了一种通过分层姿态图优化来改进相机轨迹和3D重建的方法。该方法首先在子序列级别优化鲁棒的Sim(3)图以建立全局布局和尺度，然后保持该布局固定，并为每个帧估计一个共享的SE(3)相机，将重叠窗口预测合并为单一轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于子序列的方法效率低下且难以解决规模漂移问题，而帧级别的优化虽然灵活，但大规模应用耗时且可能破坏度量尺度。因此需要一种方法来平衡这种权衡。

Method: HiP3R采用了一种分层的优化策略，首先在子序列级别优化Sim(3)图以确定全局布局和尺度，然后在每个帧上进行SE(3)相机的优化，从而将多个预测结果整合成单一的轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明HiP3R在多个基准测试中提高了相机轨迹和3D重建的效果，并且该方法适用于子序列和流式后端，无需额外的训练或微调。

Conclusion: HiP3R提供了一种有效的解决方案来优化相机轨迹和3D重建，这种方法通过优化分层的姿态图实现了全局一致性和局部精度的结合。

Abstract: Feed-forward models accurately estimate camera motion and 3D geometry from short video windows. However, computational and memory costs prevent direct inference on sequences containing thousands of frames. Long videos must therefore be processed as overlapping chunks. This division produces independent local reconstructions with inconsistent coordinate frames and duplicate estimates of shared cameras. Achieving global consistency requires propagating constraints from adjacent overlaps and distant revisits across the sequence. This requirement naturally motivates pose graph optimization. However, the choice of graph level creates a trade-off. Chunk-level graphs efficiently resolve scale drift and close large loops. Yet, their rigid transformations cannot correct internal errors or conflicting frame predictions. Conversely, frame-level optimization offers local flexibility, but scaling it globally is computationally expensive and can break metric scale. HiP3R resolves this trade-off using a hierarchical pose graph. HiP3R first optimizes a robust chunk-level $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ graph to establish the global layout and scale. It then holds this layout fixed and estimates one shared $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ camera per frame, turning overlapping window predictions into a single trajectory. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of HiP3R in improving camera trajectories and 3D reconstruction. Our method applies to both chunk-based and streaming backends without additional training or fine-tuning. The codes are available at https://kkakkkka.github.io/HiP3R.

</details>


### [93] [WorldForge V2: Eliciting Camera-Trajectory Alignment in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31640)
*Chenxi Song,Yanming Yang,Tong Zhao,Ruibo Li,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WorldForge V2的方法，通过使用变形参考和选择性引导，实现了准确的相机控制，同时保持了预训练视频模型的生成能力。方法包括使用光学流分析分离运动控制和外观，使用双重路径策略对比指导和未指导的预测，以及引入Trajectory LoRA集中训练以减少辅助模型评估的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散模型在空间任务中应用受限，本文旨在解决相机控制不精确、空间和时间不一致以及相机运动与场景动力学纠缠等问题，提高视频生成的质量和场景控制能力。

Method: 方法包括利用变形参考和选择性引导框架来逐步改善目标相机路径上的生成结果。通过光学流分析识别与运动相关的潜在通道，进行选择性引导，避免了视觉伪影，同时使用双重路径策略比较并纠正不完美的参考带来的伪影。此外，引入了Trajectory LoRA和两阶段训练策略，集中在最响应轨迹引导的网络块上进行训练，以进一步提高生成质量。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在多数据集上的生成质量和轨迹准确性达到了新高，实现了在生成视频过程中相机控制的可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文方法可以实现准确的相机控制并在未见的场景中进行泛化。通过选择性引导和双重路径策略，大幅减少了辅助模型的使用。

Abstract: Video diffusion models have learned rich 3D/4D priors, making them promising for scene generation and video re-rendering. However, their use in spatial tasks is limited by imprecise camera control, spatial and temporal inconsistencies, and entangled camera motion with scene dynamics. Existing approaches, such as task-specific fine-tuning and post-process warping, often introduce visual artifacts, generalize poorly, or incur high computational costs. We propose WorldForge V2 to achieve accurate camera control while preserving the generative capabilities of pretrained video models. We first develop a training-free guidance framework using warped references to iteratively refine generation along a target camera path. Optical-flow analysis identifies motion-related latent channels for selective guidance, helping separate motion control from appearance. A dual-path strategy further compares guided and unguided predictions to correct artifacts from imperfect references. Together, these components guide viewpoint changes while preserving scene appearance and object motion. To reduce refinement costs, v-cache approximates the correction using the preceding denoising prediction, avoiding an additional model evaluation. We further extend motion localization from latent channels to network blocks and introduce Trajectory LoRA, which concentrates adaptation on blocks most responsive to trajectory guidance. A two-stage training strategy teaches these adapters to denoise the states encountered during guided generation, using limited supervision effectively. This enables reliable camera control with very little training data, with a single training scene already supporting generalization to unseen scenes. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations across multiple datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and trajectory accuracy in camera-controlled video generation.

</details>


### [94] [UniVOE: A Removal-Driven Framework for Interaction-Aware Universal Video Object Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31641)
*Shuzhou Yang,Lanqing Guo,Lingen Li,Weiqi Li,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniVOE 是一个基于移除任务训练的框架，它能够同时训练视频中的对象移除和注入模型。通过一种基于效果的移除器，它可以从真实视频中生成大规模配对监督，然后在反方向上训练注入模型以恢复对象及其动态与互动效果。为了提高感知真实性，引入了一个基于奖励的后训练方法，用于优化注入结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，视频对象移除和注入操作虽然彼此相对立，但训练难度却存在显著差异。注入操作需要大量配对监督以精确建模对象的外观、动态及场景依赖性等效果，然而这在实践中较为困难。为解决这一问题，UniVOE框架应运而生。

Method: 该方法首先通过视频修补先验知识训练一个效果感知移除器，并微调于含有和不含对象的视频对。然后，将该移除器用于将真实含有对象的视频转换为不含对象的视频，从而生成大规模配对监督。之后，在这些配对监督上反向训练注入模型，以恢复对象及其动态和互动效果。此外，提出了基于奖励的方法，将注入结果与人类偏好对齐，以提升感知真实性。

Result: 实验结果表明，UniVOE在所有测试的案例中，尤其是在复杂互动效果情景中，表现优于开源和商业系统的最佳版本。

Conclusion: 综上所述，UniVOE框架通过采用移除驱动的方法，有效地解决了视频编辑领域的挑战，并在对象移除和注入方面取得了卓越表现。

Abstract: Video object removal and injection are reciprocal editing operations between object-present and object-absent video states, yet they differ substantially in training difficulty. Removal can be effectively bootstrapped from video inpainting and further adapted to erase object-related interaction effects. Injection, however, requires large-scale paired supervision to model object appearance, dynamics, and scene-dependent effects, while perfectly aligned real-world pairs are difficult to collect and synthetic data lacks sufficient realism and diversity. To address this challenge, we present UniVOE, a removal-driven framework for training both video object removal and injection models. We first train an effect-aware remover by transferring video inpainting priors and finetuning on object-present/object-absent pairs. The trained remover converts real object-present videos into object-absent counterparts, creating large-scale paired supervision for injection. An injection model is trained on these pairs in the reverse direction to recover objects together with their dynamics and interaction effects. Moreover, since pixel-level reconstruction alone cannot adequately capture perceptual realism, we introduce a reward-based post-training method that aligns injection results with human preferences using a proprietary reward model. Experiments against strong open-source and commercial systems demonstrate that UniVOE achieves state-of-the-art performance on both object removal and injection, particularly in challenging interaction-effect cases.

</details>


### [95] [GigaBench: Benchmarking Learnable Representations for Gigapixel Image Compression and Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31644)
*Matteo Fusconi,Alex Costanzino,Pierluigi Zama Ramirez,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GigaBench 是首个专注于在 gigapixel 图像上部署可学习表示 (LRs) 的基准和实验评估工具，涵盖图像压缩和超分辨率，通过系统评估识别了不同方法的一致趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学、文化和工业领域，gigapixel 图像正变得越来越普遍，而可学习表示 (LRs) 由于其紧凑性、分辨率无关性和适应直接神经处理的特性，引起了科学社区的兴趣。

Method: GigaBench 采用将 Implicit、Hybrid 和 Explicit 方法为代表的LRs，进行跨领域的图像收集与合成渲染，系统地评估了重建质量、模型大小、压缩时间及推理时间。

Result: 通过全面的评估，GigaBench 识别出了不同方法的一致趋势，并为未来的深入研究提供了有益的指导。

Conclusion: GigaBench 为大规模图像处理提供了宝贵的参考和建议，有助于推动相关领域的技术进步。

Abstract: Learnable Representations (LRs) can fit parametric families of functions to diverse kinds of signals. These emergent neural-native data formats are gathering interest in the scientific community due to their compactness, resolution-agnosticism and amenability to direct neural processing. On the other hand, gigapixel images are becoming increasingly widespread across a variety of scientific, cultural, and industrial domains. In this paper, we present GigaBench, the first benchmark and experimental evaluation focused on deploying LRs with gigapixel imagery. GigaBench includes images collected, acquired, and synthetically rendered across diverse domains, and addresses image compression and super-resolution. We adapt a representative set of LRs, spanning Implicit, Hybrid, and Explicit methods, so as to operate reliably at the gigapixel scale on consumer hardware. Through systematic evaluation across reconstruction quality, model size, compression and inference times, we identify consistent trends across methods and suggest actionable guidance for future investigation.

</details>


### [96] [OmniRay: Camera-Agnostic Monocular 3D Reconstruction with Composite Ray Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31645)
*Namitha Guruprasad,Abhay Yadav,Cheng Peng,Rama Chellappa,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniRay 是一种用于单目三维重建的前馈模型，通过组成部分包括单调径向基、球谐残差和自由形式残差，能够适应多种相机几何结构，无需使用相机信息即可从单张图像中恢复 3D 点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究现存单目三维重建方法（如单一pinhole相机假设）在处理宽视角影像（如鱼眼和全景镜头）时的表现欠佳，因此提出了一种新的OmniRay模型。

Method: OmniRay包括一个由单调径向基（捕捉径向对称结构）、球谐残差（处理平滑偏置）和自由形式残差（处理局部变化）组成的Ray Module。这些组件通过在球体上进行连续旋转组成，使表示能够容纳多样的相机几何。

Result: OmniRay能够在各种相机配置下进行无相机数据的单目3D重建，并在不同实验中展示了比之前方法更优越的表现。

Conclusion: OmniRay为相机无关的单目三维重建设定了一种新的标准，展现了对广泛相机几何结构的良好适应性。

Abstract: Monocular models recover 3D geometry from a single image in one pass, but most assume a pinhole camera and produce distorted geometry on the wide-angle imagery produced by fisheye and panoramic lenses. Camera-aware models remove this assumption by predicting a per-pixel ray field. However, they represent different camera geometries using a single fixed basis to capture both the overall camera projection and its finer variations. We argue that camera geometry can be viewed as having a hierarchy of complexity for representing rays. We present OmniRay, a feed-forward model whose Ray Module is composed of three components: a monotone radial base that captures radially symmetric structure, a spherical-harmonic residual for smooth deviations from it, and a free-form residual for remaining local variation. These components are composed as successive rotations on the sphere, allowing the representation to accommodate diverse camera geometries. Given a single image, OmniRay predicts a ray direction and an up-to-scale radial distance for every pixel, from which a 3D point cloud is recovered without needing camera information at inference time. Extensive experiments across different camera configurations demonstrate that OmniRay establishes a new state-of-the-art in camera-agnostic monocular 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [97] [Gate3DGen: A Pre-processing Model that Selectively Purifies Real-World Images for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31649)
*Xiaolin Li,Zihao Huang,Fanpeng Meng,Chen Yang,Guanjun Wu,Kai Zhang,Jiemin Fang,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gate3DGen 是一种选择性预处理模型，能够针对不同类型的输入图像进行加工，以增强 3D 生成的质量，而无需重新训练现有的 3D 生成器。该模型通过合成数据引擎收集监督信息，并在合成基准测试中显著提高了图像质量和下游 3D 几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单张图像 3D 生成技术在现实世界的图像上表现不佳，尤其是在不均匀光照、模糊和遮挡的情况下。Gate3DGen 旨在解决这一问题，通过选择性处理图像输入来提高 3D 生成的质量。

Method: Gate3DGen 设计了一种带有降级感知路由器的结构，能够识别并保留干净输入，同时对需要处理的输入进行净化处理。净化模型通过对降级特征进 行处理，鼓励保持对象身份、轮廓和纹理线索，从而进行单步净化。此外，作者还构建了一个合成数据引擎，以提供配对监督。

Result: Gate3DGen 在合成基准测试上表现出色，平均仅需 2.3 秒即可提高图像和 3D 几何精度，TRELLIS.2 网格距离相对降低 61.5%。在真实照片上的 VLM 评估和人类偏好测试中也表现出对身份保护和降级纠正的支持。

Conclusion: Gate3DGen 是一个有效且高效的解决方案，通过在不需要重新训练 3D 生成器的情况下对图像输入进行处理，提高 3D 生成的质量和精度。

Abstract: Single-image 3D generation has achieved high fidelity on curated benchmarks, yet degrades sharply on real-world images with uneven illumination, blur, and occlusions. Purifying such inputs requires deciding when to intervene and preserving the visual cues needed for 3D generation, while leaving clean inputs unchanged. We introduce Gate3DGen, a selective pre-processing model that gates image inputs to existing 3D generators without retraining them. A degradation-aware router bypasses inputs classified as clean and extracts features to guide purification of the remaining inputs. Conditioned on these features, a 3D-oriented one-step purifier purifies degraded inputs with spatial losses that encourage preservation of object identity, contours, and textural cues. To provide paired supervision, we construct a synthetic data engine that renders clean views of 3D assets and synthesises composite degradations. On the synthetic benchmark, the purifier improves image fidelity and downstream 3D geometry in only 2.3s on average, reducing TRELLIS.2 mesh distance by 61.5% relative to degraded inputs. VLM evaluation and human preferences on real photographs further support identity preservation and degradation correction. More demos and code are available at https://supra-3d.github.io/gate3dgen.

</details>


### [98] [BrickWright: Agentic Creation of Physically Feasible Brick Builds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31651)
*Ava Pun,Kevin Dai,Sally Chen,Maxwell Jones,Ruixuan Liu,Kangle Deng,Changliu Liu,Maneesh Agrawala,Deva Ramanan,Jun-Yan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrickWright 是一个框架，通过编写代码使用 Python 工具 BrickLayer，该工具包含一个优化的积木组装 API 和一个验证物理可行性功能，从文本或图像提示生成物理可行的积木构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决当前前沿编码代理在构建可装配的物理对象时面临的挑战，特别是创建有效的组装（如 LEGO 作品），并确保这些组装符合输入提示。

Method: BrickWright 使用了基于 Python 的工具 BrickLayer，其中包括一个优化的积木组装 API 和一个验证物理可行性的组件。

Result: 使用 BrickWright 引导的代理在 512 个提示样本中实现了 100% 的物理可行性，同时提高了轻量级代理的提示遵从性，而强大的代理保持了原有的水平。此外，BrickWright 比专门为此任务优化的模型表现更好。

Conclusion: BrickWright 为代理提供了创建物理可行、遵循输入提示的积木构建的方法，并展示了其在多个场景中的优越性。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are becoming increasingly adept at 3D modeling. Yet, they still face challenges when building objects meant to be assembled in the physical world, often creating invalid assemblies with floating, colliding, or disconnected parts. We argue that providing agents with tools for assembling builds and verifying their physical feasibility is sufficient for them to produce physically valid assemblies that still adhere to input prompts. Focusing on the domain of LEGO brick builds, we present BrickWright, a framework for generating physically feasible brick builds from text or image prompts using pre-trained agents, where a build is physically feasible if it uses only real parts, has no colliding or floating parts, and forms a connected structure if possible. BrickWright guides agents to create builds by writing code with BrickLayer, a Python toolkit with two main components: an ergonomic brick assembly API that halves inference cost compared to working in raw LDraw, and a validator that provides feedback on physical feasibility. Whereas baseline agents achieve physical validity rates ranging from only 9% (GPT-6 Luna) to 69% (GPT-6 Astra), BrickWright-guided agents achieve 100% validity on a sample of 512 prompts, while improving prompt adherence for lightweight agents and maintaining it for stronger agents. Additionally, they outperform specialized models on the text-to-brick task. Code is available on our project page: https://avalovelace1.github.io/brickwright/

</details>


### [99] [SpectralCanary: Embarrassingly Simple Low-Frequency Traces for T2I Dataset-Use Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31659)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Anirudh Satheesh,Soham Chawathe,Huy Nghiem,Yongkyun Lee,Andrew Adams Schoen,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpectralCanary 通过在每个集合图像中平滑低通掩模下融合固定信号图像，而不更改图像的描述者，提高了数据集使用审计的可验证性，稳健性和部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在验证性、保真度、鲁棒性和部署性之间常常需要做出权衡，因此需要一种新的方法来解决这个问题。

Method: SpectralCanary 使用平滑低通掩模将固定信号图像融合到每个集合图像中，无需生成辅助图像或对每个图像进行优化。

Result: SpectralCanary 在八次针对 Pokemon 数据集和三种现实数据变换的测试中，实现了最高的平均生成的 TPR@1% FPR（91.1%）和 30 生成的固定阈值模型级准确性（94.8%）。在五个涵盖艺术、医学、卫星和时尚领域的数据集中，SpectralCanary 平均实现了 92.4% 的每生成 TPR@1% FPR 的同时保持图像的一致性（SSIM 0.989，LPIPS 0.022），并且数据集间的变化极低（均值 ΔFID 0.05）。在 Pokemon 和 ROCOv2 上进行的能量相等化删除支持了这种低频视觉信号作为有效的数据集级对图像模型审计追踪的有效载体。

Conclusion: 低频视觉信号作为数据集级对图像模型审计追踪的有效载体被证明是有效的。SpectralCanary 在保持数据集的可识别性的同时，提高了数据集使用审计的验证性、鲁棒性和部署性。

Abstract: Auditing whether a text-to-image (T2I) model was fine-tuned on a particular image collection is difficult: a model can absorb collection-level visual characteristics without reproducing training images. Proactive dataset-use auditing plants a shared signal in a dataset before release, then later checks whether that signal appears in a suspect model's generated outputs. Existing approaches often trade off verifiability, fidelity, robustness, and deployability. We introduce $\textbf{SpectralCanary}$, which uses a smooth low-pass mask to blend a fixed canary image into every collection image while leaving captions unchanged and requiring neither auxiliary generation nor per-image optimization. A lightweight verifier detects the transferred signal in suspect-model outputs. Across eight tests on the Pokemon dataset and three realistic data transformations, spanning two architectures families, SpectralCanary achieves the highest average per-generation TPR@$1\%$ FPR ($91.1\%$) and fixed threshold model-level accuracy using 30 generations ($94.8\%$) among the evaluated methods. Across five datasets spanning artistic, medical, satellite, and fashion domains with SD3.5, SpectralCanary averages $92.4\%$ per-generation TPR@$1\%$ FPR while preserving the released images (SSIM $0.989$, LPIPS $0.022$) with a near-zero mean change FID (mean $Δ$FID $0.05$) across datasets. Matched-energy ablations on Pokemon and ROCOv2 support our design choice, low-frequency placement transfers more reliably compared to mid/high-frequency placement. Under the evaluated adaptive attacks, the most effective removal requires aggressive modification and incurs more than $25$ $Δ$FID points. These results identify low-frequency visual signals as effective carriers for collection-level T2I audit traces.

</details>


### [100] [Reading, Writing, Sharing, and Interacting with the World through Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31668)
*Shengqiong Wu,Meng Luo,Bobo Li,Ziwei Liu,Shuicheng Yan,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Philip Torr,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了视频基础模型和视频世界模型的发展，并将其视为一个统一的视角。重点探讨了从观测视频推断世界属性、从指定或学习的世界条件合成视频、整合理解和生成以及预测和更新在行动和不断变化的观察下的世界状态四大操作。文章还追踪了从片段级识别和感知保真度向持久状态、可控制的演变、物理和因果有效性以及基于交互和下游决策的评估的转变，并提炼了随之而来的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在为视频基础模型和视频世界模型的研究提供一个统一的视角，以更好地理解和推动视频领域的发展。

Method: 本文通过将观测视频视为潜在时间世界的时间上部分、失真、时间顺序的投影来组织文献，围绕四大操作进行讨论：阅读、写作、共享和交互。并对这些模型的核心关注点进行了轨迹分析。

Result: 本文总结了从片段级识别和感知保真度向持久状态、可控制的演变、物理和因果有效性以及基于交互和下游决策的评估的转变趋势，并指出了在这些转变中出现的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为视频基础模型和视频世界模型领域的发展提供了一个全面的概述，并提出了许多前沿问题和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Video foundation models have advanced rapidly in understanding and generating visual sequences, while video world models seek to capture the underlying dynamics of the world they depict. Despite their different objectives, both concern the relationship between video observations and an evolving world. This survey adopts world modeling as a unifying lens for examining these developments. Viewing an observed video as a partial, lossy, and temporally ordered projection of a latent temporal world, we organize the literature around four complementary operations: reading infers world properties from observed video; writing synthesizes video from specified or learned world conditions; sharing integrates understanding and generation through common modeling mechanisms; and interacting predicts and updates world states under actions and evolving observations. This perspective distinguishes temporal coherence in observed or generated sequences from consistency in the underlying world state. Across tasks, representations, models, datasets, and benchmarks, we trace a shift in emphasis from clip-level recognition and perceptual fidelity toward persistent state, controllable evolution, physical and causal validity, and evaluation grounded in interaction and downstream decisions, and we distill the open challenges that follow.

</details>


### [101] [Sensorimotor Alignment: A Platonic Proxy for Embodied Policy Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31670)
*Yunfei Xie,Howard H. Qian,Shiyi Lan,Kaiyu Hang,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种低成本的无训练度量$S^2$，用于评估模型的感知-运动相合性，这与下游机器人任务的成功率正相关，可作为选择模型的优选指标，并扩展了Platonic Representation Hypothesis到具身模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过研究机器人基础模型通常通过昂贵的闭环卷出进行评估，寻找一种低成本且无需训练的代替方法。

Method: 提出了Sensorimotor Alignment ($S^2$)，该无训练度量测量模型感知表示与物理运动几何结构的一致性。

Result: $S^2$与具身模型的下游机器人任务成功率正相关，能够在无需精细化调优的情况下识别出有潜力的感知-语言模型。

Conclusion: $S^2$不仅提供了一种衡量具身模型感知-运动相合性的方法，还扩展了Platonic Representation Hypothesis，为理解为何具身模型在闭环评估中取得成功提供了见解。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models are typically evaluated through costly closed-loop rollouts, which motivates cheap offline proxies for model capability. The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) suggests that as representations improve, different modalities such as vision and language converge toward a shared statistical model of reality. We ask whether motion trajectories form another such modality: as embodied models become more capable, should their sensory representations converge toward the structure of physical motion? To examine this, we propose Sensorimotor Alignment ($S^2$), a training-free offline metric that measures how well a model's sensory representation aligns with the geometry of physical motion. We find that $S^2$ correlates positively with downstream robot task success across embodied model families, making it a low-cost offline proxy for prioritizing models before closed-loop evaluation. $S^2$ can also identify promising vision-language model (VLM) backbones even before costly fine-tuning into vision-language-action models (VLAs). Beyond this correlation, $S^2$ offers representation-level explanations for phenomena observed in VLAs and world action models (WAMs). With $S^2$, we extend PRH to embodied models and make sensorimotor alignment empirically measurable, offering insight into why an embodied model succeeds beyond its closed-loop success rate.

</details>


### [102] [A Unified Framework for Image-to-3D Part Generation via Variable Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31671)
*Qiwen Gu,Xin Zhou,Xiwu Chen,Zhongyang Zhu,Zhuo Zhang,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexPart 是一种新型的图像到3D部分生成框架，支持在单一模型内实现不同粒度的几何控制，通过集成多种输入方式和引入非对称几何引导机制，提升了几何一致性与整体网格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D生成方法灵活性不足，无法满足多样化用户流程中的不同控制需求，FlexPart 的引入旨在解决此问题。

Method: FlexPart 通过引入门控自适应调制机制无缝集成稀疏点、密集边界框和掩码等不同类型的输入，并使用非对称几何引导机制平衡不同粒度下的几何表示。

Result: FlexPart 在 PartVerseXL 条件下，相对于 TripoSG + SAM3D 减少了 16.6% 的 Part CD，相对于 OmniPart 减少了 10.5%，同时保持了与 OmniPart 相当的整体网格质量。

Conclusion: FlexPart 为图像到3D部分的生成提供了新的解决方案，提高了生成的精确度和一致性，展示了其在多种场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Controllable 3D part generation is pivotal for digital asset creation, yet existing methods lack the flexibility to accommodate diverse user workflows requiring varying levels of control. In this paper, we introduce FlexPart, a unified image-to-3D part generation framework that supports variable-granularity geometric conditions within a single model. By seamlessly integrating heterogeneous inputs, ranging from sparse points to dense bounding boxes and masks, via gated adaptive modulation, FlexPart unlocks a powerful cross-modal synergy. We also propose an asymmetric geometric guidance mechanism that leverages strong spatial priors to align representations across granularities, improving geometric consistency and facilitating inter-task synergy. Under mask conditioning on PartVerseXL, FlexPart reduces Part CD by 16.6% relative to TripoSG + SAM3D and by 10.5% relative to OmniPart, while maintaining competitive whole-mesh quality. The code can be found at https://github.com/Gucci233/FlexPart

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [baseVultra: Compact Nonlinear Recurrence with Reallocated Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30154)
*Linke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为baseVultra的紧凑型循环单元，它将大部分计算预算集中在单个高阶候选生成矩阵上，用O(H)对角交互替代循环门矩阵，并通过密集分支与两个低秩辅助分支、EMA缓存和可学习输出混合来结合候选更新；门还通过对角项接收轻量级的h_{t-2}二级访问。在TinyShakespeare字符级别语言建模中，baseVultra在紧凑的一层200K设置下的验证损失为1.594，与参数匹配的LSTM（1.604）和参数匹配的GRU（1.616）竞争，且其密集的矩阵向量乘法计算量约为GRU的54%。在text8基准上，baseVultra与全门单元竞争（1.854 bpc vs. GRU 1.849和LSTM 1.837）。在形式语言探测中，当隐藏状态容量受限时（H=16时 Counter OOD2精度0.991 vs. GRU 0.761和LSTM 0.691），受益最大，但随着隐藏尺寸增长，这种优势消失。研究结果表明，当空间受限时，候选导向的循环计算重新分配有益，而全门单元在更大规模下依然具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索循环计算是否可以从门变换重新分配给候选生成，以提高紧凑型循环神经网络（RNN）的性能。针对传统的LSTM和GRU模型中，门机制占据了大部分计算资源的现状，提出了一种更加专注于候选生成的机制。

Method: 在现有的循环神经网络（RNN）的基础上，设计了一种名为baseVultra的紧凑型循环单元，实现方式包括：将循环计算预算集中在H x H的单个候选生成矩阵上；用O(H)的对角交互替代循环中的门矩阵；候选更新包括与两个低秩辅助分支、EMA缓存和可学习输出混合的密集分支；门还通过对角项接收轻量级的h_{t-2}的二级访问。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare字符级别的语言建模中，baselineVultra在参数匹配LSTM(1.604)和GRU(1.616)的基础上，验证损失为1.594，而其密集矩阵向量乘法计算量约为GRU的54%。在更大规模的text8基准上，baselineVultra实现了1.854 bpc的性能，与全门单元的GRU(1.849)和LSTM(1.837)接近。在形式语言探测任务中，受到隐藏状态容量限制时，基本vultra显示出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过这种方式重新分配循环计算资源，可以在某些具体任务和场景中提高紧凑型循环神经网络的性能。不过，在更大规模的场景中，全门单元仍然有竞争力。

Abstract: Nonlinear recurrent networks remain useful for streaming sequence modeling, yet gated cell design is still dominated by LSTM and GRU. We study whether recurrent computation can be reallocated away from gate transforms and toward candidate generation. We propose baseVultra, a compact recurrent cell that concentrates the dense recurrent budget on a single H x H candidate-generation matrix, while replacing recurrent gate matrices with O(H) diagonal interactions. The candidate update combines this dense branch with two low-rank auxiliary branches, an EMA cache, and learnable output blending; the gates additionally receive lightweight second-order access to h_{t-2} through diagonal terms. On TinyShakespeare character-level language modeling, baseVultra achieves a validation loss of 1.594 in the compact one-layer 200K setting, compared with 1.604 for a parameter-matched LSTM and 1.616 for a parameter-matched GRU, while its dense recurrent matrix-vector computation is about 54% of GRU under matched hidden size for this configuration. On text8, a roughly 45x larger benchmark, baseVultra is competitive with full-gate cells (1.854 bpc vs. 1.849 for GRU and 1.837 for LSTM). On formal-language probes, the largest gains appear under constrained hidden-state capacity (0.991 vs. 0.761 GRU and 0.691 LSTM OOD2 accuracy on Counter at H=16) and vanish as hidden size grows. These results characterize where and when candidate-focused recurrent capacity allocation helps: the benefit is concentrated in compact regimes, while full-gate cells remain competitive at scale.

</details>


### [104] [Lighthouse: On Building a Trustworthy Digital Assistant for Exploring History with Civil Rights Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30158)
*John A. Aydin,Angelica Williams,Steven Dindl,Nathan Saunders,Kate Boyd,Vandana Srivastava,Biplav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Lighthouse的数字助理，它是首个用于探究美国民权运动时期的聊天机器人，基于神经符号方法，重点关注内容的可信度和减少潜在伤害。它通过一个大型语言模型进行神经学习，并结合规则的符号解算，以平衡答案风险与覆盖面的广度。该系统通过事实客观问题和主观问题进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI技术的发展，为了更高效和有效地研究历史，特别是通过多模态数据如文本、图像和音频，本文提出了Lighthouse聊天机器人来探究美国的民权运动时期的数据资料。其创新在于强调内容的可信度和减少潜在的用户伤害。

Method: Lighthouse采用了神经符号方法，通过大型语言模型进行神经学习，结合规则的符号解算，平衡了生成的回答风险和覆盖的广度。

Result: Lighthouse已被用于实际的评估，包括事实性客观问题和主观问题。同时，研究人员创建了一个包含专家答案的主观问题-答案对数据集作为参考。

Conclusion: 通过多模态数据和新颖的设计原则，Lighthouse展示了在数字助手领域对探究历史问题的新途径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI), as a decision-support aid, provides new tools for studying history effectively using multimodal data, including text, images, and audio. We present, to our knowledge, the first digital assistant named Lighthouse--commonly called a chatbot--for exploring the Civil Rights period of the United States using data from South Carolina. From an AI perspective, the system's novelty lies in its guiding design principles of (a) focusing on trustworthiness of content and (b) by modeling and reducing potential harm to users from generated answers. It implements these with a neuro-symbolic approach that combines neural learning through a large language model (LLM) with rule-based symbolic solving, balancing risk of answers with generality of coverage. We evaluate Lighthouse with both factual objective questions that admit a single acceptable answer as well as subjective questions. For the latter, a dataset of subjective question-answer pairs (QAs) is created by discovering a "gold standard" by aggregating answers from independent experts. Lighthouse has access to over 18k primary sources from a range of collections and can be easily extended.

</details>


### [105] [TauIndianBankBench: Procedure-Aware, Action-Oriented Evaluation of Tool-Using Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30161)
*Krishanu Adhikary,Prashant Devadiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了TauIndianBankBench，这是一个针对印度零售银行任务的动作导向基准，包含1000个任务，评估助理是否执行了所有必要步骤和未做不必要的更改，这比仅评估最终记录提供了更准确的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的基准测试主要基于最终记录，无法准确评估助手是否遵循了所有必要步骤和未做不必要的更改，因此需要一个更精准的评估方式。

Method: 开发了一个名为TauIndianBankBench的动作导向基准，其中包括1000个印度零售银行业务，对每一步骤是否完成以及是否存在额外更改进行评估。

Result: 当仅评估最终记录时，6.5%到12.0%的会话被认为成功，但实际上助手可能忽略了必要步骤或进行了不必要的更改。采用TauIndianBankBench后，能更准确地反映语言模型的表现。

Conclusion: TauIndianBankBench提高了对基于语言模型的银行助手的评估精度，有助于改进和优化这些助手以更好地服务客户。

Abstract: Many Indian banks serve customers through FAQ pages and scripted chatbots. Assistants based on language models could respond faster and carry out requests such as blocking a card, but only if they give correct information and follow the bank's rules. These rules prescribe a procedure, a set of required steps, such as obtaining a closure quote before closing a deposit. Adapting models to this domain requires measuring how well they follow the procedure, before and after training. Benchmarks usually judge only the records left at the end of a conversation. Many required steps, however, leave the records unchanged, so an assistant that skips them can still be judged successful. We present TauIndianBankBench, an action-oriented benchmark of 1,000 Indian retail-banking tasks where an assistant helps a simulated customer. Besides the final records, it checks that the assistant took every required step and made no unrequested change. For every model, 6.5-12.0% of sessions are judged successful when only the final records are checked, although the assistant skipped a required step or made an unrequested change. Scoring both procedure and outcome shows more accurately how well language models perform in Indian retail banking.

</details>


### [106] [FusionEval: Benchmarking Model Fusion for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30162)
*Shuo Cai,Yanggan Gu,Yuhang Liu,Su Lu,Yuanyi Wang,Yang Yu,Zhen Li,Zhitian Hou,Guanghao Zhu,Ming Li,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FusionEval 是一个基准，旨在整合多专家模型的能力，同时控制专家池，评估融合方法在不同工具使用和推理性能上的表现，发现无训练方法能保持推理性能但工具使用大幅下降，而 MOPD 能够保留工具使用并平衡能力，但需要额外的数据准备和训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型融合评估方法覆盖面有限，无法全面评估多专家模型在推理长链条思考和工具使用的具体表现，且平均表现良好的模型可能隐藏了部分专家能力的损失。FusionEval 引入了一个新的基准，旨在全面评估模型融合方法在保持各专家能力的同时，减少额外的计算资源消耗。

Method: FusionEval 提供了独立训练的专家池，不同模型规模在八个公开基准下进行统一的评估，包括能力保留、计算成本、运行时间和峰值 GPU 内存使用等指标。研究采用了训练型和非训练型融合方法，研究了它们在不同任务上的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，无训练方法在推理能力上保持良好，但在工具使用上显著下降；而 MOPD 在保留工具使用方面表现更佳，但在计算成本上有所增加。因此，FusionEval 成功展示了在低计算成本下同时保持推理、指令跟随和工具使用的挑战依然存在。

Conclusion: FusionEval 为未来研究提供了一个全面的基准，以高效地保留专家模型的能力。研究团队已开源了此工具以便未来的研究使用。

Abstract: Model fusion aims to integrate the capabilities of multi-expert models into a single model. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage of reasoning with long chains of thought and tool use, while strong average performance can mask substantial losses in individual expert capabilities. We introduce FusionEval, a benchmark that provides controlled expert pools, integrates fusion methods with and without additional training, and offers a unified evaluation suite. The pools contain domain experts independently trained from Qwen3 and Llama base models across math, code, science, instruction following, and tool use. The suite evaluates capability retention and computational cost on eight public benchmarks under comparable data access rules, reporting normalized average and worst performance, runtime, and peak GPU memory usage. Extensive experiments show that training-free methods preserve reasoning performance but substantially degrade tool use at both model scales. In contrast, MOPD preserves tool use and achieves a more balanced capability profile, but requires additional data preparation, inference, and training. Jointly retaining reasoning, instruction following, and tool use at low computational cost remains a challenge. We hope FusionEval supports future research on efficient model fusion that jointly preserves expert capabilities. Our code is available at https://github.com/Baicaihaochi/FusionEval_Benchmark.

</details>


### [107] [Measuring Research Progress and Linguistic Inclusion in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30296)
*Atnafu Lambebo Tonja,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Jesujoba Oluwadara Alabi,Israel Abebe Azime,Benjamin Rosman,Ehsan Shareghi,Thamar Solorio,Pontus Stenetorp,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了2020年至2025年间发表的1158篇关于低资源语言和多语言NLP的论文，发现尽管出版物数量增长了5.4倍，但主要集中在已代表的语言上，而未充分扩展低资源语言的覆盖范围。提出了一种语言包容性框架（LIF），涵盖研究增长、代表扩展、代表连续性和分布公正四个维度，结果显示尽管研究增长，但各地区在语言包容性方面仍有待改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型在低资源语言和多语言NLP领域的研究现状与挑战，并提出评估语言包容性的框架方法。

Method: 收集和分析过去五年间发表的相关论文，通过自定义的LIF框架，从多个维度评估语言包容性。
