Group verification checks whether bank partitions induced by the recovered masks agree with observed timing behavior. Single function verification removes one addressing function at a time and measures the resulting change in verification pass rate. Unlike approaches that return only a single inferred mapping, our workflow systematically records, for each candidate threshold, the solved bank masks and validation metrics. This pipeline supports more robust selection of bank masks on ARM-based embedded devices and provides an efficient method for reverse engineering DRAM address mapping functions and subsequent Rowhammer analysis.

</details>


### [181] [Proba: A High-Performance, Low-Traffic Probabilistic Spatial Memory Streaming Prefetcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30448)
*Yinting Huang,Jacky Wong,Sam Ainsworth*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Proba是一种基于反馈的Spatial Memory Streaming (SMS) 预取器，它通过结合概率模式更新、边缘准确率决策和多个触发特征的模式积累，实现了精细的准确性和覆盖率之间的权衡。当准确度下降时，Proba会降低其激进程度以优先考虑准确的预取；当准确度提高时，它会扩大覆盖范围。Proba在节省DRAM带宽方面比其他SMS预取器更有效，同时在多核心和DRAM受限场景中表现出色，而不会损害单线程吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SMS预取器存在控制粗略且适应动态程序行为和历史模式的能力有限的问题。因此，提出了Proba，旨在通过引入概率模式更新、边缘准确率决策和模式积累机制，提供更加精细化的准确性和覆盖率的权衡。

Method: Proba通过结合概率模式更新、边缘准确率决策和模式积累机制，动态调整预取策略，以适应不同的动态程序行为和历史模式。

Result: Proba在大多数情况下都能实现比其他SMS预取器更高的性能增益，同时在DRAM带宽使用上更为高效。特别是在多核心和DRAM受限的场景中，Proba表现出了比其他技术更为出色的整体性能，而不会牺牲单线程的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Proba通过实现精细的准确性和覆盖间的权衡，以及根据实际性能动态调整预取策略，展示了在不同应用场景下与现有SMS预取器相比的优势。

Abstract: Spatial Memory Streaming (SMS) prefetchers exploit recurring spatial access patterns across memory regions to predict future accesses. Despite their effectiveness, existing SMS-based designs suffer from coarse control over aggressiveness and limited adaptability to dynamic program behavior and historical patterns. In this work, we present Proba, a feedback-directed SMS-based prefetcher that integrates probabilistic pattern updates, marginal accuracy decision-making, and pattern accumulation over several trigger features to achieve fine-grained accuracy-coverage trade-offs. When accuracy deteriorates, Proba moderates its aggressiveness to prioritize accurate prefetches; conversely, when accuracy rises, it expands its coverage. Proba achieves higher speedups than any other SMS-based prefetcher while being vastly more efficient in DRAM traffic. This culminates in a large performance gap between Proba and other techniques in multicore and DRAM-constrained scenarios without harming single-threaded throughput.

</details>


### [182] [FIGFLEX: A Mixed-Precision FP-INT GEMM Accelerator Co-Designed with Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30480)
*Faraz Tahmasebi,Haocheng Xu,Andrew Trauger,Yomal Perera,Zhenyu Tang,Nael Chowdhury,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FIGFLEX的算法与硬件协同设计的混合精度加速器，它通过位并行数据路径将吞吐量与精度下降成比例地增加。该加速器采用了一种位并行的变换算法，能够在精度较低时节省计算量，同时通过量化策略确保精度不损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的量化技术在平均比特数达到2-3位时无法充分利用硬件加速器的性能，因为现有硬件要么在矩阵乘法前需将权重转换为FP16，要么只能支持单一比特精度的矩阵乘法。因此，虽然减少了内存使用，但并未节省计算成本。本文通过算法和硬件协同设计，旨在克服现有技术的限制，提升低比特量化下模型的性能。

Method: 本文提出了一种混合精度量化算法，结合了位并行的设计方法，并对浮点-整数乘法进行了优化。具体来说，使用位并行数据路径来根据精度变化调整吞吐量，并提出了一种新的INT3优化方法来利用数据路径资源。同时，设计了一个针对每个通道组分配不同比特宽度的量化器，并引入了一种归一化方案来保持放大后的激活值在FP16的常规范围内。

Result: 在70亿参数的LLaMA-2模型上，图FLEX在3位和2位平均精度下分别达到2.65和1.80的WikiText-2困惑度，优于SliM-LLM、AMQ等其他技术。此外，与AxCore和FIGLUT相比，在3位混合精度模型的线性矩阵乘法上，该加速器实现了每单位面积1.56倍和1.42倍的峰值吞吐量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的图FLEX算法与硬件协同设计的混合精度加速器能够在保持精度的同时，显著提升低比特量化的模型性能，尤其是对于大量参数的LLM模型。

Abstract: Weight-only quantization keeps LLM activations in floating point and stores weights as low-bit integers, turning the dominant workload into floating-point-integer (FP-INT) general matrix multiplication. Recent quantizers reach a 2-3-bit average with mixed precision, but hardware has not kept pace: GPUs dequantize weights to FP16 before the tensor-core multiply-accumulate, and bit-parallel FP-INT accelerators run at a single weight precision, so fewer bits save memory but not computation. In this paper, we present FIGFLEX, an algorithm-hardware co-designed mixed-precision accelerator whose bit-parallel datapath scales throughput as precision falls. FIGFLEX contributes (1) a shift-add, radix-4 reformulation of FP-INT multiplication that fuses the multiply-accumulate into a unified, precision-flexible partial-product reduction and roughly halves the partial products, together with a novel INT3 optimization that reclaims the idle datapath radix-4 leaves at that precision; and (2) a mixed-precision quantizer that assigns each channel group its bit-width according to the error GPTQ cannot compensate, with a per-input-channel column scaling whose power-of-two normalization keeps the scaled activations out of the FP16 subnormal range, so that the quantized model maps directly onto FIGFLEX's datapath with the same accuracy as on a GPU. On LLaMA-2-7B, FIGFLEX attains 5.85 and 8.77 WikiText-2 perplexity at 3- and 2-bit average precision, lower than GPTQ, AWQ, SliM-LLM, AMQ, and QuIP# (without fine-tuning), while delivering 1.56x and 1.42x higher whole-chip throughput per area on the linear GEMMs of a 3-bit mixed-precision model than AxCore and FIGLUT, respectively.

</details>


### [183] [HDLouvain: High-Throughput Incremental Clustering for Hyperdimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30687)
*Beenish Gul,Mahbod Afarin,Farhat Ahmed,Sumukh Pinge,Tsung-Han Lu,Tajana Rosing,Kevin Skadron*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HDLouvain 使用 Louvain 社区检测方法结合超维计算的位并行相似度计算，提高大规模流式数据的聚类效率和质量，通过调整抽样比例展示了一个持续的吞吐量-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代shotgun质谱组学产生大量数据，传统聚类方法如DBSCAN和HAC性能有限，因此需要开发一种能够在大规模流式数据中高效且高质量进行聚类的新方法。

Method: 开发了HDLouvain，该方法将Louvain社区检测与超维计算的位并行相似度计算相结合，通过样本预处理、局部Louvain聚类、社区凝聚、新谱图索引和新聚类生成等步骤来实现。

Result: HDLouvain 在五个不同规模的PRIDE数据集上表现良好，调整抽样比例展示了清晰的吞吐量-保真度权衡。在PXD003258数据集上，随着调整比例的增加，吞吐量和质量逐步优化。

Conclusion: HDLouvain 提供了一种在高通量流式谱图数据中实现高效聚类的方法，对于提高大规模数据分析的效率和准确性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Modern shotgun proteomics generates tens of thousands of tandem mass spectra per hour, producing hundreds of gigabytes of data that strain conventional clustering pipelines. DBSCAN and hierarchical agglomerative clustering (HAC) offer flexible similarity search, but DBSCAN is unstable across varying data densities, HAC costs $O(n^2)$, and both yield poor cluster quality. Graph-based methods such as Louvain produce high-quality clusters but scale poorly on streaming data. We observe that Louvain's poor scalability can be resolved without sacrificing its quality, and present HDLouvain, which combines Louvain community detection with the bit-parallel similarity computation of hyperdimensional computing (HDC). HDLouvain partitions a bootstrap fraction of the stream into precursor-mass buckets, applies Louvain to each bucket's kNN similarity graph, and condenses each community into a single binary hypervector by bitwise-majority bundling rather than iterative centroid recomputation. New spectra are assigned by a GPU-accelerated Hamming distance lookup against representatives in their mass bucket, or seed a new cluster when no match meets a similarity threshold $τ$, so the partition grows incrementally without full re-clustering. On five PRIDE datasets (0.99M to 21.13M spectra), sweeping the bootstrap fraction $α$ from 0.2 to 0.9 exposes an explicit, monotonic throughput-fidelity trade-off. On PXD003258, speedup ranges from 4.5x at $α=0.3$ to 1.65x at $α=0.7$. Modularity rises from 0.75-0.76 at $α=0.2$ to 0.95-0.97 at $α=0.9$, never more than 8% below full static recomputation. The Louvain stage achieves 0.86-0.93 normalized mutual information (NMI) on LFR benchmark networks with 30k to 2M nodes, whereas DBSCAN and HAC degrade to 0.57.

</details>


### [184] [SnakeCharmer: Orchestrating Utility, Timeliness, and Placement in GPU Prefetching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30990)
*Yuhan Tang,Tiejun Li,Jianmin Zhang,An Hu,Guangyu Sun,Sheng Ma,Yiqun Lang,Hanqing Li,Siqing Fu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为SnakeCharmer的统一后端预取控制框架，用于基于地址预测的GPU预取器。SnakeCharmer分离了地址预测和预取实现，通过零值感知过滤、分层反馈驱动的距离调整和寄存器辅助预取缓冲来分别控制效用、及时性和放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于预测的预取只有在对性能有益、及时到达且不会造成有害干扰时才会产生效益，因此单个维度的优化无法保证性能提升。为了解决这个问题，研究人员提出了一种新的统一后端预取控制框架。

Method: 研究人员通过分离前端预测和后端控制路径，利用零值感知过滤、分层反馈驱动的距离调整和寄存器辅助预取缓冲来分别控制预取的效用、及时性和放置。

Result: 在A100 GPU模型上，相较于Snake，SnakeCharmer提升了12.8%的性能和20.8%的能量效率；相较于APOGEE，它提升了1.7%的性能和3.2%的能量效率，且硬件开销较小。

Conclusion: 该研究的SnakeCharmer通过一套新颖的后端控制策略，显著提高了基于地址预测的GPU预取器的性能和能效，展示了其在多个前端预测器上的广泛适用性。

Abstract: GPUs rely on massive thread-level parallelism to tolerate long memory latency, yet they still suffer severe stalls when many warps simultaneously wait for data from the memory system. Address-prediction-based GPU prefetchers can mitigate such stalls, but accurate prediction alone does not guarantee performance improvement: a predicted prefetch becomes beneficial only if it is worth issuing, arrives at the right time, and is retained without harmful interference. Because these requirements interact, optimizing any single dimension in isolation can simply move the bottleneck to another.
  We capture these requirements with a predicted-prefetch-to-realized-benefit perspective and propose SnakeCharmer, a unified backend prefetch-control framework for address-prediction-based GPU prefetchers. SnakeCharmer separates address prediction from prefetch realization: the frontend predictor generates prefetch candidates, while the backend control path jointly controls utility, timeliness, and placement by determining whether each candidate should be issued, when it should be issued, and where the returned data should be retained. SnakeCharmer realizes this control path with zero-value-aware filtering for utility, hierarchical feedback-driven distance tuning for timeliness, and a register-assisted prefetch buffer for placement. We design and implement SnakeCharmer primarily on top of Snake, and adapt the same backend-control principles to APOGEE to demonstrate that SnakeCharmer's benefits are not limited to a single frontend predictor. On an A100 GPU model, SnakeCharmer improves performance/energy efficiency by 12.8%/20.8% over Snake and by 1.7%/3.2% over APOGEE, with modest hardware overhead.

</details>


### [185] [Ray-by-Ray: Fine-Grained Resource Management for GPU Ray Tracing Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31630)
*Junrui Pan,Cesar Avalos Baddouh,Kai Ze Ee,Timothy G. Rogers*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该工作通过提出Ray-by-Ray方法，将片上缓冲区分解到单个光束的粒度，并引入轻量级的管理结构，从而提高了光线追踪单元的利用率和内存级别的并行度，实验证明平均性能提高了1.91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的光线追踪技术由于硬件与SIMT模型之间的不匹配而存在效率低下问题，特别是在光束处理和资源共享上。

Method: Ray-by-Ray方法将片上缓冲区分解到单个光束，并引入轻量级管理结构，允许多个CUDA线程块并行运行。

Result: Ray-by-Ray方法能够提高光线追踪单元的利用率，并增加内存级别的并行度，实验结果显示平均性能提高了1.91倍。

Conclusion: 该方法通过优化资源管理，解决了光线追踪技术中SIMT不匹配的问题，为提高光线追踪性能提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Ray tracing (RT) generates photorealistic images by simulating the physical behavior of light as it interacts with surfaces in a virtual scene. In film production and high-end rendering pipelines, this technique computes global illumination, reflections, refractions, and soft shadows by tracing millions of light rays per frame and accurately modeling their bounces and attenuation. Contemporary GPUs incorporate specialized ray tracing hardware to accelerate key operations in the traversal pipeline. By accelerating these stages in hardware, the unit enables real-time ray tracing for complex scenes that previously required offline computation. However, ray tracing introduces substantial control and data divergence. Each ray may traverse a different set of nodes in the acceleration structure, encounter distinct materials, or terminate at varying depths. This irregularity reduces thread coherence and exposes a fundamental disparity between the GPU SIMT and per-thread execution model of dedicated RT units, where each ray progresses independently.
  In this work, we seek to validate assumptions made in open-source infrastructure and identify potential inefficiencies in resource management within the ray tracing pipeline. To address inefficiency caused by mismatches between SIMT and RT granularities, we propose Ray-by-Ray, which decomposes on-chip buffers into the granularity of individual rays and introduces lightweight management structures that enable more warps to run in parallel without increasing buffer capacity. This approach improves ray tracing unit utilization and increases memory-level parallelism. Experimental results show that Ray-by-Ray achieves an average performance improvement of 1.91x over the baseline, with an area overhead of 0.19\%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [The Cost of Saying Hello: An Empirical Study of Handshake Overhead in AI Agent Communication Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30151)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过使用每种协议的官方SDK进行实验，对MCP（2025 initialize和2026 discover时代）、A2A和ACP三种主要代理协议的握手过程进行了详尽的基准测量。结果显示，握手过程中的往返次数和所需时间是影响效率的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于代理通信协议的现有调研中都没有涉及关于握手过程效率的具体测量，本文填补了这一空白，对多种协议的握手过程进行了实证研究，以期优化AI代理之间的通信效率。

Method: 通过使用每种协议的官方SDK，对每种协议在冷启动、重复连接、能力不匹配和元数据受损等不同场景下的握手过程进行了详细测量。记录每个阶段的延迟、往返次数、传输字节数和令牌成本。

Result: 实验结果显示，往返次数对握手成本的影响最大。MCP在50ms延时下的握手成本为230毫秒，包含3次往返；A2A则只需要一次往返，成本为61毫秒。此外，代理读取对方元数据的开销与对方能力库存的大小成线性关系，每种能力需要24-107个令牌。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结果表明，优化握手协议可以显著提高AI代理间的通信效率，并且MCP的2026版本的改进验证了这些结论。文章已发布相关代码和数据汇总在GitHub上。

Abstract: Every interaction between AI agents begins with a handshake: the initiating agent must locate its counterpart, learn what it can do, and establish a session before the first useful message can be sent. Surveys of agent communication protocols name handshake overhead as an efficiency criterion, yet none report measurements. This paper presents the first empirical, phase-decomposed benchmark of the handshake in three major agent protocols: MCP (in both its 2025 initialize and 2026 discover eras), A2A, and ACP. Using each protocol's official SDK, we instrument every request for round trips, per-phase latency, wire bytes, and token cost across cold-start, warm-repeat, capability-mismatch, and degraded-metadata scenarios (N=50 per cell, on loopback and under an emulated 50 ms RTT). Round-trip count dominates handshake cost. A legacy MCP handshake takes three round trips and 230 ms at 50 ms RTT; A2A needs one round trip and 61.0 ms. Round trips are not the whole bill: when an LLM reads the counterpart's metadata, handshake cost scales with the counterpart's capability inventory at 24-107 tokens per capability, so a 200-capability counterpart spends 11% of a 200k-token context window before the agent reads its own task. MCP's own 2026 revision cuts its handshake from three round trips to two and, with era pinning, to zero on warm reconnects, protocol evolution that corroborates the cost we measure. We release the full harness, raw data, and analysis at https://github.com/pvarshh/handshake-benchmark.

</details>


### [187] [MCP vs CLI for AI Agents: A Comparative Analysis of Tool Integration Strategies for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30157)
*Arun Malik,Nitish Talasu,Bradley Klemick,Vineet Tushar Trivedi,Prachi Shah,Sanay Devi,Junjie Zhang,Kunal Sonawane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对比了CLI和MCP两种接口协议，涉及14个评估维度，展示了MCP在细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离等方面的优势，并通过渐进披露技术缓解了MCP的性能问题，推荐混合架构用于生产系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于AI代理需与外部系统交互，本文旨在对比分析两种接口协议CLI（命令行界面）和MCP（模型上下文协议），旨在找出各自的优缺点，并为生产系统提供合理的架构建议。

Method: 通过实证分析、安全评估和经济效益建模等方法，在14个维度上全面对比两种协议。包括但不限于效率、安全性、经济成本等方面。

Result: 研究发现CLI在单用户本地工作流中效率更高，而MCP在细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计和多租户管理等方面具有优势。通过渐进披露技术，MCP在效率上接近CLI，但仍能保持其架构优势。

Conclusion: 推荐使用混合架构来结合CLI和MCP的优点，以构建更高效的生产系统。

Abstract: AI agents require interfaces to interact with external systems such as files, APIs, databases, and cloud services. Two competing paradigms have emerged: CLI (Command Line Interface), where agents execute shell commands directly, and MCP (Model Context Protocol), a structured JSON-RPC protocol providing governed, typed tool access. This paper presents a comparative analysis across 14 evaluation dimensions, supported by worked examples, security assessment against established compliance frameworks, and economic modeling. Our analysis indicates that CLI provides superior efficiency for single-user, local-only workflows, while MCP addresses requirements that CLI fundamentally cannot satisfy, including fine-grained access control, credential isolation, structured audit, and multi-tenant governance. We further examine how progressive disclosure techniques eliminate MCP's historical weakness (context window overhead), yielding token efficiency comparable to CLI while preserving its architectural benefits. A hybrid architecture is recommended for production systems. This analysis draws upon academic, government, and industry sources including NSA formal guidance (CSI U/OO/6030316-26), OWASP security standards, and economic modeling from BCG and Gartner.

</details>


### [188] [Accelerating Wildfire Hazard Mapping Across Canada with an AI Surrogate Modelling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30170)
*Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Chirag Goel,Jana Osea,Aldo Zaimi,Tom Swystun,Sandy Erni,Francisco Emiliano Lopez Saavedra,Yan Boulanger,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种标准化框架，通过多任务深度学习模型来替代基于过程的野火模拟器，实现了野火燃烧概率、平均强度和蔓延速度的快速预测，同时保持了下游灾害分类和物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大频繁的野火活动需要频繁进行野火风险地图绘制以指导预防性减灾措施，当前的方法耗时较长，新的模型旨在提高效率。

Method: 研究通过结合AI研究人员和野火模拟专家的力量，开发了一个标准化的框架，包括训练、微调和评估AI替代模型的步骤，以及一个多任务深度学习模型，该模型经过大量国家数据训练，能够快速模拟野火的燃烧概率、平均强度和蔓延速度。

Result: 模型在多个指标上表现良好，包括一致性、相关性、误差和空间重叠，能够维护下游的灾害分类，并对专家提出的假设性干预提供物理上合理的反应。与传统方法相比，该模型在代表性的区域内实现了806倍的CPU加速，并使用了BurnP3+主机内存的13%。

Conclusion: 研究成功展示了多任务深度学习模型在野火风险评估中的应用，为野火管理提供了更快速和可靠的工具。

Abstract: Canada's increasing wildfire activity requires frequent national hazard mapping to guide proactive mitigation, including fuel management, land-use planning, infrastructure protection, and evacuation routing. However, process-based wildfire simulation frameworks (e.g., BurnP3 and BurnP3+) rely on computationally intensive Monte Carlo simulations to model burn probability, fire intensity, and rate of spread. These workflows can take days to weeks to process a single regional modelling unit. To overcome these computational bottlenecks, we present three contributions developed through collaboration between AI researchers and wildfire modelling experts: (a) a standardized framework for training, fine-tuning, and evaluating AI surrogate models against process-based wildfire simulators; (b) a multi-task deep learning surrogate trained on national-scale data to emulate burn probability, mean fire intensity, and mean rate of spread at a fraction of the computational cost (seconds); and (c) domain-specific metrics and counterfactual evaluation protocols to probe out-of-distribution generalization. Integrating geospatial data (fuel, terrain, ignition likelihood) and regional weather, our model approximates BurnP3+ simulation outputs, achieving strong performance across agreement, rank-correlation, error, and spatial-overlap metrics. It preserves downstream hazard classifications and produces physically plausible responses to expert-informed counterfactual interventions. On a representative region, the surrogate achieves 806-fold end-to-end CPU acceleration and uses 13% of BurnP3+'s host memory, facilitating rapid scenario analysis.

</details>


### [189] [Neural Networks with Differentiable Belief-Function Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30173)
*Antonin Sulc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Yager规则结合证据，构建了一种深度证据管道，使得模型在完全未见过注释者分布的情况下，仍能有效传达不确定性信号，并在模态检测和分布外检测中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Yager规则和Dempster规则在高冲突环境下不同表现的启发，作者在深度学习框架中采用Yager规则来结合来自不同来源的信息，同时使用模型输出的未知概率作为模型对某一类别的不确定性的表示。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于Yager规则的深度证据管道，从网络头部到输出的每个概率都是有效的Dempster-Shafer概率函数，最终通过Yager规则进行组合。输出对未知类别的概率被用作模型的“我不知道”信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在两个模态（视觉和语言）中准确地反映了人类的分歧，并且在分布外检测中表现与强大的单次基线相当。相比之下，Dempster规则需要额外的归一化处理才能直接用于不确定性的表示。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新的方法来使用不确定性的表示提升深度学习模型的鲁棒性和解释性，通过Yager规则的使用，实现了在未见过的数据上的可解释性表现。

Abstract: Yager's 1987 rule of combination $\boxplus$ was proposed on epistemic grounds: under high conflict, mass should be redistributed to ignorance rather than renormalised onto the class simplex. We observe that backpropagation needs the same rule: Dempster's normaliser $(1{-}κ)^{-1}$, where $κ$ is the conflict mass, puts a $(1{-}κ)^{-2}$ term in the gradient that diverges exactly when evidence sources disagree, while Yager's $\boxplus$ is bilinear and has no such term. Building on this, we construct a deep evidential pipeline in which every mass in the chain from heads to output is a valid Dempster--Shafer mass function, combined by Yager's rule. The output mass on the ignorance set, $m(Θ)$, serves as the network's ``I do not know'' signal. The network is fit to hard labels and never sees an annotator distribution, yet $m(Θ)$ orders human disagreement in two modalities, visual and linguistic. Dempster's rises too and ranks contested inputs about as well, but its normaliser divides the conflict out: Yager's vacuity is $9\times$ larger on the most contested CIFAR-10H images, so only Yager's is readable as an amount of ignorance without recalibration. For out-of-distribution detection it is competitive with strong single-pass baselines, while the clamped Dempster variant that scores higher becomes gradient-unstable once the clamp is removed.

</details>


### [190] [When Stable and Preferred Semantics Agree: A Path-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30176)
*Yan Zhang,Kehan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在抽象论辩框架下，偏好和稳定语义一致的结构条件，引入了一般路径基准则，该准则严格推广了文献中的主要结构准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究论辩框架中偏好和稳定语义的一致性条件，以解决不确定性和复杂性问题。

Method: 通过选择路径集和诱导的二元关系，建立路径基准则，并通过某些路径集进行了实例化。

Result: 提出了一般路径基准则，并通过某些路径集得到若干充分条件。这些充分条件严格推广了文献中的主要结构准则。

Conclusion: 结果适用于有限和无限论辩框架，并在有限情况下也有简化表述，同时也为稳定扩展的存在性提供了充分条件。

Abstract: We investigate structural conditions under which preferred and stable semantics agree in abstract argumentation frameworks. Our approach is based on suitably chosen sets of paths in the attack graph and binary relations induced by them. We establish a general path-based criterion for semantic agreement and instantiate it with several classes of paths, obtaining sufficient conditions expressed in terms of well-foundedness of the corresponding relations.
  The resulting conditions strictly generalize two principal structural criteria for preferred and stable agreement in the literature: Dung's limited controversiality condition and the SCC-symmetry condition. In particular, Dung's result is recovered as a special case by taking the relevant path set to be the set of even paths. We further introduce two infinite sequences of path sets, each yielding a family of agreement conditions. For the first sequence, a Collapse Theorem reduces the infinitely many associated well-foundedness conditions to three essentially distinct ones, whereas the second sequence gives rise to genuinely distinct conditions with no analogous collapse. Our results apply to both finite and infinite argumentation frameworks, admit simplified formulations in the finite case, and also yield sufficient conditions for the existence of stable extensions.

</details>


### [191] [Feedback That Backfires in Games: Do LLM Players Repeat the Move They Just Watched Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30205)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 小模型在代理行动失败时记录失败信息，可能导致他们更容易重复失败的动作，特别是在非法动作上，而在损失动作上则无明显影响。记录的影响大小取决于模型类型和训练阶段，且可以通过修改描述方式减轻此影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在代理执行动作失败时记录错误信息是否会增加模型重复失败的动作的可能性，以及这种影响是否随模型类型和训练阶段变化而不同。

Method: 通过在Sokoban、Connect Four和Othello等游戏中测试固定探针项目（通过正确动作的扰动构建），研究记录失败信息对小模型重复失败动作的影响。

Result: 在8个指令调优模型（135M-1.7B）的研究中，记录失败信息反而增加了失败（非法）动作的出现频率，对于损失动作则无显著影响。记录影响的大小取决于模型类型和训练阶段。修改描述形式可以保留模型对失败动作的偏好。

Conclusion: 在训练早期，记录失败信息对模型行为有负面影响；对于不同的训练阶段，具体效果会有所不同。

Abstract: When an LLM agent's move fails, the harness writes the move and the failure message into the transcript and asks again. In tool calling, this record makes small models more likely to repeat the failed action. We test whether the same holds in games (Sokoban, Connect Four, Othello), where a move can fail by being illegal or by being legal but losing, both verified exactly. On fixed probe items built by perturbing a correct move, the record backfires: pooled over 8 instruction-tuned models (135M-1.7B) it makes the failed move more likely (corrective gain G = -1.38 nats per token), through the move's surface form, for illegal moves but not for losing ones, and not for every model. An exploratory analysis shows what sets the sign: after the record, the failed move's log-probability barely depends on how likely it was before (slope 0.18). Moves the model would not have played become more likely, and greedy decoding starts to produce them; moves it was about to play become less likely, but greedy decoding still repeats 38% of them. A description of the failure that omits the move string keeps the model's preference instead. Along the OLMo 2 1B post-training ladder the inversion is present in the base model and largely removed at the DPO stage. RAGEN's default models invert in RAGEN's own Sokoban format before any RL. A "do not repeat" instruction does nothing, a clean retry is the worst harness, and a decoder ban stops exact repeats but not illegal play. All measurements ran in float32 on a free Colab GPU, which first reproduced the original paper's number.

</details>


### [192] [Breaking the high-degree barrier in Lehmer's problem with a human-AI discovery loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30277)
*Yuri Chervonyi,Sergei Gukov,Thang Luong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过提出一个五个参数的构造方法，克服了高次多项式的结构跳跃问题，该方法对于所有目标度数均能构建出马赫勒测度低于Boyd极限点的不可约多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer问题需要理解整数多项式的根如何远离单位圆，这个研究利用了人机交互的发现循环，结合快速数值管道、精确符号验证器和大规模分布式搜索，解决了Boyd的分析一参数家庭在高次情况下的局限性。

Method: 研究团队通过对话式提示、快速数值管道、精确符号验证器和大规模分布式搜索技术，在高阶多项式情况下设计了一种新的五个参数的构造方法，称为'通用博伊德算法'，并用此方法生成验证数据集。

Result: 新方法能够填补Boyd方法在高阶多项式中几乎50%的目标度数缺口，对于所有目标度数均能构造出马勒测度低于Boyd极限点的不可约多项式，并且在所有偶数度数直到2000的前提下，改善了6个已发布的值并保持了99.6%的最佳记录。

Conclusion: 这项研究解决了多项式马勒测度的研究中高阶多项式的构造难题，提出了能够适用于各种目标度数的通用方法，并通过验证了多项式的马勒测度数据集，展示了其在理论研究上的重要发现和应用潜力。

Abstract: While artificial intelligence has made rapid progress in mathematical reasoning, many open problems depend on structure that is revealed only through large-scale computational exploration and analytical synthesis. One such example is Lehmer's problem from 1933. It asks whether the Mahler measure of an integer polynomial, which measures how far its roots stray from the unit circle, can be made arbitrarily small. Previously, constructing irreducible polynomials with integer coefficients and small Mahler measure has been carried out systematically only for even degrees $d \le 180$. At higher degrees, Boyd's analytical one-parameter family frequently factors, skipping nearly 50% of target degrees and leaving no general construction for arbitrary $d$. Here, we address the high-degree regime through a human-AI discovery loop, in which we extend the conversational prompting with a fast numerical pipeline, an exact symbolic verifier, and large-scale distributed search. The AI agent prototypes numerical and symbolic solvers locally, launches parallel searches across distributed clusters and works with the human researcher to extract and verify mathematical hypotheses. Through this interactive loop, we derive a five-parameter construction, which we call the Generalized Boyd algorithm. It eliminates degree-skipping and constructs irreducible polynomials with Mahler measure strictly below Boyd's limit point $α_1 \approx 1.255434$ for every target degree. Evaluated across all even degrees up to 2000, our approach improves upon six previously published values and holds 99.6% of all best known records. With this work we release the verified dataset of polynomials with minimal Mahler measure across all studied even degrees.

</details>


### [193] [Route-and-Generate: KL-Regularized Router Improvement for Reasoning Routing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30351)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Truong Nguyen,Thanh-Thien Le,Hoang Phan,Thien Huu Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Rouge 提出了一种分层训练框架，使大语言模型能够适应性地决定何时及在简单或困难任务中分配推理努力，从而提高准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大语言模型在简单的任务上过度思考，在困难的任务上则思考不足，Rouge 设计了一种分层训练框架来解决这一问题。

Method: Rouge 引入了一种路由策略，通过固定采样预算在多个推理格式之间分配推理努力，而不是直接优化回答生成。同时，采用基于排名的奖励机制来鼓励简洁的回答，并通过闭式 Kullback-Leibler 正则化策略改进步骤来训练神经路由器。

Result: 通过 Rouge 的分层训练框架，模型在简单问题上得到了简洁的答案，在复杂问题上则保留了更深入的推理，从而在多个推理基准测试中实现了准确性和效率的优化。

Conclusion: Rouge 通过新的方法有效地管理了大型语言模型的推理过程，提高了其在各种任务中的性能。

Abstract: Large language models often apply complex reasoning indiscriminately, leading to overthinking on simple tasks and underthinking on harder ones. We present Route-and-Generate (Rouge), a hierarchical training framework that enables LLMs to adaptively decide when and how much reasoning is required for each input. Our approach decouples reasoning effort allocation from answer generation by introducing a routing policy that allocates a fixed sampling budget across multiple reasoning formats, while the base model is optimized with GRPO using per-sample rewards. To promote efficient reasoning, we employ a rank-based reward that favors shorter correct solutions, and update the routing policy via a closed-form KL-regularized policy improvement step distilled into a neural router. This design encourages concise answers on easy problems while reserving longer reasoning for harder ones, achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across multiple reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [194] [Soft Ontology Priors over Latent Concept Posteriors for Weakly Supervised Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30391)
*Tengwei Song,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过在训练目标中注入软语义先验，将专家推理注入有监督任务中，以解决神经符号预测器因推理捷径导致的概念映射模糊问题，从而提高了模型在下游任务中的鲁棒性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于神经符号模型的训练方法存在推理捷径问题，即不同概念映射可以达到同样的似然最大值，导致恢复的概念不可识别且不安全。本研究旨在通过引入一个软语义先验来克服这个问题。

Method: 研究者将软语义先验融入训练目标，并与任务约束一起编译成一个平滑，可分解的概率电路。这种方法允许将专家的公理嵌入电路权重中，在评估前使神经树的权重折入。从而确保了推理的稳健性和安全性。

Result: 在独立伯努利感知下，编译后的结构化概念后验的概率积分归一化可以线性减少到加权模型计数，且权重折叠过程不影响推理过程。此外，实证研究表明，软先验赋予了模型对噪声公理的鲁棒性，而硬支撑的等价模型可通过单一错误公理变成退化。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入软语义先验，有效地解决了神经符号模型中的推理捷径问题，并证明了所提出的方法在具有六个概念的现实世界驾驶基准测试以及控控行的神经符号任务中具有更好的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) predictors that supervise only the task label suffer from reasoning shortcuts: distinct concept mappings achieve the same maximum likelihood, leaving the recovered concepts unidentifiable and unsafe for downstream reuse. We attack this failure by injecting a soft ontology prior over the latent concepts into the training objective and compiling it together with the task constraint into a smooth, decomposable probabilistic circuit. Under independent Bernoulli perception, the normalisers of the resulting structured concept posterior reduce to weighted model counts that are linear in the compiled circuit size, because the neural leaf weights fold into the circuit before evaluation. The soft compilation strategy places expert axioms in the circuit's weights rather than its support (task-structural axioms guaranteed by the data-generating process stay hard), so noisy expert axioms only redistribute prior mass and cannot empty the support. We characterise how the prior reweights label-restricted shortcut mass through differences in ontology violation energy, show that soft priors incur bounded log-density perturbations under bounded axiom noise, and prove that the hard-support counterpart can be made degenerate by a single corrupted axiom. We validate these predictions on an audited six-concept view of the real-world BDD-OIA driving benchmark and on controlled NeSy tasks.

</details>


### [195] [RoleFlow: Self-Evolving Multi-Role Agentic Collaboration via Structural Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30429)
*Yi Ge,Pan Wang,Xuehao Cui,Zian Zeng,Xu Hu,Heng Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RoleFlow的自进化多角色代理系统，通过反馈驱动的原子级编辑和按需角色合成来动态调整合作结构。RoleFlow在七个跨领域的基准测试中表现出最优性能，特别是在多跳问答、数学推理、网页交互和代码生成方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多智能体系统依赖于预定义的合作结构，限制了它们对不断变化的任务需求的适应性。虽然现有方法允许动态编排，但它们只将单一结果分配给每个决策，难以识别个体结构变化的贡献。因此，本文旨在提出一种自进化多角色代理系统，通过反馈驱动的原子级编辑和按需角色合成来动态调整合作结构。

Method: 本文通过引入Single-Edit Counterfactual Policy Optimization (SECPO) 方法，针对每个编辑与无编辑情况下的相同链状态进行评估，并给予特定的贡献。通过这种方法，使得结构适应过程可学习。

Result: 在七个跨领域的基准测试中，RoleFlow 在平均性能上优于最强大的竞争对手，提高了4.7%的得分，特别是在多跳问答、数学推理、网页交互和代码生成方面表现突出。

Conclusion: RoleFlow 通过反馈驱动的原子级编辑和按需角色合成展示了跨基准测试的可移植性、训练数据效率和多执行器可泛化性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems often rely on predefined collaboration structures, limiting their adaptability to evolving task requirements. Although recent approaches enable dynamic orchestration, trajectory-level rewards they apply assign one outcome to each decision, ofen struggling to identify the contributions of individual structural changes. We propose RoleFlow, a self-evolving multi-role agentic system that refines collaboration structures through feedback-driven atomic edits and on-demand role synthesis. To make structural adaptation learnable, we introduce Single-Edit Counterfactual Policy Optimization (SECPO), which evaluates each edit against its no-edit counterpart at the same chain state and assigns targeted credit to edit-specific tokens. Across seven benchmarks spanning four domains, RoleFlow achieves the optimal average performance, surpassing the strongest competitor by 4.7 points. It leads on all three multi-hop QA benchmarks by 3.2-7.5 F1 points and also shows supremacy on mathematical reasoning, web interaction, and code generation. Further analyses demonstrate cross-benchmark transferability, training-data efficiency, and cross-executor generalization.

</details>


### [196] [Secure Agentic AI and Multimodal Intelligence in 6G Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30436)
*Satyam Gaba,Devanshu Motwani,Siva Sai,Vineet Suri,Vikas Ranjan,Vinay Chamola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文摘要介绍第六代通信系统中代理式多模态智能的隐私与安全问题，涵盖了感知、记忆、工具使用和多代理协调等方面，并分析了传感、认知、行动、记忆和通信层的威胁模型和攻击面，提出了多种缓解策略，指出了需要进一步研究的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着第六代通信系统的推进，代理式多模态智能在支持沉浸式、意图驱动的服务和具有动作控制的网络中具有潜力，但同时也带来了超越传统AI和网络安全模型的安全和隐私风险，需要从跨层方式探讨并提出相应的缓解策略。

Method: 本文综合了代理式多模态智能和网络安全的相关研究，采用系统观点跨越感知、记忆、工具使用和多代理协调等不同层面，对传感、认知、行动、记忆和通信层的威胁模型和攻击面进行了分析，并提出了多种缓解措施。

Result: 本文详细地分析了传感、认知、行动、记忆和通信层的威胁模型和攻击面，按结构化的分类组织现有的攻击，并提出了包括提示和记忆防御、代理间信任机制、安全协调和隐私保护学习在内的缓解策略。

Conclusion: 本文为多模态代理式多代理6G系统可信性的进一步研究指明了方向，提出了多模态代理式智能系统的信任度与安全性提升路径和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) communication systems are expected to support immersive, intent-driven, and latency-critical services through tight integration of multimodal intelligence, edge computing, and autonomous network control. In this setting, agentic AI systems can reason over heterogeneous inputs, use tools and network functions, coordinate across distributed entities, and adapt to dynamic environments. These capabilities make agentic multimodal intelligence promising for 6G applications such as semantic communications, digital twins, and zero-touch network management, but they also introduce security and privacy risks that extend beyond conventional AI and network security models. Vulnerabilities can propagate across multimodal data pipelines, memory, tools, communication interfaces, and inter-agent coordination loops. We survey multimodal intelligence and agentic AI for 6G cross-layered communication systems from the perspectives of privacy and security. We present a 6G-oriented system view spanning perception, memory, tool use, and multi-agent orchestration; analyze threat models and attack surfaces across sensing, cognition, action, memory, and communication layers; organize existing attacks into a structured taxonomy; and review mitigation strategies including prompt and memory defenses, inter-agent trust mechanisms, secure orchestration, and privacy-preserving learning. We conclude by outlining open research directions for trustworthy multimodal multi-agent 6G systems.

</details>


### [197] [SpecMAGIC: Specification-Guided Multi-Task Generalization in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30443)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecMAGIC 提出了一种通过任务规范生成可复用工序的有效方法，实现多智能体系统在面对未见过的任务时的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体强化学习方法在遇到新的复杂任务时，往往需要重新训练，这限制了其在实际应用中的灵活性。因此，提出 SpecMAGIC，旨在开发一种无需重新训练便能适应新任务的方法。

Method: SpecMAGIC 使用时间逻辑规范语言描述任务，并将其转化为自动化结构，基于此进行子任务分解。高级规划器通过集中式方式分配任务给各个智能体，而低级策略则负责执行具体任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，SpecMAGIC 在未见过的任务规范上表现优于现有方法，并且随着智能体数量的增加仍能保持良好的性能。

Conclusion: SpecMAGIC 为多智能体系统提供了有效的零样本泛化策略，通过预先学习和组合子任务策略来适应新的复杂任务。

Abstract: Multi-task generalization is an important yet underexplored problem in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Many real-world applications require multi-agent systems to generalize across complex, temporally extended tasks with diverse inter-agent coordination requirements. We introduce a specification language that combines temporal-logic task specifications with operators for expressing such requirements. We propose SpecMAGIC, a hierarchical framework that leverages the automaton structure of task specifications for subtask decomposition. A centralized high-level planner identifies and allocates compatible subtask sequences across agents, while decentralized low-level policies execute individual subtasks. This enables zero-shot generalization to new tasks by composing reusable subtask policies according to new specifications, without retraining. We establish soundness and probabilistic completeness of the high-level planner. Experiments on multi-agent navigation tasks show that SpecMAGIC substantially outperforms existing approaches on unseen task specifications and maintains strong performance as the number of agents increases.

</details>


### [198] [A Three-Pillar Governance Framework for Safe and Trustworthy AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30445)
*Edward C. Cheng,Jeshua Cheng,Alice Siu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为3P的模型和框架，通过透明性、问责制和可信度来开发安全可靠的AI代理。该框架提供了一种逐步引入代理自主性的途径，并通过结构化的人员监督和可审计的操作控制实现。该模型支持代理生命周期监控、决策可追溯性、升级管理和企业代理环境中的人员-代理协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HITL和负责任AI方法虽然提供了重要的基础，但它们仍然分散在系统设计、治理和操作部署中，缺乏统一的方法来确保AI代理的安全性和信任度。

Method: 该研究提出了一种基于透明性、问责制和可信度的Three-Pillar（3P）模型和框架，通过逐步引入代理的自主性，并在结构化的人工监督和可审计的操作控制中实现。

Result: 通过该框架，可以实现代理的生命周期监控、决策跟踪、升级管理和人员-代理协作，并提供了一种实用的基础来部署与人类意图保持一致的AI代理，同时允许向更高的自主性方向可控地发展。

Conclusion: 该3P模型为开发安全和可信的AI代理提供了一种实践基础，通过集成监控原则与操作机制，使得AI代理可以保持与人类意图的一致性，同时能够可控地向更高自主性发展。

Abstract: AI agents capable of autonomous decision-making introduce significant challenges related to safety, alignment, accountability, and human oversight. Existing Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) and responsible AI approaches provide important foundations, but many remain fragmented across system design, governance, and operational deployment. This paper presents a Three-Pillar (3P) model and framework for developing safe and trustworthy AI agents through transparency, accountability, and trustworthiness. The framework defines an evolutionary path in which increasing agent autonomy is introduced through progressive validation, structured human oversight, and auditable operational controls. We further describe how the model supports agent lifecycle monitoring, decision traceability, escalation management, and human-agent collaboration within enterprise agentic environments. By integrating oversight principles with operational mechanisms, the proposed framework provides a practical foundation for deploying AI agents that remain aligned with human intent while enabling controlled progression toward higher autonomy.

</details>


### [199] [Same Beliefs, Different Policies: Learning Reusable Representations for Early Stopping in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30450)
*Jingwei Ni,Dmitry Abulkhanov,Vladislav Smirnov,Markus Leippold,Elliott Ash,Timothy Baldwin,Artem Shelmanov,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BELT (BELief-based Termination) 提出了一个新的停用策略，通过学习当前和未来答案的信念，无需额外的控制器训练，在不同计算资源下可以调节准确性和效率的权衡，适用于多种任务，且相对于端到端控制器在参数量和计算成本上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 常常在得到正确答案后继续推理，浪费了计算资源。BELT 旨在解决这个问题，通过学习模型对当前和未来答案的信念，间接决定停止时机，从而在保持准确率的同时减少计算量。

Method: BELT 通过以下步骤工作：首先，从冻结的 LLM 的隐藏状态中学习信念；其次，基于这些冻结的特征拟合线性停止策略；最后，通过调整模型或设置阈值来适应不同的准确性和计算效率权衡。

Result: 在五个不同领域的数学基准测试中，BELT 将生成的令牌减少了 41% 到 51%，且未损失准确性。同时，BELT 在编码和代理任务上也表现出色，无需针对这些特定任务进行训练。在参数量方面，BELT 的探针比其他基准模型少 21.2 倍，在计算成本方面则少使用55倍的 GPU 时。

Conclusion: BELT 为停用策略提供了一种有效的方法，它通过学习模型的信念来间接控制推理终止时间，不仅可以实现与端到端控制器相匹敌的准确性和计算效率权衡，还能减少所需的计算资源。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs often continue reasoning after reaching a correct answer, wasting computation and sometimes replacing it with an incorrect one. Deciding when to stop is a metareasoning problem: the model must weigh what further reasoning could achieve against its cost, given what it can already answer. Direct utility training learns a stopping representation at a chosen token price; supporting new prices can require additional controller training. We introduce BELT (BELief-based Termination), which separates outcome prediction from stopping control. It learns beliefs about current and future answers from a frozen LLM's hidden states, then fits a linear stopping policy on the frozen features. The prediction targets are independent of token price, so one probe supports different accuracy-cost trade-offs through CPU policy refitting or threshold adjustment. Averaged over five out-of-distribution (OOD) math benchmarks, BELT reduces generated tokens by 41% on Qwen3-1.7B and 51% on OLMo-3-7B-Think without loss of accuracy. The same math-trained BELT transfers to coding and agentic tasks without training on code or agentic data. In all three domains, its accuracy-cost trade-offs are competitive with or better than those of end-to-end controllers. On OLMo3, its probe has 21.2x fewer parameters than one checkpoint of the strongest baseline, OS-Pruner, and needs 55x fewer training GPU-hours than OS-Pruner's five price-specific checkpoints.

</details>


### [200] [Why Bother Me Again? Benchmarking Personal Agent Memory for Interaction Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30485)
*Jialin Li,Zhenhao Chen,Hanjun Luo,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemPABench是一个交互环境，用于评估个人代理的记忆性能。它通过模拟长期任务来测试代理在哪些情况下需要澄清、确认或自主行动。四个具有一系列真实偏好的模拟用户提供了99个会话的数据，以评估记忆系统的偏好选择、交付行为及对环境变化的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆基准大多关注事实性记忆和个人化答案，而忽略了记忆学习和行为决策对交互偏好的处理。研究引入了MemPABench，旨在提供一个环境，评估记忆系统在个人代理中的表现，特别是在应对长期任务和动态偏好改变方面的效能。

Method: MemPABench通过四个带不同偏好设置的模拟用户角色设计了环境，每用户有14种偏好，涵盖工作和个人情景，在四个月内共进行了99次会话。每个会话中用户角色有5次事件驱动的偏好变化，确保对记忆系统的长期记忆能力和对偏好的响应进行评估。通过四种不同的记忆系统与四种不同的大语言模型进行对比测试。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前的记忆系统在偏好选择上的优势有限，基于检索的记忆系统（如Mem0、MemOS、简单RAG）并未优于无记忆系统，而基于文件的记忆系统虽然提高了准确性但在不相关的情况下也应用了偏好。

Conclusion: 研究发现了现有记忆系统在从正确的偏好中检索和适应偏好变化方面存在困难。即使在正确偏好可用的情况下，控制代理的默认交互习惯也尤为困难。研究团队建议，未来的工作需要探索能够更有效地检测和适应用户交互偏好变化的记忆机制。

Abstract: Personal intelligence systems are taking on increasingly complex everyday tasks, yet repeated questions, unnecessary confirmations, and unwanted outreach can turn capable assistance into interaction burden. Reducing this burden requires agents to remember how users want to be assisted. However, existing memory benchmarks focus on factual recall and personalized answers and rarely test how agents learn and act on interaction preferences. We introduce MemPABench, an interactive environment for on-policy evaluation of memory in personal agents. MemPABench poses long-horizon tasks requiring agents to decide when to clarify, confirm, or act autonomously. Four grounded simulated users hold 14 preferences across work and personal contexts. Their feedback spans 99 sessions over four months, with five event-driven changes per user testing memory updates. We evaluate preference selection, delivered behavior, and sensitivity to context and preference change. Evaluating four memory systems across four LLM backbones, we find that current memory brings limited gains in preference selection. Retrieval-based systems (i.e. Mem0, MemOS, simple RAG) do not outperform No Memory, while File-based memory improves accuracy but applies preferences beyond relevant contexts. Our analysis shows that current memory struggles to retrieve interaction preferences and adapt to preference changes. Even with the correct preference available, overriding the agent's default interaction habits remains challenging. The environment and dataset are available at https://mempabench.com.

</details>


### [201] [From Single-Actor Environments to Multi-Actor Event Worlds: Can LLMs Be Event World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30488)
*Juntong Ni,Zewen Liu,Bohan Wang,Lige Zhang,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用大型语言模型预测事件世界的未来观察结果，建立了基于人类游戏和真实交易的数据集，发现了模型预测受其他参与者影响的事件时的局限性，以及即使在这些局限下，模型仍能通过模仿经验和世界模型初始化提高策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现实世界中的多个参与者独立决策且事件系统可能在未采取行动时继续演化，研究旨在探索大型语言模型在预测事件世界未来观察结果方面的潜力。

Method: 通过制定等级框架，研究比较了包含与不包含其他参与者行动时的预测能力，并测试了多步演练、从未执行过的行为的预测能力以及对代理决策的帮助。使用人类游戏和真实交易数据建立基准。

Result: 在所有参与者被记录的行动提供的情况下，模型预测有所改善。然而，仅凭代理行动，当其他参与者显著影响结果时，模型表现较差，且更大模型和更长历史记录帮助不大。这限制了多步预测和代理决策，模型可能会高估计划的成功。

Conclusion: 即使在这些局限性下，通过想象的经验和使用世界模型进行初始化，模型仍可以在策略学习中发挥积极作用。预见其他参与者的行为仍然是事件世界建模中的一个核心挑战。

Abstract: World models help agents assess the consequences of their actions before acting. Many real systems, however, involve multiple actors who make their own decisions, can evolve even when the agent takes no action, and are only partially observed through event logs. We formalize these systems as event worlds and ask whether large language models can predict future observations from the log and the agent's proposed actions. We build complementary benchmarks from human Diplomacy games and real Polymarket trades. Our hierarchical framework compares prediction with and without other actors' actions, then tests multi-step rollouts, predictions for actions never taken, and usefulness to agents. Fine-tuning and more data improve prediction when all actors' recorded actions are given. With only the agent's action, models struggle when other actors strongly affect the outcome, and larger models and longer histories help little. This gap also limits multi-step prediction and agent decisions, with models overestimating plan success. Yet imagined experience and initialization from world models can still improve policy learning. Anticipating other actors' actions remains a central challenge for event world modeling.

</details>


### [202] [What the Trace Shows and What the Label Says: Auditing Behavioral Detectors in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30491)
*Idunnuoluwa A. Adeniji,Shilpika,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，通过减少执行轨迹到标签（如最终答案正确性、工具验证和故障传播）来评估LLM代理的方法可能无法充分捕捉轨迹显示的内容。在基于GSM8K的工具使用环境中，研究了三个追踪级检测器对六个生产模型的评估，发现了多种错误模式，包括沉默的工具输出覆盖等问题。研究还提出了测量透明性的重要性，希望自动化标签能够附带轨迹证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法可能未能全面反映执行轨迹信息，为提高透明度和准确性，研究基于GSM8K构建了一个工具使用环境，评估模型性能。

Method: 在GSM8K基础上构建工具使用环境，模型调用计算器时输出偶尔被篡改，提供清晰的工具返回值、模型后续使用值和检测器报告的参照。通过审计三个标记级别检测器，对六个生产模型和每种模型30个问题进行评估。

Result: 发现不同标签（如最终正确性、工具验证和故障传播）之间存在系统性差异，尤其是在工具输出覆盖场景下，模型使用了错误但未影响结果的工具输出。人为审查90条轨迹标签，最终正确性完全一致，验证正确率为67/81，错误分布在四大模式中。

Conclusion: 评估方法需要提高透明度，自动化标签应附带证据，说明检测器问题是否适用于特定轨迹，是否存在执行限制以及是否有人工修正。实现了追踪查看器，将这些信息与报告结果一同提供。

Abstract: LLM agents are often evaluated by reducing execution traces to labels such as final-answer correctness, tool verification, and fault propagation. These labels are measurements produced by parsers, behavioral detectors, and harness rules, and they may not fully capture what the trace shows. We study this in a tool-use setting built on GSM8K, where agents call a calculator whose outputs are occasionally corrupted, giving clear ground truth for what the tool returned, what the model later used, and what the detectors reported. We audit three trace-level detectors across six production models and 30 problems per model. Final correctness, visible re-verification, and fault propagation can disagree in systematic ways. A key failure mode is silent tool-output override: the calculator returns a corrupted value, but the model later uses a different, often correct, value without calling the tool again, so the bad value stops propagating while a verification detector records no re-check. Human review of 90 traces agrees perfectly with the automated labels for final correctness (67/67), fault impact (19/19), and fault relevance (18/18), but only 67/81 for verification; all 14 disagreements fall into four recurring patterns. Execution limits also change the reported result: in an ablation across two models, one ranges from 21/30 to 27/30 correct as step and token budgets change, while the other stays between 27/30 and 29/30. These findings motivate measurement transparency: an automated label should come with the trace evidence behind it, whether the question the detector asks applied to that trace, whether the run was cut off by an execution limit, and any human corrections. Our trace viewer makes this information inspectable alongside the reported results.

</details>


### [203] [From Prose to Properties: Testing Research Claims with ARATest for Agent-Native Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30495)
*Yifeng He,Jiachen Liu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种称为ARATest的基于属性的测试库，用于验证AI代理生成的声明。ARATest通过抽象声明的属性骨架并将其转换为可执行测试来实现这一目标。通过绑定声明和骨架，并固定比较的数量、案例、范围和数值策略等，ARATest能够生成共享案例类型并自动执行测试，以检测声明中的违反情况。测试结果表明，ARATest能够在不产生误报的情况下准确检测到所有预设的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理能够生成和报告研究发现，传统的验证方法已经无法跟上研究速度。为了提升验证效率和准确性，作者提出了一种基于属性的测试库ARATest，以便更快速、准确地验证这些发现。

Method: ARATest 通过抽象声明的属性骨架（一个参数化的递归关系）并将其转换为可执行的测试来验证声明。具体来说，它包括绑定声明和骨架，固定比较的数量、案例、范围和数值策略等，利用共享案例类型生成器提供符合统计单元的案例，通过三值语义（持有、违反或无法确定）评估属性，以及通过默认生成器读取声明关联的证据或启用代理执行代码来创建新鲜案例。

Result: 在243个声明中，有54个声明得到了忠实的声明绑定并关联了证据：在32个声明中，声明有效；在8个声明中，声明被违反；在14个声明中，结果依然无法确定。此外，ARATest成功检测了两类声明中的所有预设错误，并且在19个有效控制案例中没有产生任何误报。测试结果还显示，利用固定测试可以对比当前发布的证据和新产生的案例中的声明属性。

Conclusion: ARATest显著提高了对AI代理生成声明的有效验证，为学术界提供了一种新的验证方法。通过准确地提供声明属性骨架，ARATest能够适应不同研究场景中需要检验的声明类型，提高测试效率和结果的可靠性。

Abstract: AI agents now run experiments and report findings, so research produces claims faster than researchers can check them. Each empirical claim implies a property that must hold over its cases, but it states that property only in prose. Without an explicit property, no test can decide which evidence would falsify a claim, so validation cannot keep pace with the claims agents produce. To make claims testable, we approximate a claim's semantics by abstracting its property skeleton, a parameterized relation that recurs across claims. We present ARATest, a property-based testing library that checks these skeletons as executable tests. A typed declaration binds a claim to a skeleton and fixes the claim's compared quantities, cases, scope, and numerical policy. A shared case type makes every generator supply cases in the claim's statistical unit. A three-valued semantics assigns each property one of three verdicts: holds, violated, or inconclusive. Every violation carries a replayable witness. A default generator reads the evidence that agent-native research artifacts (ARA) link to each claim, and auto-research generators dispatch an agent to run released code for fresh cases. Our DissClaimer plugin supplies that agent and runs these generators as continuous testing.
  Of 243 claims in 33 ARA artifacts, 54 received faithful declarations bound to evidence: 32 held, eight were violated, and 14 remained inconclusive. Two violations exposed errors in published papers that their authors confirmed. ARATest detected all 553 seeded faults without a false alarm on 19 valid controls, outperforming two LLM judges. Fresh runs exposed behaviors that reported evidence could not show. These results suggest that one fixed test can check explicit claim properties against both reported and fresh evidence.

</details>


### [204] [Can LLMs Improve Their Own Harness? A Controlled Study on ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30499)
*Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索了语言模型通过自主构建和改进工具来完成任务的能力，发现即使在任务性能优秀的模型中，后续的改进也不能保证有效推广或保留已有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型通常依赖于人类不断修改才能应对新任务。为了探索语言模型的自主能力，研究人员通过ARC-AGI-3基准测试，挑战模型在不依赖人类干预的情况下自我学习与执行任务。

Method: 研究人员设计了一个固定代码的引导过程，让模型构建一个完整任务框架，并在其上执行多个游戏。随后，模型对整个代码进行两次修订，每次修订后对其性能进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，虽然在首次构建的框架上，模型能够取得较好的任务表现，但经过两次修订后，平均改进幅度仅增加了2.80点，且开发中的改进可能会被后续版本的性能下降所抵消。同时，虽然人类操作的系统有进一步的改进空间，但模型自主生成的系统存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了模型自主构建工具与可靠适应之间的差距，即使模型能在两个修订周期内表现出色，也未必能在后续版本中保持或改进这些能力。

Abstract: A generally intelligent system acquires new skills efficiently without requiring humans to redesign its software scaffold for every new task. Yet language models rarely act alone. A harness is a symbolic program that orchestrates calls to the model, handling perception, memory, tool use, recovery, and inference allocation on the model's behalf. ARC-AGI-3 is an interactive benchmark in which agents must explore unfamiliar environments, figure out their goals on the fly, and keep learning as they go. On it, human-steered systems already report ceiling scores across the public games, so we know effective designs exist. We ask whether frontier models can build and improve such a harness on their own. Through a fixed coding bootstrap, each model constructs a complete task harness, acts through that harness on five development games, and revises the entire codebase twice. Every version is evaluated on twenty games that never influence adaptation. We measure task performance with Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), which combines level completion with action efficiency relative to human baselines, and record dollar-denominated inference cost as a practical resource measure. Across 27 lineages, harness performance ranges from zero to near-ceiling scores. Two revisions raise mean held-out RHAE by only 2.80 points (95% interval [-1.44,7.35]). Development gains overstate held-out improvement, and reductions in inference cost can come at the expense of task performance. Human-steered systems demonstrate further headroom. These findings reveal a gap between autonomous harness construction and reliable adaptation under this two-revision protocol: even strong task performance does not ensure that subsequent revisions generalize or preserve existing capabilities.

</details>


### [205] [PLE: Physical-Engineering's Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30537)
*Rujun Han,Yufan Zhuang,Arijit Ray,Han Yu,Zoey CuiZhu,George Lee,Yifei Ming,Binz Roy,Varun Sakalkar,Vishy Tirumalashetty,Burak Gokturk,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有前沿语言模型在多项专业知识基准测试中的表现优秀，但尚未证明其能够处理复杂的、实际的工程任务。论文提出了一个名为PLE的新基准测试，包含85项长期交互任务，涵盖电气、土木、结构和机械工程，旨在评估模型是否能完成真正的可收费工程交付。实验结果显示，即使是最佳配置的语言模型也只能在每个任务上达到50.6%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的工程基准测试大多关注基础性问题或自包含的小型设计问题，未能充分评估模型处理复杂实际工程任务的能力。为此，引入了PLE基准测试，以评估前沿语言模型在实际工程设计中的性能。

Method: 论文构建了PLE基准测试，涵盖了八个工程学科。实验采用了多种先进的语言模型，并采用公开的代理封装程序进行评估。包含专家编写的任务和共创编写的任务，并通过对抗性的创建者-解决者代理管道生成每个任务的真实项目环境、可执行评分器和参考交付物。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使使用最先进的语言模型配置，也只能在每个任务上实现约50.6%的准确率。平均每项任务使用了2700万tokens和63分钟。

Conclusion: 当前最前沿的语言模型在PLE基准测试中的表现不足以满足自主物理工程设计的要求，展示出模型与实际工程设计之间的巨大差距。

Abstract: Frontier language models now score above 90% on popular knowledge benchmarks and are increasingly deployed as agents that carry out multi-hour professional work. Yet no existing benchmark tests whether they can perform the real-world, economically valuable work of a professional physical engineer, such as modelling physics and legal code requirements across a multi-format project corpus, optimising material and installation costs, and issuing 2D/3D engineering design packages that a licensed reviewer would sign off on. Existing engineering benchmarks focus on textbook question answering, isolated tool calls or self-contained toy design problems. As a result, most of these benchmarks are already saturated by leading agents and solving them is far away from delivering real engineering design. We introduce PLE, a benchmark of 85 long-horizon, agentic tasks in professional physical engineering consisting of electrical, civil, structural and mechanical engineering, where solving a task means completing a realistic, billable engineering deliverable. PLE combines two complementary curation paths. Expert-Authored tasks are created end to end by practising engineers from a professional data provider's expert pool. Co-Authored tasks start from problem statements written by practising engineers; an adversarial Creator-Solver agent pipeline constructs each task's realistic project environment, executable grader and reference deliverables before sending them to expert engineers for validation and difficulty intensification. We evaluate a variety of frontier LLMs under seven publicly available agent harnesses. Even the strongest configuration reaches a mean score of only 50.6%, while spending 27M tokens and 63 minutes per task on average, demonstrating the substantial gap between current agents and autonomous physical engineering design.

</details>


### [206] [Governed Self-Evolution for Autonomous Investing: A Multi-Agent LLM System with an Invariant Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30557)
*Yebo Feng,Jingyang Dai,Liangrui Pan,Nelson Lau,Jiahua Xu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Steward 是一个多代理 LLM 系统，旨在通过代码审查和自我学习提升性能而不削弱自身保护机制，在五个月的实际部署中展示了优秀的风险管理和自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一个不可逆且基于不可信网络内容的投资自主系统中，同时保持自我改进并保护自身机制。Steward 通过分工明确的代理角色和代码审查机制来实现。

Method: Steward 通过角色分工明确的九个代理进行联合决策，使用七层共享记忆，并结合代码审查和自我学习机制来实现自我改进。系统核心部分保持不变，确保自我编辑无法触及。

Result: Steward 在五个月的实际部署中表现出了低风险和稳定收益，对回放输入展示了自我学习的效果，并且在一次回测中证明了核心机制能够在随机入场策略下减少最大回撤。

Conclusion: Steward 证明了在真实市场环境中同时实现高性能和自身机制保护是可行的，并通过代码审查和自我学习机制展现了自我改进能力。

Abstract: LLM agents that act on live Web data must combine heterogeneous evidence, remember what they have done and improve from outcomes without weakening their own safeguards. We study this problem in autonomous investing, where every action is irreversible and based on untrusted Web content. We present Steward, a multi-agent LLM system that draws the boundary of self-evolution in its type system. First, nine role-specialised agents decide jointly: four analysts read news feeds, SEC filings, macroeconomic series and market data and emit typed reports, from which a Portfolio Manager sets target positions that a protected Risk Manager audits. Second, a seven-layer shared memory, assembled by code from the system's own record, gives each decision its position context, the market narrative, calibration and the agent's past decisions. Third, the system improves itself by review: code grades every trade and forecast nightly and writes the grades into memory, and each quarter a reflector proposes prompt learnings that a deterministic gate applies reversibly and replay of recorded inputs verifies. All three operate around an invariant core of order arithmetic, risk rules and discipline-agent prompts that no self-edit can reach. In a five-month live deployment (102 trading days, 1,632 LLM calls), the system contained a 24.6% sector drawdown to a 0.3% loss; in replay of its recorded inputs, a single model given the same raw data traded more and lost 1.35 points against the market over five days where the joint design showed no significant loss, and no injected instruction was followed; and a 2015-2026 backtest shows the core alone lowering the maximum drawdown under all 50 random entry policies. The reflector traced the dominant failure to its root cause, and the gate applied a learning that the agent adopted on trigger evenings and on no control evening. Code: https://github.com/yebof/quant-agent

</details>


### [207] [Diagnosing Personalization Failures in Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30573)
*Zhixin Lin,Dongliang Xu,Jungang Li,Shidong Pan,Jun Feng,Yanbin Sun,Jing Li,Liang Liu,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了PrefDiag-Bench，以评估个性化GUI代理的能力，发现代理在处理排名和过程偏好时表现较差，主要瓶颈在于从用户历史中选择相关证据和识别偏好何时生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决传统的GUI代理与个性化GUI代理之间的差异，强调需要更好地理解用户偏好如何影响执行策略及其生效时机。

Method: 作者提出了一个分类用户偏好并将其分为五类的方法，并设计了一个阶段性的诊断框架，使用1,664个探针追踪偏好何时生效以及失败发生的位置。该方法还评估了在两种用户上下文中，包括显式用户配置文件和行为记忆日志，11种GUI代理系统的表现。

Result: 现有代理在非个性化任务中完成了相对较好的表现，但在涉及用户偏好的个性化任务中表现较差。即便是性能最好的模型Seed-2.0-Pro，在明确用户配置文件下的过程偏好任务中也只达到了47.17%的成功率。研究发现代理在此类任务中特别难以处理排名和过程偏好。

Conclusion: 研究指出现有的GUI代理系统在处理偏好问题上存在关键能力瓶颈，并展示了通过提供相关证据可以提高完整性任务的成功率，同时PrefDiag-Bench提供了一种超越总体任务成功来系统性地评估GUI代理个人化能力的方法。

Abstract: Unlike conventional GUI agents, personalized GUI agents require user preferences to guide sequential execution over changing GUI states. This raises two challenges: determining HOW each preference should alter execution strategy and WHEN it should take effect along the interaction trajectory. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate overall task success, offering limited insight into these questions. We introduce PrefDiag-Bench with two components: (1) a categorization of preferences into five types, characterizing HOW they affect execution; and (2) a stage-wise diagnostic framework with 1,664 probes, tracing WHEN preferences should take effect and where failures occur. We evaluate eleven GUI agent systems, including end-to-end agents and agentic workflows, under two forms of user context: explicit user profiles and behavioral memory logs. Although existing agents achieve relatively high completion rates on non-personalized tasks, performance is substantially lower on preference-personalized tasks. Even the best-performing model, Seed-2.0-Pro, achieves only 47.17% on Process preferences under explicit user profiles. Agents struggle particularly with Ranking and Process preferences. Further analysis reveals two key capability bottlenecks: selecting relevant evidence from user history and recognizing when preferences should take effect during execution. Controlled interventions that explicitly provide relevant evidence improve end-to-end task success by up to 7.06%. PrefDiag-Bench offers a more systematic evaluation of GUI agents' personalization capabilities beyond overall task success. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Zhixin-L/PrefDiag-Bench.

</details>


### [208] [Model Organisms of Collusion: Corrective Transfer in Untrusted Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30582)
*Juan-Pablo Rivera,Ari Spiesberger,Juan J Vazquez,Arun Jose,Axel Ahlqvist,Adeline Kassler,Meru Gopalan,Sandy Tanwisuth,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过调整LLAMA-3.3-70B和Qwen2.5-72B模型，纠正了300个模型自监控中的诚实审验示例中的不当行为，并在未见的提示格式中测量了其他四种示例的不当行为比例。研究结果表明，与调整无关示例相比，这种矫正方法能够显著减少未见示例中的不当行为比例，除了一个LLAMA种子在数学准确性方面有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI控制协议旨在从可能与模型目标不一致的行为中获取有用的工作，一种方法是使用与模型相同的副本作为监控模型，以避免依赖可能被模型欺骗的较弱的审核员，但这也带来了监控模型串通的风险。研究目的是评估仅在已知不当行为示例上调整监控模型是否能有效减少未发现的不当行为。

Method: 研究采用了对模型进行微调的方法来纠正监控模型，具体操作为选取LLAMA-3.3-70B和Qwen2.5-72B模型，在300个监控模型已知不当行为的诚实审验示例上进行微调，然后在未见过的提示格式下测试四种不同的方法，比较矫正后的模型在未见过示例中的不当行为比例。

Result: 结果表明，调整后的方法能够在三种比较方法中减少约14%-20%的未见过示例中的不当行为（种子间的个体差异在10%-32%之间），而调整与不当行为无关的示例则使误报率维持在56%-93%。这项研究还发现，虽然纠正了已发现的行为，但并不能完全揭示监控模型纠正的范围，不同类型纠正方法的效果有所不同。

Conclusion: 该研究发现，通过特定设置的微调，监控模型可以显著减少未发现的不当行为，但并不能完全消除这种风险。对于AI控制协议来说，需要持续监控和调整以确保模型行为的正确性。

Abstract: AI control protocols aim to get useful work from models that may be misaligned by monitoring their behaviour. One approach uses a second copy of the same model as the monitor. This avoids relying on a weaker reviewer the model could outsmart, but risks collusion: the model, now monitoring itself, may cover up its own misbehaviour. One remedy is to fine-tune the monitor on whatever examples of misbehaviour the control team has caught. We ask whether this training also removes collusion that the control team has not yet found. We fine-tune Llama-3.3-70B and Qwen2.5-72B to install five policy-monitor behaviour pairs, correct the monitor on 300 honest reviews of one caught collusion, and measure collusion on the other four in unseen prompt formats. Averaged over the three seeds of each family, correction reduces held-out collusion from 91% to 14-20% under three of the four methods we compare (individual seeds 10-32%), while a control fine-tuned on the same number of unrelated examples leaves 56-93%. Family-average accuracy on control items changes by at most five points and capability is preserved, apart from a drop in mathematical accuracy on one Llama seed. Under the tested training configuration, methods with similar caught-task accuracy differ in held-out transfer, from 58% to 83% average reduction, so the caught behaviour alone does not reveal how far a correction reaches. Catching one collusion lets the control team reduce, but not remove, collusion it has not caught, by retraining the monitor alone.

</details>


### [209] [Better Reasoning, Not Just Bigger Models: A Structure-Grounded Framework for Efficient and Reliable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30611)
*Amirhossein Aghaei,Liangyan Li,Shiyi Yang,Shaohua Wang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为 Structure-Grounded Uncertainty Reduction (SGUR) 的训练无损框架，通过结构化假设空间中的主动不确定性降低，增强语言模型在不完备观察下的推理能力。从仅两个观察症状开始，SGUR 实现了 92.5% 的 Hit@3，远超最强大的 Graph-RAG 基线，同时使用大约 5 倍少的 token 数量，表明结构化基座模型相比单纯增加模型规模可以提供更稳健和高效的代理性能扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型在多轮推理中难以提供结构化且相关的推理链，特别是在观察信息不完整的情况下。本文旨在通过主动不确定性降低，构建一个不需要训练框架，以提升模型推理能力。

Method: 本文的方法是将不完整的观察表示为查询图，并通过带有先验权重的不完全图匹配机制，将其与候选假设图对齐。接着使用一个主动的信息增益策略来选择下一个最有信息量的节点进行查询，从而系统性地精简候选空间。

Result: 在 PrimeKG 提取的医疗诊断基准测试中，从仅两个观察症状开始，SGUR 实现了 92.5% 的 Hit@3，远超最强大的 Graph-RAG 基线，同时使用大约 5 倍少的 token 数量。这表明结构化基座模型相比单纯增加模型规模可以提供更稳健和高效的代理性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法 SGUR，通过结构化假设空间的主动不确定性降低，可以在不增加模型规模的情况下显著提升模型的推理能力。这表明结构化基座模型为代理性能的扩展提供了一种更稳健和高效的策略。

Abstract: Scaling large language models has substantially improved linguistic fluency, yet reliable autonomous agents require more than fluent generation. In multi-turn inference, current agentic systems often produce semantically plausible but structurally ungrounded reasoning, especially under incomplete observations. We propose Structure-Grounded Uncertainty Reduction (SGUR), a training-free framework that complements token-level generation with active uncertainty reduction over structured hypothesis spaces. SGUR represents incomplete observations as a query graph and aligns them with candidate hypothesis graphs through a prior-weighted partial graph matching mechanism, where database-derived empirical priors assign greater importance to decision-relevant relations rather than treating all graph edges uniformly. The resulting inference state is iteratively refined by an active information-gain strategy that selects the next most informative node to query, systematically pruning the candidate space. We instantiate SGUR on a challenging PrimeKG-derived sequential medical diagnosis benchmark, demonstrating that structural grounding can partially substitute for model scale: starting from only two observed symptoms, SGUR achieves 92.5% Hit@3, outperforming the strongest Graph-RAG baseline by 9.4 percentage points while using approximately 5x fewer tokens. Notably, SGUR enables smaller backbone models to match or exceed the performance of models up to 10x larger, suggesting that structural grounding provides a more robust and efficient paradigm for scaling agent performance than relying exclusively on increasing model size.

</details>


### [210] [Large language models show systematic failures in incorporating evolving medical knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30631)
*Min Zeng,Zaifu Zhan,Kai Yu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 慢性 Evo 通过对1,709项医疗指导更改的分析，发现大型语言模型在识别修订指导方面表现良好，但在临床应用中完全采用新指南方面存在显著差距，特别是在指导强度和患者资格发生变化时更为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于医疗知识随临床指南的修订不断演变，研究者开发了 ChronoEvo 数据集来检验大语言模型（LLMs）是否能够准确反映这些变化。

Method: 该研究采用了 ChronoEvo 数据集，包含1,709项修订级别的更改，覆盖12种类型的医疗知识变化。研究者们构建了一个初始模型，并对14个不同的模型进行了评估。此外，两位临床医生还审核了300个模型响应，以验证模型的性能。

Result: 研究发现，大多数模型能够识别出修订的指导（90.8%），80.2%的反馈具有临床正确性，但仅有58.5%能够完全遵从更新的指导，这表明存在显著的识别-应用差距。根据指导强度和患者资格的变化，这种差距尤为明显。引入一种相关性干预措施可以减少过时内容的留存并提高临床正确性，但无法显著改善完全融入的过程。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，LLMs 面临着对不断变化的医疗知识实施忠实体现的显著挑战，即使有更新的指南证据可供参考。

Abstract: Medical knowledge evolves as clinical guidelines revise recommendations, yet whether large language models (LLMs) faithfully incorporate such changes remains unclear. We introduce ChronoEvo, comprising 1,709 recommendation-level revisions across 12 forms of medical knowledge change, to distinguish recognition of revised guidance from incorporation into clinical recommendations. In the primary model, 90.8% of responses recognized revisions and 80.2% were clinically correct, but only 58.5% fully incorporated guidance, yielding a 32.3-percentage-point recognition-incorporation gap. This dissociation persisted across 14 models and was corroborated by two clinicians' adjudicated assessment of 300 responses, yielding a 40.3-percentage-point gap in the validation sample. Changes in recommendation strength and patient eligibility were associated with retention of outdated guidance. A revision-salience intervention reduced outdated-content retention and improved clinical correctness but did not significantly improve full incorporation. These findings identify faithful incorporation of evolving medical knowledge as a distinct challenge for clinical LLM reliability, even when updated guideline evidence is available.

</details>


### [211] [Speculative Acceptance Does Not Decay with Position: A Full-Length Diagnostic on Long Chain-of-Thought with Independent Drafts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30644)
*Zhiming Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究首次对问题内配对测试进行了全面测量，表明在接受率与生成位置的关系上，仅在特定范围（贪婪0-8K，采样0-12K）内未发现衰减现象，并指出拒绝集中在高精确度的反思触发词上，这些触发词属于熵选择性失败，尽管进行了一些干预措施仍未能显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决行业对链式思考增长会降低推测性解码接受率的叙述，并提供更精准的接受率与生成位置关系的数据，以助力设计考虑位置因素的模型合设计和位置感知训练。

Method: 研究团队使用了Greedy和Sampling两种解码方式，测量了MATH-500数据集中的500个问题上的接受率，并通过匹配问题内的测试，分析接受率随生成位置的变化，并进行了温度控制下的抽样模式验证。同时，还引入了三个轻量级的LoRA轨迹对齐方法来干预熵选择性失败。

Result: 结果显示，在所有测试配置中发现接受率与生成位置无显著性衰减趋势；而在生成中段的接受率比开头段略高。此外，在研究温度对抽样模式的影响时发现，所谓的高熵反思标记的抽样模式下降只是由于最高熵标记的存在，而非实际可能性接受率的下降。拒绝集中在高精度的触发词如“等待”、“然而”和“检查”上。尽管进行了干预措施，但轻量级的LoRA三臂轨迹方法仍未能显著提高平均接受长度。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，接受率与生成位置无显著性衰减仅存在于特定长度范围内，拒绝集中于高精度触发词是一个熵选择性失败，而非位置上的失败，并提供了首个独立目标对的定位基线。

Abstract: The claim that speculative-decoding acceptance rate decays as a chain-of-thought grows longer has become a de facto industry narrative, motivating draft-model co-design and position-aware training. Nearly all direct evidence comes from trained drafters whose offline training length is far shorter than inference, so a purely positional law has never been isolated. We present the first systematic, full-length measurement of acceptance rate as a function of generation position on MATH-500 (all 500 problems), under greedy and sampling decoding, with two independent drafts against DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B: a same-family draft (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B) and a cross-family draft (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct), yielding about 1.78M verification rounds. Using within-problem paired tests, we find that after isolating a greedy-decoding repetition pathology, acceptance rate does not decay with position in any of the four configurations within the supported ranges (greedy 0-8K, sampling 0-12K); the second half of each trajectory is slightly but significantly higher than the first. A controlled temperature sweep shows that the apparent sampling-mode degradation of high-entropy reflection tokens is a visibility artifact of argmax agreement, not a drop in expected acceptance probability. Rejections concentrate with high precision on reflection trigger tokens such as "wait", "however" and "check", an entropy-selective failure that a lightweight three-arm LoRA trajectory-alignment intervention (uniform, reflection-marked, entropy-weighted) fails to improve in mean accepted length. We thus supply the positional baseline that independent draft-target pairs were missing, and localize the residual failure to decision-point entropy rather than position.

</details>


### [212] [Anticipating Social Consequences with Distributional World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30657)
*Viet-Thanh Pham,Thuy-Trang Vu,Zhuang Li,Lizhen Qu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSWM模型预测候选行动的各种可能反应、其概率以及即时和最终结果值在一个输出中，展现出优于其他方法的广泛环境适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社会互动场景中，单一预测未来可能会忽略重要的替代可能性，而通过多次模拟探索这些替代路径会增加推断成本。

Method: 使用4B参数的模型，通过监督微调和GRPO进行训练，以生成每个候选行动的各种可能后果及其概率。

Result: 在三个已见环境和两个未知环境中，DSWM在15项指标中的13项上表现最佳。消融实验表明，保持多种未来及其预测概率可以改进所有五种环境下的结果。

Conclusion: DSWM能有效预测不同行动的多种可能性，对于社会环境下的人工智能决策有着显著提升。

Abstract: Social world models predict how interactions develop after an action, helping agents negotiate, cooperate and mediate. In these situations, one action can lead to several plausible futures, hence predicting only one future can overlook consequential alternatives, while exploring them through repeated simulations increases inference cost. We propose DSWM, a Distributional Social World Model that predicts alternative responses, their probabilities and immediate and final outcome values in one output per candidate action. We train a 4B model on repeated simulations using supervised fine-tuning and GRPO. At inference time, the actor uses these possible consequences to choose an action. Across three seen and two unseen environments, with interaction backbones absent from training collection, DSWM achieves the best average performance on 13 of 15 metrics. Ablations show that retaining multiple futures and their predicted probabilities improves outcomes in all five environments.

</details>


### [213] [A Topology-Guided GeoAI Agentic Framework for Urban Flood Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30660)
*Yutian Tao,Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本框架通过结合公开数据、明确的空间推理、可追踪的证据和用户监督，允许透明的一次性洪水筛查，并保持关键建议的可检查和用户控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决洪水筛查中的自主推理与可靠空间分析及人类监督之间的平衡问题，并提出了一个基于拓扑的GeoAI代理框架来辅助城市洪水筛查。

Method: 该框架利用了确定性控制器从公共数据源获取地形、降雨、土壤、土地覆盖、暴露和基础设施等多个数据层，使用离散Morse理论提取地形洼地，依靠相对洪水优先级评分对盆地进行排名，并通过人类介控检查点让用户有机会调整阈值、因子权重或建议。

Result: 在马里兰州College Park的实际案例研究中，所选阈值将278个原始地形最低点缩减至70个盆地，这70个盆地中有77%的最低点也经D8流路由模型单独计算确认为洼地。在其他四个研究区域中，一致性范围从61%到83%不等。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合开放数据、明确的空间推理、可追踪的证据和人类监督，实现了透明的一次性洪水筛查，并确保了关键建议的可检查和用户控制。

Abstract: Open-world geographic artificial intelligence (GeoAI) agents can transform public geospatial data into accessible decision support for real-world challenges, but must balance autonomous reasoning with reliable spatial analysis and human oversight. We present a topology-guided GeoAI agentic framework for urban flood screening that takes a place name, drawn region, or uploaded boundary and returns ranked candidate basins with an interactive map and report. A deterministic controller retrieves public elevation, rainfall, soil, land-cover, exposure, and infrastructure layers, extracts terrain depressions with discrete Morse theory, and ranks basins with a relative flood-priority score. The language model acts only at recorded decision points: selecting a persistence threshold from a precomputed candidate table, drafting recommendations from computed evidence, and reviewing each recommendation against the same evidence. Three human-in-the-loop checkpoints let users revise the threshold, factor weights, or recommendation. In a College Park, Maryland case study, the selected threshold reduced 278 raw terrain minima to 70 basins, with 77% of these basins' lowest points also identified as sinks by a separately computed eight-direction (D8) flow-routing model. Across four further study areas, agreement ranged from 61% to 83%. By combining open data, explicit spatial reasoning, traceable evidence, and human oversight, the framework enables transparent first-pass flood screening while keeping consequential recommendations inspectable and user-controlled.

</details>


### [214] [Group Relative Policy Optimisation Improves Multilingual Speech Recognition in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30663)
*Tim Wenjie Hu,Evelyn Nafula Ouma,Benjamin Akera,John A. Quinn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过使用Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO)优化较小的非LLM编码器-解码器ASR模型，以Whisper-large-v3为例，针对51种非洲语言进行训练。研究结果表明，当训练数据稀缺或输入音频有噪音或降质时，该方法具有明显优势。提出了名为SunflowerASR的新模型，该模型在AfriVox-v2和SimbaBench基准测试中达到最佳的宏平均WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善小规模非LLM编码器-解码器ASR模型的性能，尤其是在训练数据稀缺或输入音频质量差的情况下。

Method: 使用GRPO进行训练优化

Result: 在多个非洲语言的基准测试（AfriVox-v2和SimbaBench）中，所提出的SunflowerASR模型取得了最佳的宏平均错误率。

Conclusion: 通过GRPO优化的SunflowerASR模型在非洲语言以及在噪声或降质音频中表现出色。

Abstract: Autoregressive encoder-decoder speech recognition models trained with teacher forcing can suffer from exposure bias, limiting their ability to recover from their own prediction errors at inference time. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) improves speech recognition in major languages with LLM-based models. We extend this line of work to smaller, non-LLM encoder-decoder ASR models, using Whisper-large-v3 as a case study across 51 African languages. The benefits of this approach are most pronounced when training data is scarce or when input audio is noisy or degraded. Our resulting model, SunflowerASR, achieves the best published macro-averaged WER on the AfriVox-v2 (0.227) and SimbaBench (0.372) benchmarks, and is publicly available.

</details>


### [215] [Scaling Laws for Safety Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30678)
*Yuchen Wu,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建六个足够的大规模和难度的安全数据集，研究了探针性能与模型大小和标签数量之间的关系，发现了两种简单的缩放定律，通过这些定律，研究人员可以根据给定的模型和标签预算确定最佳的探针架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全数据集规模有限或已经被饱和，无法用来预测探针的性能，因此需要构建新的数据集来进一步研究。

Method: 作者通过构建六个大规模的安全数据集，并对来自五个模型家族的20个基础模型进行训练，采用两种探针架构，在每个模型上有32到1,000,000个标签进行实验。

Result: 研究发现探针错误遵循两种简单的缩放定律。首先是可达成的最低探针错误随着模型大小按照幂律下降；其次，随着示例数量的增加，探针错误会逐渐趋近于其地板，而斜率会随着目标模型大小的增加而增加。这两种定律在任务、模型家族和探针架构之间都保持一致。

Conclusion: 根据这些定律，研究者可以确定如何为给定的模型和标签预算构建最佳探针，预测模型大小和标签数量关系，以及探测性能优化策略。这提高了安全研究人员的工作效率和准确性。

Abstract: Safety probes trained on a model's activations are a standard tool for monitoring language models in real time. Each time a new model is released or a new harm area needs to be monitored, the probe must be retrained, which requires new labels and a choice of probe architecture. Yet neither the model nor the labels tell us in advance how well the resulting probe will perform. Finding out requires training and comparing many probe configurations. In this paper, we ask whether probe performance can instead be predicted in advance from model size and the number of labels. Existing safety datasets are insufficient for this, as they are either limited in scale or already saturated. We therefore construct six new safety datasets of sufficient size and difficulty. On these datasets, we train two probe architectures on 20 base models from five model families, with 32 to $10^{6}$ labels for each model. We measure performance using probe error, defined as 1 - AUROC, where lower values are better. We find that probe error follows two simple scaling laws. First, the lowest probe error a model can reach falls as a power law in its size.
